*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的轨迹推断算法Acceleration Matching (AM)，通过将插值问题提升到相空间并对显式条件加速度场进行回归，生成与给定边缘分布一致的光滑随机轨迹。该算法仅需位置数据、训练过程无需轨迹模拟和昂贵预处理，在多个基准问题上表现优于或媲美现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域的基础问题。现有算法存在计算挑战：一类依赖预处理子程序来保证光滑性，另一类依赖基于模拟的训练目标，两者都代价高昂。因此需要一种既无需昂贵预处理、又无需轨迹模拟的轻量级算法。

Method: AM算法将原始插值问题提升到相空间（位置+速度），然后对显式条件加速度场进行回归，从而诱导出与规定边缘分布一致的光滑随机轨迹。训练过程仅需要位置数据，不涉及轨迹模拟，也无需昂贵的预处理步骤。

Result: 在多个现有文献中的基准问题上，AM算法表现具有竞争力，或优于现有的轨迹推断算法。

Conclusion: AM算法通过相空间提升与条件加速度场回归的方式，有效克服了现有轨迹推断算法的计算瓶颈，实现了高效且高质量的光滑轨迹生成。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [48] [Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs for Efficient Options Pricing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02778)
*Harris Cobb,Wenbo Hao,Yingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新应用——局部收敛输入的神经网络（NNLCI），用于提升多资产期权定价数值方法的效率。NNLCI通过神经网络局部修正粗网格和细网格解的差异，仅需少量高保真训练数据，在Black-Scholes模型（1-3维）和Heston随机波动率模型下，可将细网格数值解的均方根误差降低约4-12倍，显著减少高维问题的计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多资产期权定价的高维数值方法（如有限差分、蒙特卡洛等）计算成本极高，实时交易和风险管理中难以满足速度和精度的双重需求。现有的粗网格方法速度快但精度不足，细网格方法精度高但计算昂贵，迫切需要一种能够在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算开销的解决方案。

Method: NNLCI方法的核心思想是：在粗网格和细网格上分别求解数值解，然后训练一个神经网络来学习两者之间的局部差异（即误差修正项），从而在推理阶段仅需粗网格解即可快速逼近细网格精度。论文引入了最简洁的NNLCI输入格式以提高效率和便利性，仅需少量高保真训练数据即可实现良好的泛化能力。

Result: 在Black-Scholes方程下的现金或零期权（1维、2维、3维空间）以及Heston随机波动率模型下的单资产向下敲出障碍看涨期权（2维定价PDE）上进行了验证。在所有测试案例中，NNLCI将细网格数值解的均方根误差（RMSE）降低了约4-12倍，且仅在少量参数组合子集上训练即达到此效果，展示了低训练成本和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: NNLCI显著降低了高维期权定价问题的计算需求，为实时期权交易和风险管理提供了高效方案，兼具低训练成本和强大的泛化能力，是一种有前景的数值方法加速技术。

Abstract: We present a novel application of Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs (NNLCI) to improve the efficiency of existing numerical methods for pricing multi-asset options. The most concise input format for NNLCI has been introduced, offering substantial convenience and efficiency. NNLCI uses a neural network to locally correct solutions from a coarse mesh and a refined mesh (relative to the coarse one), requiring only a minimal amount of high-fidelity training data. We demonstrate this approach on cash-or-nothing options under the Black-Scholes equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions, and on single-asset down-and-out barrier call options under the Heston stochastic-volatility model (whose pricing PDE is two-dimensional in the spot price $S$ and the instantaneous variance $v$). In each case, NNLCI reduces the root-mean-square error (RMSE) of the refined-mesh numerical solution by a factor of approximately 4-12 on test sets, even when the neural network is trained on only a small subset of parameter combinations. These results demonstrate that NNLCI significantly reduces computational requirements for high-dimensional problems in real-time options trading and risk management, offering low training costs and strong generalization ability.

</details>


### [49] [Evaluation Blindness: How Silent Measurement Failures Corrupt AI Systems from Training to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02786)
*Priyanka Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估盲区"（evaluation blindness）概念：测量函数在系统实际故障时仍产生与健康状态无法区分的读数，且无任何辅助信号提示差异。该问题横跨训练与部署两个生命周期阶段，作者提供了统一的正式可检测性谓词、四个训练时案例（含TRL PR #6594真实实现bug）、六类生产故障分类体系（基于50个真实事件验证，53%可验证故障为静默），并提出故障预算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统可能静默失败，失败在训练循环、评估管线和生产监控栈中传播，直到下游危害使其可见。现有文献将训练时和部署时的测量失败分开处理，缺乏统一框架来理解和检测这类"看似健康实则故障"的系统状态。

Method: 1) 形式化定义评估盲区的可检测性谓词，统一训练与部署两个阶段。2) 通过四个训练时案例研究（包括TRL PR #6594中的真实梯度损坏bug）展示具体失效机制。3) 构建六类生产故障分类体系，基于50个来自法庭文件和监管文件的实际事件进行验证。4) 提出故障预算框架，将可接受故障率与用例风险等级关联。

Result: 在50个可验证的公开故障事件中，53%属于静默失败；六类分类体系中包含一个结构性100%静默的操作类（Operational category）；训练时的失效率显示损失曲线正常时梯度仍可能被损坏。

Conclusion: 测量基础设施是贯穿整个AI生命周期的正确性问题，而非仅在评估阶段。应将监控与测量的可靠性当作系统核心关切，并通过故障预算框架将可接受失败率与用例风险关联。

Abstract: AI systems can fail silently. The failure propagates through training loops, evaluation pipelines, and production monitoring stacks until downstream harm makes it visible. This paper introduces evaluation blindness: a measurement function M exhibits evaluation blindness with respect to failure class F when it produces readings indistinguishable from a healthy state while the system is actually failing, with no auxiliary signal flagging the gap.
  The problem surfaces at two lifecycle stages the literature has treated separately. At training time, reward models are gamed, importance-sampling corrections are silently miscalculated, and benchmark contamination inflates fine-tuning evaluations, all while loss curves look healthy and gradient updates proceed normally. At deployment time, monitoring fails to catch six classes of production failure, including an Operational category that is 100% silent by structural definition.
  We provide a formal detectability predicate unifying both stages. Four training-time case studies trace concrete breakdowns, including a real implementation bug in TRL PR #6594 where gradients are corrupted as loss decreases normally. A six-class taxonomy validated against 50 real-world incidents from court documents and regulatory filings finds that 53% of verifiable public failures were silent. A failure budget framework ties acceptable failure rates to use-case risk class.
  The implication is direct: measurement infrastructure is a correctness concern across the full AI lifecycle, not just at evaluation time. Data, code, and taxonomy schema are at https://github.com/priyanka25aug/llm-failure-taxonomy.

</details>


### [50] [Topological Simplification in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02816)
*Adam Shaw,Jiayu Li,Michael Sperling,Michael Kim,Alvin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用逐层持续同调分析预测编码网络（PCNs）中学习表征的拓扑结构，发现较小模型比大模型更早跨越层折叠连通分量（Spearman ρ∈[0.72,0.79]），简化深度与重建误差呈强负相关（ρ=-0.58），且PCNs比匹配的MLPs平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量，表明持续同调为PCNs中的压缩-重建权衡提供了定量视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码网络（PCNs）作为神经启发的双向架构，其学习表征的拓扑结构尚未被系统研究。作者希望利用定量、逐层的持续同调分析来揭示PCNs在不同架构和激活函数下拓扑特征如何随网络层变化，从而更深入理解模型的压缩-重建权衡机制。

Method: 在合成分类数据集（≥99.9%测试准确率）和MNIST（≥95%测试准确率）上训练性能良好的PCNs，采用逐层持续同调分析测量不同架构和激活函数下拓扑特征（连通分量）随网络层的变化。通过Spearman相关分析关联模型尺寸（隐藏层宽度之和）与拓扑简化时机，并利用种子级bootstrap比较PCNs与匹配MLPs的拓扑行为差异。

Result: （1）较小PCNs比大模型更早地在层间折叠连通分量，Spearman ρ∈[0.72,0.79]（跨激活函数）；（2）简化发生的深度与重建误差呈强负相关（ρ=-0.58），即更晚简化的架构重建效果更好；（3）PCNs始终比匹配的MLPs平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量。

Conclusion: 持续同调为理解PCNs中压缩-重建权衡提供了定量分析工具。模型容量和预测编码推理中的循环双向动力学共同决定了该权衡的跨层解决时机，为神经启发架构的拓扑分析开辟了新视角。

Abstract: We study the topology of learned representations in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a neuro-inspired bidirectional architecture, using a quantitative layer-wise persistent homology analysis. We train well-performing PCNs on a synthetic classification dataset ($\geq 99.9\%$ test accuracy) and on MNIST ($\geq 95\%$ test accuracy), and measure how topological features change across layers for different architectures and activation functions. We find that smaller PCNs collapse connected components across layers earlier than larger models (Spearman $\unicode{x1D70C} \in [0.72, 0.79]$ across activations), with model size measured as the sum of hidden-layer widths. We also observe a strong negative correlation ($\unicode{x1D70C} = -0.58$) between the depth at which simplification occurs and reconstruction error; i.e., architectures that simplify later reconstruct better. Finally, a seed-level bootstrap comparison across architectures and activations shows that PCNs consistently collapse connected components later than matched MLPs, with an average difference of $3.6$ layers. These results suggest that persistent homology offers a useful quantitative lens on the compression--reconstruction tradeoff in PCNs, and that both model capacity and the recurrent, bidirectional dynamics of predictive coding inference shape when this tradeoff is resolved across layers.

</details>


### [51] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统刻画了将预训练大模型（1.4B）转换为更小兄弟模型（410M）的可能性。研究发现模型家族的表示在不同规模间高度对齐（R²=0.84），而参数对齐很弱；最优线性映射后的权重残差与噪声不可区分。转换价值存在于初始化阶段——通过最小二乘补偿和方差保持重缩放两个独立杠杆，在低预算下显著优于从头训练，但随预算增大收敛至持平，且在更大规模（6.9B→1.4B）时因补偿问题病态而过度校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型家族通常从零开始训练每个规模，这在计算上非常浪费。作者探索一个关键问题：能否将预训练的大模型转换为更小的"兄弟"模型，从而避免重复的大规模训练成本？

Method: 在Pythia家族中端到端刻画1.4B→410M的模型转化：(i)使用Ridge回归量化表示对齐（R²=0.84）与参数对齐；分析稠密权重投影为何功能性地破坏模型（旋转位置编码、多头、GELU、LayerNorm结构被基混合破坏）；(iii)最优线性算子后权重残差与噪声不可区分；(iv)分解转换为两个独立杠杆——最小二乘补偿（优化零样本性能）和方差保持重缩放（优化训练动态）；在匹配预算的持续预训练下评估，并映射方法在6.9B→1.4B更大规模下的边界。

Result: 补偿是token高效、低预算的胜利：在30M token下，宽度缩减对（84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7）和深度缩减对（109.3 vs 117.9）均胜过最强子克隆变体（3/3种子）；但33倍预算下两者收敛至持平（40.0 vs 40.0）。转移初始化始终优于从头训练，低预算下最高达18倍优势。在6.9B→1.4B规模下，叠加两个杠杆导致过度校正，根源是补偿求解在较大宽度下的病态问题。

Conclusion: 模型转换的价值存在于初始化而非权重本身；最小二乘补偿加方差重缩放是一种低预算、token高效的转换策略，但并非普适优越，随预算增大趋于与基线持平，且在大规模、宽宽度场景下需引入维度感知的正则化来解决病态问题。作者发布了代码、检查点和冻结评估语料库。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [52] [NOMADD: Numerical Optimization of Models Adapting to Data Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02845)
*Swapn Shah,Keith Burghardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后置（post-hoc）方法以减少概念漂移对表格模型性能的影响，通过将基模型分别拟合到各标记训练周期，测量其参数相对于锚定模型的变化，用低秩分解压缩这些变化，并对每个潜在因子进行阻尼正则化外推预测。在18个数据集的Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上，该方法使每个基模型家族性能均有提升，且训练仅需数秒，与需要1300 GPU小时预训练且推理慢数个数量级的SOTA方法相比极具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格模型在特征分布变化（数据漂移）或特征与目标变量关系变化（概念漂移）时性能会下降。实时缓解这些问题的难点在于标签数据可能无法立即获取，或重新训练模型不切实际。现有漂移缓解工具通常只适用于神经网络架构并修改训练方式，缺乏适用于多种模型（从树模型到神经网络再到表格基础模型）的通用后置方法。

Method: 该算法包含四个步骤：(1) 将基模型分别拟合到每个有标签的训练周期；(2) 测量模型参数相对于一个跨所有周期池化的锚定模型的变化；(3) 用低秩分解（low-rank factorization）压缩这些参数变化；(4) 通过阻尼正则化预测（damped, regularized forecast）对每个潜在因子进行前向外推。

Result: 在18个数据集组成的Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上，按照该基准自身的协议和指标评估，外推方法使所有基模型家族的性能都有所提升，并达到与SOTA方法Drift-Resilient TabPFN相竞争的性能，而训练仅需数秒。相比之下，Drift-Resilient TabPFN需要在上百万个合成数据集上预训练约1300 GPU小时，且推理速度慢数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效、通用且后置的概念漂移缓解方案，特别适用于需要在高精度、有限推理时间和模型大小等约束下选择不同模型的场景。此外，文章探讨了将该工具扩展到其他模态（如图像、文本等）的潜力与挑战。

Abstract: Tabular model performance degrades when feature distributions change over time or the relationship between features and outcome variables change over time, known as data drift and concept drift, respectively. These issues are challenging to mitigate in real time because labeled data may not be immediately available, or re-training a model could be impractical. While tools exist to reduce drift, they are typically bespoke to neural network architectures and adapt how models are trained. In this paper, we offer an alternative post-hoc method to reduce concept drift, which is applicable to a variety of models, from trees to neural networks to tabular foundation models. This new tool is especially useful when constraints, such as high model accuracy, bounded inference time, or model size requires users to choose between different models for their specific use-cases. Our algorithm fits the base model separately on each labeled training period, measures how its parameters evolve against a single anchor model pooled over all of those periods, compresses those changes with a low-rank factorization, and extrapolates each latent factor forward with a damped, regularized forecast. On the 18-dataset Drift-Resilient TabPFN benchmark, evaluated under that benchmark's own protocol and metric, the extrapolation improves every base family it is applied to, and achieves performance competitive with the state-of-the-art Drift-Resilient TabPFN with seconds of training. In contrast, Drift-Resilient TabPFN requires pre-training on millions of synthetic datasets over approximately 1,300 GPU-hours, and is orders of magnitude slower in inference (depending on the model). In the discussion, we explore the promise and challenges of extending this tool to other modalities.

</details>

*Boyang Xu,Mostafa Reisi Gahrooei,Mohammad Ilbeigi,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合成本感知贝叶斯优化与水平集估计的框架，用于引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）在灾后动态环境中高效定位最关键的信息区域，从而快速、准确且低成本地评估建筑损伤，支持紧急响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灾后损伤评估依赖静态、劳动密集型的数据采集方式，成本高昂且难以适应灾后动态变化的环境，亟需一种快速、可靠且经济高效的自动化评估方法。

Method: 提出一种成本感知的贝叶斯优化与水平集估计（level-set estimation）相结合的框架，持续引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）前往信息量最丰富的区域，通过动态更新不同地理区域的损伤估计，在最小化运行成本的同时系统性地降低不确定性。

Result: 在受控合成玩具研究中，该方法能高效追踪损伤边界、恢复底层损伤地图并快速降低预测不确定性；在R2D软件生成的高保真灾害数据上，算法提供了准确且及时的损伤估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够支持信息丰富且快速的紧急响应，为灾后损伤评估提供了一种兼顾准确性与成本效益的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Natural disasters frequently inflict severe damage to the built environment, which demands a rapid, reliable, and cost-effective damage assessment for emergency response. However, traditional methods for post-disaster damage assessment often rely on static, labor-intensive data collection strategies that can be prohibitively expensive and struggle to adapt to dynamic post-disaster conditions. In this study, we propose a cost-aware Bayesian optimization framework combined with level-set estimation that continuously guides autonomous data collectors, e.g., an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), toward the most informative regions. By dynamically updating damage estimates across different geographic zones, our approach systematically reduces uncertainty while minimizing operational costs. The proposed framework is first validated using a controlled synthetic toy study, demonstrating the agent's ability to efficiently trace damage boundaries, recover the underlying damage map, and rapidly reduce predictive uncertainty. Furthermore, the approach is evaluated using high-fidelity disaster data generated by the Regional Resilience Determination (R2D) software. The results of the algorithm provide accurate and timely damage estimates that support informative and fast emergency response.

</details>


### [54] [Contrast-invariant deep ptychography neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02869)
*Albert Vong,Steven Henke,Oliver Hoidn,Hanna Ruth,Junjing Deng,Apurva Mehta,David Shapiro,Alexander Hexemer,Nicholas Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种因子分解策略来解决Ptychography神经网络在不同分布下重建时的尺度不一致问题，通过将物体纹理与测量尺度解耦，并在实虚部空间中预测物体，配合合成物体采样策略，在5个实验数据集上将傅里叶误差较基线降低最多5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ptychography神经网络在分布外泛化时存在尺度不一致问题，严重限制了其在实际应用中的可用性。

Method: 1) 提出因子分解策略，解耦学习的物体纹理与测量尺度，使单个训练网络能在不同照明条件下产生与测量一致的重建；2) 改用实部和虚部单位预测物体，替代传统的幅值和相位表示；3) 引入合成物体采样策略，最小化合成训练数据与实验目标的相位分布不匹配。

Result: 在跨多个束线和设施的5个实验数据集上，与之前的PtychoPINN-torch基线相比，傅里叶误差最多降低5倍。

Conclusion: 通过解耦尺度与纹理并优化表示方式及训练数据分布，可以显著提升Ptychography神经网络在实际环境中的泛化能力和重建精度。

Abstract: Ptychography neural networks suffer from scaling inconsistencies when generalizing out of distribution, limiting their real world viability. We address this scaling mismatch using a factorization strategy which decouples the learned object texture from measurement scaling, enabling a single trained network to produce measurement-consistent reconstructions across varying illumination conditions. This requires predicting the learned object in real and imaginary units instead of the canonical amplitude and phase representation. We additionally introduce a synthetic object sampling strategy that minimizes phase distribution mismatch between synthetic training data and experimental targets. These improvements yield up to a 5x reduction in Fourier error over the previous PtychoPINN-torch baseline across 5 experimental datasets spanning multiple beamlines and facilities.

</details>


### [55] [Maglev: Sliding Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02870)
*Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有固定大小记忆的循环Transformer架构，该架构推广了滑动窗口注意力，同时保持训练时的可并行性。该方法由预填充器Q和解码器P两个耦合模型组成，通过记忆一致性损失进行训练，使推理时仅需P即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动窗口注意力虽然高效，但表达能力有限；而完整的注意力机制虽然强大，但计算成本高且无法处理任意长的序列。需要一种既能保持高效推理又能获得更强表达能力的架构。

Method: 提出双模型架构：预填充器Q利用全注意力（实际中结合全注意力和滑动窗口注意力）生成记忆目标m'_t；解码器P仅使用滑动窗口注意力和循环K/V注入产生解码器记忆m_t用于下一个token预测。通过记忆一致性损失对齐m_t和m'_t，使得推理时可以仅使用P。参数共享可减少参数占用。

Result: 在验证损失和下游预训练基准上，该方法优于滑动窗口注意力和潜在循环Transformer基线。参数共享在减少参数占用的同时保留了大部分性能增益。

Conclusion: 实验证明该双模型蒸馏式训练策略有效，能够在不牺牲训练并行性的前提下显著提升滑动窗口注意力的表达能力，且参数共享提供了良好的性能-参数权衡。

Abstract: We introduce \ours{}, a recurrent Transformer architecture with fixed-size memory that generalizes sliding-window attention while remaining parallelizable during training. \ours{} consists of two coupled models: a prefiller $Q$, which leverages full attention\footnote{In practice, we use interleaved full and sliding-window attention for $Q$, as this yields stronger performance. The essential requirement is that $Q$ be more expressive than $P$, with access to the full history.} to produce memory targets $m'_t$, and a decoder $P$, which uses only sliding-window attention and recurrent K/V injection to produce decoder memories $m_t$ for next-token prediction. We train \ours{} with a memory consistency loss that aligns $m_t$ with $m'_t$, allowing inference to use $P$ alone. Empirically, \ours{} improves validation loss and downstream pretraining benchmarks over sliding-window and latent recurrent transformer baselines. Moreover, sharing parameters between $P$ and $Q$ reduces parameter memory while preserving most of the gains.

</details>


### [56] [GoT-CD: Graph-of-Thoughts Causal Discovery and the Fragility of Post-hoc Path-Specific Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02877)
*Nitish Nagesh,Elahe Khatibi,Thomas Dean Hughes,Mahdi Bagheri,Pratik Gajane,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GoT-CD方法，将完整候选边集作为推理单元进行并行图生成、确定性有效性评分和硬联合约束合并，在五个基准上均返回有效DAG，并在三个数据集上取得最佳DAG有效F1分数。但研究发现结构保真度并不保证公平审计的忠实性：在已知不公平路径的阿尔茨海默病基准上，八张发现的图中五张无法恢复敏感属性到结果的路径，导致审计报告零总体效应而介导效应仍存在，强调下游路径特定公平分析的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在临床公平审计中被使用，但路径特定反事实公平性依赖于所发现的因果图，而传统评估仅用聚合结构指标（所有边等权）打分，没有考察审计所依赖的特定路径是否在发现中幸存，也没有考察路径缺失时审计报告的内容。这构成了结构发现与公平审计之间的关键鸿沟。

Method: GoT-CD（Graph-of-Thoughts Causal Discovery）以完整候选边集为推理单元，并行生成多个候选图，通过确定性有效性函数评分，在硬联合约束（禁止发明新边）下合并，并通过贪心投影确保DAG约束，最终输出有效DAG。同时引入路径特定事后审计方法评估发现图在公平性审计中的忠实性。

Result: GoT-CD在全部五个基准上输出有效DAG，在Asia、Alzheimer's和COVID-Respiratory数据集上取得了LLM方法中最佳的DAG有效F1分数。在阿尔茨海默病基准的事后路径特定审计中，八张发现图中五张未恢复敏感属性到结果的路径，报告空总体效应但介导效应持续存在。

Conclusion: 全图Graph-of-Thoughts推理产出的DAG在结构上与LLM基线竞争，但结构忠实度不等于公平审计忠实度。路径特定公平审计必须与结构发现结合进行，因为路径缺失可能导致对不公平效应的高估/低估（如报告零总体效应而介导效应仍存）。

Abstract: Causal discovery recovers directed structure from observational data and is increasingly used in clinical settings to support mechanism reasoning and fairness audits of predictive models. Path-specific counterfactual fairness asks whether a protected attribute influences an outcome through illegitimate pathways, but these estimands are defined relative to a supplied causal graph and therefore inherit whatever errors the discovery step introduces. Discovery methods are routinely scored on aggregate structural metrics that weight all edges equally, and no established evaluation asks whether the specific pathway an audit depends on survives discovery---or what the audit reports when that pathway is missing. Here we show that full-graph Graph-of-Thoughts reasoning yields acyclic discovered graphs that are structurally competitive with large language model (LLM) baselines, yet that structural fidelity alone does not guarantee fairness-faithful audits. We introduce GoT-CD, in which the reasoning unit is a complete candidate edge set: multiple graphs are generated in parallel, scored by a deterministic validity function, and merged under a hard union constraint that forbids invented edges, with greedy projection enforcing a DAG before commitment. GoT-CD returns a valid DAG on all five reported benchmarks and achieves the best DAG-valid F1 score among LLM methods on Asia, Alzheimer's, and COVID-Respiratory datasets. On an Alzheimer's benchmark with known unfair path, a post-hoc path-specific audit shows that five of eight discovered graphs recover no path from the sensitive attribute to the outcome and therefore report a null overall effect while mediated effects persist, necessitating downstream path-specific fairness analysis along with structural discovery.

</details>


### [57] [Population-Robust Feature Selection via Generalized Welfare Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02887)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PopFS是一种学习方法，用于学习一个共享、可部署的特征集，该特征集对群体差异具有鲁棒性，同时允许每个群体训练自己的模型。它通过可调福利目标平衡整体预测收益与最弱势群体的保护，利用多任务稀疏学习缩减候选池后直接搜索硬特征集，在六个预测任务的八个人口分割及43州COVID-19现在流研究中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择是部署决策：有限的问卷、测试面板或传感器集可能需要服务多个异质人群。标准的特征选择方法通常针对单一大规模人群优化，而现有的鲁棒方法倾向于为每个群体学习一个共享模型。这两类方法都未充分解决"共享特征集但各群体独立建模"这一实际问题。

Method: PopFS首先使用多任务稀疏学习来缩减候选特征池，然后通过排序有前景的特征添加和交换直接搜索硬特征集，仅对短名单进行完全重新拟合。它采用可调的福利目标函数，允许实践者在整体预测收益与对受益最少群体的更强保护之间进行权衡。

Result: 在来自五个表格和公共卫生数据集的六个预测任务的八个人口分割中，PopFS在平均和最差群体性能上均表现强劲，且可扩展至数千个候选特征。43州COVID-19现在流研究显示，改变福利目标可以改善服务最少的州，平均性能变化很小，并产生所选症状信号的可解释变化。

Conclusion: PopFS成功实现了跨异质人群的共享特征集学习，在保持整体性能的同时显著改善最弱势群体的表现，且具备良好的可扩展性和可解释性，为实际部署中的特征选择提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Choosing which features to collect is a deployment decision: the same limited questionnaire, test panel, or sensor set may need to serve several heterogeneous populations. Standard feature-selection methods typically optimize for one large population, while existing robust approaches tend to learn one shared model for every population. We introduce PopFS, a method for learning one shared, deployable feature set that is robust to population differences while letting each pop- ulation train its own model. PopFS uses a tunable welfare objective that lets practitioners balance overall predictive ben- efit against stronger protection of the populations that benefit least. To make this objective practical at scale, PopFS first uses multitask sparse learning to reduce the candidate pool, then searches directly over hard feature sets by ranking promising additions and swaps and fully refitting only a shortlist. Across eight population splits from six prediction tasks drawn from five tabular and public-health datasets, PopFS consistently achieves strong average and worst-population performance while scaling to thousands of candidate features. A 43-state COVID-19 nowcasting study further shows that changing the welfare objective can improve the least-served states with lit- tle change in average performance and yields an interpretable change in the selected symptom signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/PopFS.

</details>


### [58] [Robust Counterfactual Policy Optimisation via Nondeterministic Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02893)
*Jessica Lally,Milad Kazemi,Nicola Paoletti,David Watson,Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将反事实策略优化形式化地扩展到概率非确定性因果模型下，将潜在混杂与不可约随机性分离，并在敏感性分析框架下提出鲁棒反事实策略优化问题，在脓毒症治疗模拟器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的反事实推断方法假设确定性因果模型，但马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)本质上是随机的。这一假设忽略了不可约的随机性，导致现有方法无法正确处理实际决策场景中的固有随机性。

Method: 形式化概率非确定性因果模型下的反事实策略优化，将潜在混杂因素与不可约随机性分离；在敏感性分析框架下提出首个实际的鲁棒反事实策略优化问题。

Result: 在脓毒症治疗模拟器上验证了该方法的有效性，其中糖尿病状态作为隐藏的全局混杂因素。

Conclusion: 该方法填补了随机MDP场景下反事实策略优化的空白，通过敏感性分析确保了在存在隐含混杂的条件下策略的鲁棒性，为顺序决策中的反事实推断提供了更实际的框架。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference approaches for sequential decision-making typically assume deterministic causal models, where all randomness stems from latent variables. However, Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are inherently stochastic. We address this by formalising counterfactual policy optimisation under probabilistic nondeterministic causal models, which properly separates latent confounding from irreducible stochasticity, and here propose a first practical optimisation problem for identifying robust counterfactual policies under a sensitivity analysis framework. We validate our approach on a sepsis treatment simulator, where diabetes status acts as a hidden global confounder.

</details>


### [59] [AnchorKV: Anchor-Residual KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02901)
*Malik Khalaf,Yara Shamshoum,Nitzan Hodos,Yuval Sieradzki,Assaf Schuster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorKV是一种KV缓存压缩方案，通过锚点存储和近似重构实现20倍压缩而不丢弃任何token，在70B规模下保持全缓存99%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是长上下文LLM推理的主要内存瓶颈。现有方案存在两难：驱逐方法永久丢弃token，若被丢弃的token后续被证明关键则性能退化；量化方法虽保留全部token但压缩率有限。

Method: AnchorKV使用少量精确存储的锚点来表示整个缓存，每个非锚点token通过与其最相似的锚点来表达，仅对最能影响模型输出的近似token进行精细化处理，实现无需丢弃任何token的20倍压缩。

Result: AnchorKV在多个模型和数据集上一致地保持准确性，在70B规模下保留全缓存分数的99%，同时将整个上下文保持在极低的成本。

Conclusion: AnchorKV通过锚点实现高倍率压缩且不丢失任何token，同时兼顾精度保持与计算成本，是KV缓存压缩领域的有效方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the primary memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference. Existing approaches attack it from opposite ends: eviction methods permanently discard tokens, degrading performance whenever a discarded token later proves essential, while quantization methods retain all tokens at low precision but offer limited compression. We propose AnchorKV, a compression scheme that shrinks the cache by $20\times$ without discarding a single token. AnchorKV represents the cache using a small set of anchors stored exactly, expresses every other token through its most similar anchor, and refines only those whose approximation most affects the model's output. AnchorKV consistently preserves accuracy across models and datasets, retaining 99% of the full-cache score at the 70B scale, while keeping the entire context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [60] [Bayesian Data Reweighting Improves Multimodal Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02907)
*Jingchen Sun,Shaobo Han,Ruiyi Zhang,Naresh Kumar Devulapally,Ming Liu,Yitao Long,Vishnu Suresh Lokhande,Changyou Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯数据重加权（Bayesian Data Reweighting）框架，通过将查询-文档重要性建模为潜变量并推断后验权重来降低假负样本的负面影响，在三个检索器与七个知识型VQA基准上持续提升检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态检索器对比训练方法将所有不匹配的查询-文档对视为同等信息量的负样本，但许多不匹配文档实际上可能语义相关或部分有用，这些"假负样本"会损害训练效果。

Method: 提出一个概率框架，将查询-文档重要性建模为潜变量，通过共轭先验下的闭式后验更新和随机EM优化，自适应地推断后验权重，从而降低（downweight）可能的假负样本的影响。

Result: 该方法在三个不同的检索器和七个知识型VQA基准上一致性地提升了检索准确率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯数据重加权是一种有效且通用的策略，能够缓解多模态检索对比训练中假负样本问题，提升知识型VQA的检索性能。

Abstract: Multimodal retrievers are essential for knowledge-based visual question answering, where they retrieve external evidence for image-question pairs. However, existing contrastive training methods typically treat all unmatched query-document pairs as equally informative negatives, which is problematic because many unmatched documents may still be semantically relevant or partially useful. We propose Bayesian Data Reweighting, a probabilistic framework that models query-document importance as latent variables and adaptively infers posterior weights to downweight likely false negatives. With closed-form posterior updates under conjugate priors and stochastic EM optimization, our method consistently improves retrieval accuracy across three retrievers and seven knowledge-based VQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [61] [Forecasting Revenue with its Customer-Base Drivers: When and Why Coordination Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02911)
*Kyeongbin Kim,Daniel McCarthy,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出客户基础多任务Transformer（CBMT）模型，利用966家公司25个行业的周度交易面板数据，联合预测客户获取、重复购买、每笔订单支出等原始预测因子，并使其组合与下游总收入对齐。CBMT的总销售额均分误差比最强基准低30%，比直接预测总收入的Transformer低2.65%（虽未达统计显著），并在74.3%的企业中优于单任务模型。当客户基础动态高度波动时所有模型精度下降，CBMT优势收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总收入预测无法揭示变化的来源（是获取、重复购买、单笔支出还是相互抵消所致）。作者希望通过协调客户基础的原始预测因子，不仅提高总销售额预测精度，还能解释增长动因，为收购预算、需求规划和基于客户的估值提供更细粒度的决策支持。

Method: 提出CBMT（Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer）架构，通过多任务学习共享隐藏结构，同时保留各原始预测因子（如获取、重复购买、支出）的独立预测，并通过对齐机制使组合预测与下游总收入匹配。采用每周交易面板数据，覆盖966家公司、25个行业，进行广泛的基准对比和情景分析。

Result: CBMT平均总销售额误差比最强既有客户基准低30%；比直接预测总收入的Transformer低2.65%（配对差异p=.222，不显著）；在74.3%的企业中优于独立单任务预测。来源MAE在24个基准-结果组合中有23个低于基准。原始预测因子协同运动更强的企业更受益于联合预测；高波动环境下所有模型精度恶化，CBMT优势收窄；校准期路由规则未能优于始终部署CBMT。

Conclusion: 协调的客户基础多任务预测能显著提升总销售额精度并提供可解释的增长来源分解，但其优势在高客户动态波动场景下减弱，且收益更多地体现为诊断性而非因果性证据。研究为企业何时应信任联合客户基础预测提供了边界条件。

Abstract: Revenue forecasts guide acquisition budgets, demand planning, and customer-based valuations, yet an aggregate forecast does not show whether change reflects acquisition, repeat purchasing, spending per order, or offsetting movements. Using weekly transaction panels for 966 companies in 25 industries, the authors develop the Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer (CBMT), which learns shared structure, retains separate primitive forecasts, and aligns their combination with downstream revenue. CBMT's mean total-sales error is 30% below the strongest representative established customer-base benchmark. It is also 2.65% below a Transformer that forecasts total sales directly, although the paired difference is not statistically significant (p=.222), and it beats separately estimated single-task forecasts for 74.3% of firms. CBMT's source MAE is lower in 23 of 24 benchmark-by-outcome comparisons, with the remaining difference not statistically distinguishable from zero. Firms whose primitives co-move more strongly are more likely to benefit from joint forecasting; selected-family scenario-3 comparisons are consistent with gains from shared representation and revenue alignment but remain diagnostic rather than causal. Accuracy deteriorates for all models when customer-base dynamics are highly volatile, and CBMT's advantage narrows there. Calibration-period routing rules do not improve average accuracy over always deploying CBMT. The results show how coordinated customer-base forecasts support revenue planning and when they warrant greater caution.

</details>


### [62] [When Should Graph Attention Be Sparse? Learning a Per-Edge Tsallis Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02938)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LTGA（可学习Tsallis图注意力）层，通过联合学习Tsallis熵指数q，使图注意力在重尾（q<1）、softmax（q=1）和紧支撑（q>1）之间连续插值，适用于同质与异质图。在8个基准、10个种子上，LTGA-Edge平均排名2.75最优，但统计检验不显著（p=0.199），且学习q未超越网格搜索；其价值在于单次运行替代网格搜索，并提供可解释机制——q偏离1时将42%注意力系数剪枝为零，且精确剪除错误边。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图注意力用softmax（Shannon统计下的最大熵选择）归一化邻域分数，但同质图与异质图需要不同的注意力形状，单一的固定归一化无法同时满足两者需求。

Method: 提出LTGA层，其Tsallis熵指数q与网络权重联合学习，可在四个粒度（从全局标量到逐边索引）上连续插值于重尾、softmax与紧支撑注意力之间，并通过有界重参数化使每个模型从GAT基线出发。

Result: 在8个基准、10个种子上，LTGA-Edge取得最佳平均排名（2.75），但omnibus检验不显著（p=0.199）；学习q并未优于搜索q：验证集调优冻结网格达61.4%，调优α-entmax为62.2%，容量匹配的q≡1控制为62.0%，LTGA-Edge为61.7%。学习指数的优势是单次运行及可解释机制：q偏离1时剪枝42%注意力系数至精确零，针对性删除错误边，恢复它们损失7.1分，而随机剪枝同比例损失13.0分。

Conclusion: 学习Tsallis指数的实际收益不在于超越调优基线，而在于以单次运行替代网格搜索，并赋予注意力一个可解释的稀疏化机制：精确识别并剪除错误边，为图注意力提供了一种自适应的形状选择方案。

Abstract: Graph attention normalizes neighborhood scores with softmax, the maximum-entropy choice under Shannon statistics. But homophilic and heterophilic graphs want different attention shapes, and one fixed normalization cannot serve both. We propose \textbf{LTGA} (\textbf{L}earnable \textbf{T}sallis \textbf{G}raph \textbf{A}ttention), a graph attention layer whose Tsallis entropic index $q$ is learned jointly with the weights, interpolating continuously between heavy-tailed ($q\!<\!1$), softmax ($q\!=\!1$) and compact-support ($q\!>\!1$) attention at four granularities from a global scalar to a per-edge index, under a bounded reparameterization that starts every model at the GAT baseline. Across eight benchmarks at ten seeds, LTGA-Edge takes the best average rank ($2.75$), but the omnibus test does not reject ($p\!=\!0.199$) and learning $q$ does not beat searching it: a validation-tuned frozen grid reaches $61.4\%$, tuned $α$-entmax $62.2\%$ and a capacity-matched $q\!\equiv\!1$ control $62.0\%$, against $61.7\%$ for LTGA-Edge. What the learned index buys is one run instead of a grid, and an interpretable mechanism: where $q$ leaves $1$, it prunes $42\%$ of attention coefficients to exactly zero, and those edges are selectively the wrong ones, restoring them costs $7.1$ points, while random pruning at the same rate costs $13.0$ more. Project page: https://kleyt0n.github.io/ltga

</details>


### [63] [Federated generative event models for tokenized electronic health records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02939)
*Michael C. Burkhart,Luke Solo,Inhyeok Lee,S'Khaja Charles,Zewei "Whiskey" Liao,Kaveri Chhikara,Dema Therese,Wan-Ting Liao,Catherine A. Gao,William F. Parker,Brett K. Beaulieu-Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在三个独立医疗系统的122,251次ICU住院记录上评估了联邦训练的分词生成式事件模型（GEMs），证明其较传统监督模型（如LightGBM）具有显著更高的跨站点可迁移性，同时联邦学习可接近集中式训练的性能，但跨机构数据异质性仍是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录基础模型受限于机构间数据孤岛，且跨站点迁移时性能显著下降。现有方法缺乏在大规模多中心数据上系统评估联邦训练的生成式模型（GEMs）的可迁移性和实用价值。

Method: 将三个医疗系统的ICU住院数据统一为标准化的Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format；训练分词生成式事件模型（GEMs）；在12个入ICU后24小时的临床预测任务上，系统比较了站点内、跨站点、集中式和联邦式（FedAvg、FedAvgM）训练的ROC-AUC和PR-AUC性能，并对比传统监督模型LightGBM。

Result: GEMs在站点内和跨站点ROC-AUC上均取得最高均值；其平均跨站点惩罚仅为0.025 ROC-AUC和0.027 PR-AUC，远低于LightGBM的0.079和0.089。联邦学习（FedAvg和FedAvgM）性能接近集中式GEM训练，且大多数收益在5-10轮通信内即可获得。但集中式多站点训练的收益相比完全本地训练仅略有提升，仅在本地数据有限时体现明显优势。

Conclusion: 联邦GEM训练在技术上是可行的，能保留大部分集中式训练的性能。但核心开放挑战在于学习可迁移的表征，将更大但异质的跨健康系统数据转化为可靠的目标站点收益。

Abstract: Electronic health record foundation models are limited by institutionally siloed data and substantial performance degradation under cross-site transfer. We evaluated federated training of tokenized generative event models (GEMs) across 122,251 intensive care hospitalizations from three independent health systems harmonized to the Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format. Models were assessed on 12 post-24-hour clinical prediction tasks using within-site, cross-site, centralized, and federated training configurations. GEMs achieved the highest mean within-site and cross-site ROC-AUC and were substantially more transportable than conventional supervised models: their average cross-site penalties were 0.025 ROC-AUC and 0.027 PR-AUC, compared with 0.079 and 0.089 for LightGBM. Federated Learning (FedAvg and FedAvgM) approached the performance of centralized GEM training, with most gains obtained within 5-10 communication rounds. However, centralized multi-site training provided only modest improvements over complete local training. Multi-site models were most useful when local training data were limited, with their advantage narrowing as institutional data accumulated. These findings show that federated GEM training is technically feasible and preserves most centralized performance, but that the main open challenge is learning transportable representations to translate larger, but heterogeneous data from multiple health systems into a reliable target-site benefit.

</details>


### [64] [Sedentary Behavior Classification for Wearable Sensors with a CNN-BiLSTM Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02946)
*Yuliang Chen,Weiwei Shi,Jingjing Zou,Rong Zablocki,Animesh Kumar,Jordan A. Carlson,Sheri J. Hartman,Mikael Anne Greenwood-Hickman,Paul R. Hibbing,Marta Jankowska,Jay Yang,Arun Kumar,Loki Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将基于臀部佩戴加速度计数据训练的深度学习模型（CHAP，CNN-BiLSTM架构）迁移到手腕佩戴加速度计数据进行坐姿与非坐姿分类。零样本迁移时性能下降，但通过微调可显著恢复性能，且优于从头训练的Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 久坐行为与健康风险密切相关，但基于可穿戴传感器的姿势分类仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在手腕位置（佩戴更舒适但信号更复杂）。作者希望探索臀部训练模型能否迁移到手腕数据，以避免大规模手腕标注数据的采集成本。

Method: 使用CHAP这一CNN-BiLSTM模型（最初为臀部加速度计开发），在iWatch数据集上评估两种迁移策略：(1) 零样本迁移（直接在手腕数据上测试）；(2) 使用不同数量的带标注手腕数据进行微调。同时与从头训练的Transformer模型进行对比。

Result: 臀部训练的模型在臀部数据上表现优秀且无需重新训练，但在手腕数据上因传感器放置位置变化导致准确率下降。对CHAP进行微调始终优于从头训练的Transformer模型。

Conclusion: 臀部预训练可作为手腕部署的良好起点，但需要针对手腕数据进行特定适配以处理更高的信号变异性，这验证了迁移学习在可穿戴传感器跨位置部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate detection of sedentary behavior is important for studying health risks related to prolonged sitting, but posture-based classification remains challenging with wearable sensors, especially at the wrist. We study whether a deep learning model trained on hip-worn accelerometer data can transfer to wrist-worn accelerometer data for sitting versus non-sitting classification. We use CHAP, a CNN-BiLSTM model originally developed for hip accelerometers, and evaluate its zero-shot performance on wrist data as well as its adaptation through finetuning with varying amounts of labeled wrist data. Experiments are conducted on the iWatch dataset with ground-truth posture labels derived from wearable cameras. The hip-trained model performs strongly on hip data without retraining, but accuracy drops on wrist data due to sensor placement shift. Finetuning CHAP provides consistent advantages over transformer models trained from scratch. These findings suggest that hip-based pretraining provides a useful starting point for wrist deployment, while highlighting the need for wrist-specific adaptation to handle higher signal variability.

</details>


### [65] [ATFlash: Per-RoPE-Wavelength Attention Windows for Compute/Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02947)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RoPE波长分组的距离窗口剪枝方法。该方法按RoPE频率对的波长比例设定距离阈值，对超出阈值的query-key内积项进行剪枝。与滑动窗口不同，所有key仍可通过低频对保持可达性。该方法的剪枝率与输入无关，具有闭式的O(log N)形式，且与MInference等动态稀疏方法正交。在Qwen2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-3B上可剪枝37-48%的内积项，top-1匹配率保持96-98%，KL散度在10^-3 nat水平。在RULER、OpenAI-MRCR等长上下文基准上性能基本保持，并在FlashAttention-4和FlashInfer中实现，最高获得1.31倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE的注意力分数可精确分解为各2D旋转频率对的贡献之和，而每个频率对的波长限制其能区分位置的距离范围。据此，作者希望利用这一结构性洞察来设计一种比现有动态稀疏方法更高效、输入无关的注意力剪枝策略。

Method: 提出"per-RoPE-wavelength distance window"方法：对每个RoPE频率对，按照与其波长成比例的距离阈值剪枝query-key内积项。剪枝发生在query-key收缩轴的切片上，不改变在线softmax递归过程，可无缝移植到FlashAttention-4 prefill和FlashInfer decode中。该token级选择与频率级剪枝正交，可叠加在其他稀疏方法之上。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-3B上，原生上下文长度内剪枝37-48%的query-key内积项；top-1匹配率保持96-98%，输出分布KL为10^-3 nat量级。RULER、OpenAI-MRCR、LongCodeQA和∞Bench等长上下文基准上绝对分数基本保持。在RTX PRO 6000上Llama达到最大1.29倍加速（128K上下文），Qwen2.5-7B-1M端到端在1M上下文下剪枝57%内积项并获1.31倍加速。

Conclusion: 基于RoPE波长结构的固定模式剪枝窗口是可行的：它实现了输入无关的剪枝率（对数级闭式表达），不需要动态稀疏方法所需的在线计算，同时保持了高质量的注意力近似，且在主流注意力库中可以最小改动实现显著加速，是一种实用且高效的注意力稀疏化方案。

Abstract: The attention score with rotary position embeddings (RoPE) decomposes exactly into a sum over its 2D-rotation frequency pairs, and each pair's wavelength limits how far it can discriminate position. Aligned with this structure, we propose the per-RoPE-wavelength distance window: it prunes the query--key inner-product terms beyond a wavelength-proportional distance. Unlike a sliding window, every key remains reachable, at least through the low-frequency pairs. The reduction rate is input-independent, with a closed form logarithmic in the sequence length $N$, in contrast to dynamic-sparse methods like MInference. Such token-level selection is orthogonal to our frequency-level pruning. The window can therefore be applied on top of those methods. On Qwen2.5-0.5B and Llama-3.2-3B, the window prunes 37--48\% of the query--key inner-product terms within each model's native context length. Relative to full attention, the top-1 match rate stays at 96--98\% and the mean output-distribution KL at the $10^{-3}$-nat level on LongBench-v2 contexts. We examine absolute scores on long-context benchmarks such as RULER, OpenAI-MRCR, LongCodeQA, and $\infty$Bench: they are broadly preserved. We implement the window as a slice of the query--key contraction axis, leaving the online-softmax recurrences untouched, and port it with minimal diffs into the released FlashAttention-4 prefill and FlashInfer decode. On RTX PRO 6000 with Llama, both ports outpace stock with gains growing with context length, up to $1.29\times$ at 128K. End to end on Qwen2.5-7B-1M, with 57\% of the inner-product terms pruned, the speedup reaches $1.31\times$ at a 1M-token context.

</details>


### [66] [Rubrics as Privileged Information for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02948)
*Deepika Bablani,Ajay Gupta,Wanming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将在线自蒸馏（OPSD）从可验证领域（如数学）扩展到开放式生成任务，使用软特权信息（soft PI）——即由医生创建的评分标准（rubrics）——来替代硬参考答案作为蒸馏的监督信号。实验表明，在开放式生成中，软rubric PI相比硬参考完成PI提供更有效的训练信号，并在HealthBench和ResearchQA基准上优于rubric-as-reward强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏（OPSD）在数学等可验证领域表现良好，因为真实答案作为硬特权信息可以结构性约束有效续写。然而开放式生成任务没有唯一正确答案，如何将OPSD扩展到这类任务是一个关键挑战。现有方法仅将rubrics作为标量奖励用于强化学习，未能充分利用其丰富的token级偏好信息，本文旨在探索软rubric作为密集特权信息的更优利用方式。

Method: 提出使用评分标准（rubrics）作为软特权信息（soft PI）进行蒸馏，模型同时在学生和教师角色中运作：学生基于上下文生成回复，教师利用rubric中的偏好结构指导学生的输出分布。与硬参考完成PI（只给出有效响应集合中的一个点）不同，rubric PI捕捉了所有有效响应所共享的偏好结构，避免了过度约束。在训练中，通过匹配的配方和KL方向对rubric-PI与参考-PI和rubric-as-reward RL进行系统比较。

Result: 在HealthBench基准上，Rubric-PI蒸馏相比Rubric-as-Reward RL最高提升+0.10绝对分数；在匹配配方和KL方向下，相比参考PI蒸馏在三个模型上提升+0.034到+0.079绝对分数。在RubricHub Science语料训练的ResearchQA评估中，软rubric PI达到66.6%，优于参考PI蒸馏（64.2%）和RaR RL（57.6%）。结果覆盖Qwen和Llama模型家族。

Conclusion: 软rubric作为特权信息（RuPI）比硬参考完成PI更有效，因为参考完成只是有效响应集合中的一个点，向其蒸馏会过度约束学生；而rubric指定了所有有效响应共享的偏好结构。Rubric PI相较rubric-as-reward RL提供了更丰富的token级密集监督信号，在开放式生成任务中取得了显著更优的性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD), where a single model acts as both student and teacher with different contexts, has shown promise in verifiable domains like math, where hard privileged information (PI) in the form of ground-truth answers structurally constrains valid continuations. We extend OPSD to open-ended generation using soft PI in the form of rubrics that guide preferences but admit many valid responses. Rubrics have served as scalar rewards for reinforcement learning (RL); we show that they provide substantially richer signal as dense PI for distillation, and contrary to intuition, soft rubric PI provides a larger and more effective training signal on student roll-outs than hard reference completion PI in this regime. A reference completion is one point in a set of valid responses, so distilling towards it over-constrains the student, while rubrics specify the preference structure shared across the set of valid responses. We show the effectiveness of using rubrics as PI for open-ended generation across Qwen and Llama model families and show that it outperforms rubric-as-reward (RaR) RL using HealthBench, a benchmark that grades open-ended health responses against physician-created rubrics, providing dense token-level supervision for open-ended tasks; RuPI beats RaR RL by up to +0.10 absolute score and, under matched recipe and KL direction, beats reference-PI by +0.034 to +0.079 absolute score across three models. We further show that these findings generalize to training on the RubricHub Science corpus and evaluating on ResearchQA: soft rubric PI outperforms both reference-PI distillation and RaR RL (66.6% vs. 64.2% and 57.6%).

</details>


### [67] [Schedule-Informed Temporal Fusion Forecasting of Hourly Airport Security-Checkpoint Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02950)
*Yinxiao Zhang,Sen Wang,Yi Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将航班时刻表转换为安检吞吐量预测信号的框架。利用2023-2024年TSA吞吐量数据和Cirium Diio航班时刻表，采用截断泊松核将国内外座位容量分配至起飞前各小时，生成到达强度信号，再结合Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）模型预测亚特兰大机场安检点的小时级吞吐量。六小时直接预测的加权平均绝对百分比误差为9.33%，优于RNN（12.16%）和LSTM（11.37%），且峰期误差最低；递归更新下24-96小时预测误差保持在10.60%-11.04%之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安检点排班需要准确预测安检需求的时间分布，但航班时刻表记录的是起飞时间而非旅客到达安检点的时间，二者之间存在时间错位。现有方法难以将已知的航班计划转化为安检点可用的小时级客流预测信号，且通常需要旅客-航班匹配数据，实用性受限。

Method: 使用截断泊松核将国内外座位容量分布到起飞前各小时，生成与时间对齐的预安检到达强度信号；将生成的信号与历史吞吐量、计划活动和时间变量共同输入Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）模型进行预测。模型按时间顺序训练，以2024年7-12月作为测试集，与RNN和LSTM基线在五个随机种子上比较。

Result: 六小时直接预测中，TFT达到9.33%的加权MAPE，优于RNN（12.16%）和LSTM（11.37%），且在峰期时段误差最低。采取六小时递归更新时，24-96小时预测误差在10.60%-11.04%之间，但更长预测视界中包含的有效预测起点较少。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在不依赖旅客-航班匹配数据的前提下，将航班起飞计划转化为可解释的预起飞安检负荷信号，支持提前排班、开放安检通道和多日安检点规划。但需注意，观测到的吞吐量反映的是实际处理能力而非未受限的到达需求，预测结果应结合当地人员配置、容量、排队和等待时间信息进行解读。

Abstract: Checkpoint staffing requires accurate forecasts of when screening demand will occur, yet flight schedules record departure times rather than passenger arrival times at security checkpoints. This study develops a framework that converts known flight schedules into temporally aligned signals for forecasting hourly checkpoint throughput. Using 2023-2024 Transportation Security Administration throughput data and Cirium Diio flight schedules for Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport, domestic and international seat capacity was distributed across pre-departure hours using truncated Poisson kernels. A Temporal Fusion Transformer then combined these schedule-derived arrival-intensity signals with historical throughput, scheduled activity, and temporal variables. Models were trained chronologically, with July-December 2024 reserved for testing, and evaluated against recurrent neural network and long short-term memory models across five random seeds. For direct six-hour forecasts, the proposed model achieved a weighted mean absolute percentage error of 9.33%, compared with 12.16% for the recurrent neural network and 11.37% for long short-term memory, while also producing the lowest errors during peak periods. With six-hour recursive updates, errors remained between 10.60% and 11.04% across 24-96 hour horizons, although longer horizons contained fewer valid forecast origins. By transforming scheduled departures into interpretable pre-departure screening-load signals without requiring passenger-flight matching, the framework supports advance staffing, lane-opening, and multiday checkpoint planning. Because observed throughput reflects realized processing rather than unconstrained arrivals, the forecasts should be interpreted together with local staffing, capacity, queue, and wait-time information.

</details>


### [68] [SP3O: Reinforcement Learning from Segment Preferences without Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02951)
*Evan Assmus,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SP3O算法，一种无需奖励模型、无需评论家、基于梯度的偏好强化学习（PbRL）算法，支持片段级偏好反馈。该算法通过离线重要性采样构建策略价值差异估计器，并使用PPO型损失函数计算策略梯度，适用于随机MDP和长轨迹任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PbRL方法存在以下问题：(1) 无奖励模型的方法仅限于bandits或确定性MDP（如DPO、P3O），或使用零阶无梯度优化，收敛速度慢于基于梯度的方法；(2) 现有无奖励模型算法几乎只使用轨迹级反馈，当轨迹较长时人类评估成本高。片段（segment）更短，更容易比较和评估。

Method: SP3O算法包含三个关键设计：(1) 无奖励模型策略——不训练奖励模型，直接从偏好反馈中优化策略；(2) 无评论家——不需要价值函数估计器；(3) 基于梯度——使用PPO型损失函数计算策略梯度。核心技术是利用片段级偏好反馈，通过离策略重要性采样构建精确的策略价值差异估计器，并分析了片段长度选择的权衡。

Result: 在机器人控制和LLM微调场景中，SP3O相比其他PbRL/RLHF算法展现了更好的性能，特别是在长时域任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: SP3O为偏好强化学习提供了一种新的范式：结合片段级偏好反馈与基于梯度的优化，既降低了人类评估负担，又保证了收敛速度，是首个同时具备奖励模型无关、评论家无关和梯度性质且兼容片段偏好的PbRL算法。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) for general stochastic MDPs often requires training a reward model. Existing reward-model-free methods are either restricted to bandits or deterministic MDPs, such as DPO or P3O, or use zeroth-order, gradient-free optimization, which in general exhibits a slower convergence rate than gradient-based algorithms. Furthermore, existing reward-model-free preference-based RL algorithms almost exclusively use trajectory-level feedback, which can require significant effort from a human evaluator when trajectories are long. On the other hand, segments are much shorter, so they are easier to compare and evaluate. In this paper, we introduce a novel reward-model-free, critic-free, and gradient-based PbRL algorithm compatible with segment preferences named Segment Pairwise Proximal Policy Optimization (SP3O). SP3O utilizes segment-level preference feedback to construct an accurate policy value difference estimator via off-policy importance sampling, and then uses the estimator to compute the policy gradient via a PPO-type loss function. We provide a theoretical basis for the algorithm and analyze the tradeoff in choosing the segment length. We also evaluate it experimentally against other PbRL/RLHF algorithms in robotic control and LLM finetuning settings to show its improved performance, especially in long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Inverted Detection and Control in Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02957)
*Max Torop,Aria Masoomi,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现“反转转向向量”（ISV）现象：一些高判别性的转向向量与正向表征对齐，但持续促进相反行为。作者提供了几何特征化、提出无需生成的ISV鉴别方法，并通过符号翻转改进基于ITI的转向流水线，在27/30个实验中提升了0.9%至138%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转向向量（SV）广泛用于控制大语言模型输出中的概念表达（如真实性）。其核心假设是SV相对于概念是线性可判别的，即表现出该概念的表征与SV的方向对齐程度更高。然而，作者发现某些高判别性的SV即使与正向表征对齐，却会一致地促进相反行为，这一“反转检测-控制”现象打破了传统假设，需要被揭示和解决。

Method: 1) 对ISV效应进行几何特征化，发现沿这些方向进行转向会在判别性下游头中系统性地将表征推向“概念缺失”的状态，甚至在解码之前就已发生。2) 基于这一分析，提出一种无需生成或响应打分即可区分ISV的判别方法。3) 利用该方法进行目标性符号翻转，将其集成到基于推理时干预（ITI）的检测驱动转向流水线中。

Result: 改进方法在27/30个实验中取得更优结果，提升幅度从+0.9%到+138%不等。实验覆盖Gemma 3 12B、Qwen 2.5 14B和Olmo 3 7B三种模型及5个不同概念。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了转向向量中隐藏的“反转检测-控制”不匹配现象，证明了ISV可以通过几何分析在不生成文本的情况下被识别，且简单的符号翻转能显著提升基于ITI的转向流水线性能，为转向向量的可靠使用提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Steering vectors (SVs) are widely used to influence the expression of concepts (e.g., truthfulness) in large language model outputs. A key assumption underpinning SVs is that they are linearly discriminative with respect to the concept: representations of texts that exhibit the concept are more aligned with the SV than those that do not, motivating shifts along the positive or negative SV direction to respectively promote or suppress the concept. In this work, we identify an inverted detection-control phenomenon in which some highly discriminative SVs that are aligned with positive representations can consistently promote the opposite behavior. We refer to such vectors as inverted-steering vectors (ISVs). We provide a geometric characterization of ISVs' effects, finding that steering along these directions systematically pushes representations in discriminative downstream heads as if the concept were absent, even prior to decoding. Motivated by this analysis, we propose an approach for distinguishing ISVs without requiring generation or associated response scoring. This enables targeted sign flips, which we use to improve a foundational detection-based steering pipeline via Inference Time Intervention (ITI). Our approach improves results in 27/30 experiments, ranging from +0.9% to +138%. We evaluate our findings on Gemma 3 12B, Qwen 2.5 14B, and Olmo 3 7B across 5 concepts.

</details>


### [70] [Scaling an Autoregressive Transformer for Single-Cell Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02961)
*Aleksandr Sharipov,Yusif Mukhtarov,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究单细胞基因表达向量的自监督生成任务，提出了一种基于因果transformer和量化VAE分词器的模型，实现给定细胞类型向量集生成额外基因表达向量。作者评估了生成向量的生物学保真度和预训练损失的缩放行为，首次为单细胞基础模型拟合了双指数缩放律和计算最优前沿，并讨论了该模型在扰动响应预测中的微调应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基因表达数据的生成建模是一个重要但研究不足的问题。需要一种能够生成高保真单细胞基因表达向量的自监督模型，同时理解该模型的扩展（scaling）行为，以便在数据量和计算资源有限的情况下优化模型规模和训练策略，为单细胞基础模型提供理论指导。

Method: 采用因果Transformer与学习的量化VAE（向量量化变分自动编码器）分词器相结合，通过交叉熵损失进行训练。在评估时，以保留的某细胞类型基因表达向量为条件，生成该细胞类型的基因表达向量，并与真实分布比较。通过改变模型参数量和训练数据量研究缩放特性，拟合双指数缩放律并计算计算最优前沿。

Result: 首次在单细胞基础模型中发现并拟合了联合双指数缩放律，确定了计算最优前沿；生成的基因表达向量在生物学保真度上得到了表征验证。

Conclusion: 该架构能够生成生物学保真的单细胞基因表达向量，且其缩放行为遵循可预测的双指数规律，为后续扩展模型和优化资源分配提供了依据。该预训练模型有望通过微调用于扰动响应预测任务。

Abstract: We study a self-supervised generation task for single-cell gene expression vectors: given a set of vectors from a cell type, we aim to generate additional gene expression vectors of that cell type. For this task we characterize both the biological fidelity of the generated gene expression vectors and the scaling behavior of the pretraining loss. The model is a causal transformer paired with a learned quantized VAE tokenizer, trained with a cross-entropy loss. To evaluate the model, we condition it on held-out gene expression vectors of a cell type and generate vectors of gene expression, comparing the resulting distribution over gene expression vectors to the ground truth distribution of that cell type. We study the scaling properties of the proposed architecture by varying the number of trained parameters and the amount of training data. To our knowledge, we find the first jointly-fit two-exponent scaling law and compute-optimal frontier for a single-cell foundation model. Finally, we discuss how this pretrained model could be finetuned for perturbation response prediction.

</details>


### [71] [A Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator for Transient Magnetization Prediction in Power Magnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02965)
*Yachao Zhu,Qiujie Huang,Sinan Li,Yang Li,Gang Lei,Jianguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息混合神经算子（PI-HNO），一种用于高频高功率密度变换器中磁芯暂态磁化预测的紧凑材料专用神经模型。模型结合局部循环分支和Preisach启发的全局分支，通过B-H能量一致性正则化实现暂态H(t)预测，在MagNetX数据库的14种铁氧体材料上仅用4777个可训练参数即达到均值和95分位B-H能量一致性误差1.92%和7.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频高功率密度变换器中磁芯常受非正弦磁通密度波形驱动，包含快速跃迁、小磁滞回线、直流偏置和温度变化等复杂条件。这些条件下稳态磁芯损耗公式和单值材料曲线无法充分捕获暂态磁化响应，需要一种能够基于测量的B(t)-H(t)历史数据精确预测暂态磁化行为的模型。

Method: PI-HNO采用物理信息混合神经算子架构：1) 局部循环分支负责边界状态表征和速率依赖的响应演化；2) Preisach启发的全局分支提取波形级磁滞上下文；3) 通过B-H能量一致性正则化约束模型，确保预测的B-H轨迹在能量意义上与物理规律一致。模型输入为测量的B(t)序列以及工作条件信息，输出预测的H(t)序列及重构的B-H轨迹。

Result: 在MagNetX暂态数据库上对14种铁氧体材料训练材料专用模型，PI-HNO实现了序列精度与B(t)-H(t)能量一致性之间的紧凑权衡，均值和95百分位B(t)-H(t)能量一致性误差分别为1.92%和7.60%，每个模型仅需4777个可训练参数。消融实验表明局部分支、全局分支和能量感知正则化组件对暂态磁化预测均有独特贡献。

Conclusion: PI-HNO作为一种紧凑的物理信息神经算子，能够在复杂激励条件下实现高精度的暂态磁化预测，兼顾序列精度与能量一致性，为高频功率变换器的磁芯损耗建模提供了一种高效且物理可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Magnetic components in high-frequency, high-power-density converters are increasingly driven by non-sinusoidal flux-density waveforms with fast transitions, minor-loop operation, dc bias, and temperature variation. Under these conditions, steady-state core-loss formulas and single-valued material curves cannot fully capture transient magnetization responses. This work proposes the Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator (PI-HNO), a compact material-specific neural model with B-H energy-consistency regularization for core-loss-oriented transient magnetization prediction. Given the measured B(t)-H(t) history, the input B(t) series over the prediction interval and operating-condition information, PI-HNO predicts the H(t) series and the corresponding reconstructed B-H trajectory. The model integrates a local recurrent branch for boundary-state representation and rate-dependent response evolution with a Preisach-inspired global branch that extracts waveform-level hysteresis context. Evaluation on the MagNetX transient database using material-specific models for 14 ferrite materials demonstrates that PI-HNO achieves a compact trade-off between sequence accuracy and B(t)-H(t) energy consistency, with the mean and 95th percentile B(t)-H(t) energy consistency errors of 1.92% and 7.60%, respectively, using only 4777 trainable parameters per model. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the local, global, and energy-aware regularized components provide distinct contributions to transient magnetization prediction.

</details>


### [72] [AcceptMoE: Commitment-Weighted Self-Sizing Verifier Expert Sets for Efficient MoE Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02989)
*Shuang Liang,Hao,Chen,Zhiwen Mo,Qianzhou Wang,Guoyu Li,Lingxiao Ma,Wayne Luk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AcceptMoE是一个面向MoE目标模型的验证器侧专家选择器，通过组合目标路由器分数与离线估计的提交概率，自动调整每个验证块的可选专家数量，无需用户指定专家预算。在卸载场景下根据缓存驻留状态而非自然路由来条件化专家资格。实验表明其平均准确率仅比EAGLE-3低0.27个百分点，但吞吐量达到EAGLE-3的1.29倍（全GPU内存）和2.06倍（物理专家卸载），同时将主机到设备的流量减少了73.6%-77.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码中对MoE目标模型进行并行验证时，树中所有节点选中的专家并集都会被激活，但只有少量节点最终被接受。令牌数、激活专家并集大小和专家权重传输量是三种不同的成本度量：减少令牌负载并不一定按比例缩小专家并集，且在卸载情况下传输流量还取决于缓存驻留状态。现有方法忽略了这一关键区别，需要一种能够针对专家激活和传输成本进行优化的验证器设计。

Method: AcceptMoE作为一个验证器侧专家选择器，核心做法包括：(1)结合目标路由器分数与离线估计的提交概率来选择专家；(2)自动调整每个验证块的合格专家数量，免去用户指定专家预算的需求；(3)在卸载场景中，基于缓存驻留状态而非预测自然路由来条件化专家资格，避免额外的预取专家权重开销。该方法选择性地约束目标专家资格，以控制激活专家并集和传输流量。

Result: 在12个模型-任务对（涵盖三个MoE目标和四个基准）上的实验显示：平均准确率仅比EAGLE-3投机解码（自然路由）低0.27个百分点；使用SGLang在批大小为一的情况下，全GPU内存驻留时吞吐量达到基线的1.29倍；物理专家卸载时达到2.06倍；主机到设备传输流量减少73.6%-77.1%。

Conclusion: AcceptMoE证明了在MoE投机解码中，通过显式地管理专家选择而不是单纯依赖路由器的自然输出，可以在几乎不损失准确率的情况下大幅提升吞吐量和减少传输流量。其核心洞见是将令牌计数、专家并集大小和权重传输量作为独立的成本维度进行优化，而约束专家资格虽然会改变模型分布，但实际精度损失非常有限。

Abstract: Speculative decoding verifies a tree of draft tokens in one target-model forward pass. For a mixture-of-experts (MoE) target, however, parallel verification can activate the union of the experts selected by all tree nodes, even though only a small subset of those nodes reaches the accepted output. Token count, activated-expert union size, and expert-weight traffic are therefore distinct cost measures: reducing the token workload need not shrink the expert union proportionally, and under offloading, transfer traffic also depends on cache residency. We introduce AcceptMoE, a verifier-side expert selector that combines target-router scores with offline-estimated commitment probabilities and automatically adjusts the number of eligible experts for each verification block, eliminating the need for a user-specified expert budget. Under offloading, AcceptMoE conditions expert eligibility on cache residency instead of predicting natural routes and prefetching the corresponding expert weights. Although constraining target-expert eligibility changes the model distribution, across 12 model-task pairs spanning three MoE targets and four benchmarks, AcceptMoE's mean accuracy is 0.27 percentage points lower than that of EAGLE-3 speculative decoding with natural routing. Served with SGLang at batch size one, it reaches 1.290 times the throughput of this baseline with all expert weights in GPU memory, and 2.06 times under physical expert offloading, while reducing host-to-device traffic by 73.6 percent to 77.1 percent.

</details>


### [73] [Joint Affine Spectral Shaping: Coupling Weight and Bias Updates Beyond Weight-Only Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02991)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究矩阵谱优化器中权重与偏置分离更新的问题，提出联合规则化逆（JRI）方法，将仿射层视为联合动量矩阵并进行整体谱映射，同时生成权重和偏置更新。在BERT-mini上，JRI相较权重专属谱整形将测试准确率从85.562%提升至85.738%，并得到22-种子独立复现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵谱优化器（如Muon）重塑权重更新谱，但通常将向量值偏置委托给独立的优化器。作者质疑这种分离是否真正中性，即是否有必要将偏置与权重联合进行谱优化。

Method: 将每个仿射层建模为联合动量矩阵A=[M_W, αm_b]，对完整矩阵施加封顶的规则化逆谱映射，同时产生权重和物理偏置更新。通过严格的五种子消融实验比较：精确SVD Muon、权重专属逆整形、仿射探针逆整形、以及提出的联合规则化逆（JRI）。

Result: 权重专属逆整形将测试准确率从84.903%提升至85.562%，测试损失从0.3479降至0.3345。允许偏置影响联合SVD但保留独立Adam偏置更新并未优于权重专属逆整形。使用变换后的偏置联合更新将准确率提升至85.738%，测试损失降至0.3291，所有五种种子均优于探针基线。JRI在峰值性能窗口保留了权重更新范数，同时将偏置更新范数从0.02095降至0.00301，边界函数占比从86.58%降至78.97%，余弦从+0.030变为-0.137。22-种子独立复现得到85.743%准确率。

Conclusion: 联合仿射谱分配是权重专属谱优化的小但一致的扩展，表明权重与偏置联合谱整形能带来可复现的性能提升。

Abstract: Matrix spectral optimizers reshape weight-update spectra but usually delegate vector-valued biases to a separate optimizer. We study whether this separation is neutral. We formulate each affine layer as a joint momentum matrix $A=[M_W,αm_b]$ and apply a capped regularized-inverse spectral map to the complete matrix, producing both the weight and physical bias updates. A strict five-seed ablation on a four-layer BERT-mini trained from scratch on IMDb compares exact-SVD Muon, weight-only inverse shaping, affine-probe inverse shaping, and the proposed joint regularized inverse (JRI). Weight-only inverse shaping raises validation-loss-selected test accuracy from $84.903\pm0.242\%$ to $85.562\pm0.308\%$ and lowers selected test loss from $0.3479$ to $0.3345$. Allowing bias to alter the joint SVD while retaining an independent Adam bias update does not improve over weight-only inverse shaping. Using the transformed bias jointly raises selected test accuracy to $85.738\pm0.180\%$ and lowers test loss to $0.3291$, with all five seeds improving relative to the probe baseline. During the peak-performance window, JRI preserves the eligible weight-update norm while reducing the bias-update norm from $0.02095$ to $0.00301$, lowers boundary-function share from $86.58\%$ to $78.97\%$, and changes the cosine between weight-induced boundary motion and explicit bias from $+0.030$ to $-0.137$. An independent 22-seed replication yields $85.743\pm0.203\%$ selected test accuracy. These results identify joint affine spectral allocation as a small but consistent extension to weight-only spectral optimization.

</details>


### [74] [A Graph Signal Processing Perspective on Numerical Sequence Representations in LLM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03015)
*Jiajun Bao,Zihao Qi,Toni J. B. Liu,Gurbir Arora,Raphaël Sarfati,Nicolas Boullé,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从图信号处理视角研究大语言模型在上下文学习中进行数值推理时，数值信息在模型内部表征中的组织方式。研究发现：随着上下文长度增加，表征会依据输入动态复杂度产生更清晰的分化——简单输入产生全局连通性更强的注意力图和平滑的隐状态信号，复杂输入则产生更局部的图结构和更宽频谱的隐状态信号，且这些内部特征跨模型家族保持一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于大语言模型上下文学习中的数值推理研究主要集中在输出层面的评估（如预测误差），但对数值信息在LLM表征内部的组织方式理解不足。作者希望通过研究内部表征结构，填补这一认知空白。

Method: 采用图信号处理视角：将注意力机制视为在token上诱导的加权图，token的隐藏状态作为图节点上的信号。使用定量图频谱诊断方法（如图谱分析）和定性的token图可视化来研究表征结构。通过分析不同输入动态复杂度和不同上下文长度下的表征差异，进行跨模型家族的比较研究。

Result: 研究发现：随着上下文长度增加，表征对不同输入动态复杂度的区分度更清晰。简单输入产生更强的全局连通性和更平滑、频谱集中的隐状态信号；复杂输入产生更局部的图和更宽频谱、更高高频能量的隐状态信号。这些内部签名具有系统性、依赖上下文的特点，并在不同模型家族间保持一致。

Conclusion: LLM在数值上下文学习中的内部表征存在系统性的、与输入动态复杂度相关的图谱信号特征，这些特征在跨模型家族中保持一致性，表明数值推理在LLM内部存在可辨识的、结构化的表征模式。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated in-context learning (ICL) capabilities for numerical inference over sequences serialized as text. Prior work has identified and characterized this form of numerical inference primarily through output-level evaluations such as prediction error. However, how numerical information is organized within LLM representations remains much less understood. To study this internal organization, we adopt a graph signal processing perspective in which attention induces a weighted graph over tokens, while token hidden states define signals on its nodes. Quantitative graph-spectral diagnostics and qualitative token-graph visualizations reveal that representations become more clearly differentiated by input dynamical complexity as context length increases. Simpler inputs produce attention-induced token graphs with stronger global connectivity and smoother, spectrally concentrated hidden-state signals, whereas more complex inputs produce more localized graphs and hidden-state signals with broader spectral support and greater high-frequency energy. Together, these findings point to systematic, context-dependent internal signatures associated with numerical ICL that are conserved across model families.

</details>


### [75] [Paired Recipient-based Evaluation of Survival Prediction for Deceased Donor Kidney Transplants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03017)
*Misaki Matsuura,Mohammadreza Nemati,Dulat Bekbolsynov,Stanislaw Stepkowski,Kevin S. Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了利用生存预测模型预测肾移植移植物存活时间，并提出了一种新颖的"配对受体"评估框架，通过与同一供体匹配的不同受体进行比较，来衡量改变受体对移植结果的因果反事实收益。研究发现五种不同复杂度的生存预测模型均达到约60%的配对受体准确率，并指出C-index在该场景下的局限性，提出更具临床相关性的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肾移植术后移植物存活预测对供体-受体的术前匹配具有重要意义，可帮助识别更兼容的供受体组合以改善移植预后。然而现有评估方法（如C-index）无法充分反映真实世界中的供受体配对分配场景，缺乏对"更换受体能带来多少临床收益"这一核心问题的量化评估。

Method: 1) 基于SRTR（Scientific Registry of Transplant Recipients）数据库中已故供体肾移植数据，训练五种不同复杂度的生存预测模型（从线性模型到深度学习模型）；2) 提出一种新颖的配对受体评估框架，比较从同一已故供体接受肾脏的两个受体的移植物结局，从而评估改变受体的反事实收益；3) 将配对准确率转化为可解释的移植后寿命年数增益，并与C-index进行对比分析。

Result: 五种不同复杂度的生存预测模型在配对受体评估框架下均取得约60%的准确率；该准确率被转化为移植后获得的寿命年数这一可解释指标；同时证明了C-index在该场景下存在重大局限性，而所提出的配对受体准确率指标更能反映真实世界分配情景且更具临床相关性。

Conclusion: 生存预测模型在肾移植供受体匹配中具有一定价值，但单纯依赖传统的C-index指标评估模型性能会掩盖其在实际分配中的表现；基于同一供体的配对受体评估框架为临床决策提供了更相关且更可解释的模型性能衡量标准，为未来优化供受体匹配策略提供了方向。

Abstract: There has been significant interest in using machine learning algorithms to predict kidney transplant outcomes, such as the number of years until a graft inevitably fails. These prediction algorithms could possibly be used for pre-transplant donor-recipient matching to identify more compatible donors and recipients and thus improve post-transplant outcomes. In this study, we explore the use of survival prediction models trained on deceased donor kidney transplant data from the Scientific Registry of Transplant Recipients (SRTR). We propose a novel paired recipient-based evaluation framework that compares graft outcomes between two recipients who received kidneys from the same deceased donor, allowing us to evaluate the counterfactual benefit of changing the recipient for a certain donor. We find that five different survival prediction models, ranging in complexity from linear to deep learning-based models, all result in ~60% paired recipient-based accuracy. We further translate this accuracy into an interpretable quantity of post-transplant years gained. We also highlight major limitations of the commonly used concordance index (C-index) metric for evaluating survival prediction accuracy in this setting and demonstrate that our proposed paired recipient-based accuracy metric is more clinically relevant and better reflects real-world allocation settings.

</details>


### [76] [PLAN: Parallel Liquid-Inspired Approximation Network for Efficient Representation Learning in Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03041)
*Dhivya Dharshini Kannan,Wei Zhang,Jieyi Bi,Yingpeng Du,Tianjun Wei,Jie Zhang,Zuming Liu,Anupam Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PLAN（Parallel Liquid-inspired Approximation Network），一种轻量级表示学习框架，将液态神经网络（LNN）的连续动态状态演化重构成离散化、可并行化的形式，用于柔性作业车间调度（FJSP）。PLAN将状态演化与上下文聚合解耦，作为即插即用的骨干网络，可推广到随机FJSP和动态FJSP变体。在确定性、随机和动态FJSP基准上，PLAN相比SOTA基线分别降低平均makespan 1.2%、1.4%和2.3%（最高达10.2%），推理延迟分别降低13.2%、31.7%和26.9%（最大69.2%），参数仅用基线的22%-47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力架构的深度强化学习方法在FJSP上虽达到SOTA性能，但面临参数冗余和推理延迟过高的问题，难以随问题规模扩展。液态神经网络虽提供参数高效的替代方案，但其固有的顺序动态特性成为计算效率瓶颈。论文旨在解决参数效率与计算效率之间的权衡问题。

Method: PLAN将连续液态状态动态重构为离散化、可并行的公式。在结构上将状态演化与上下文聚合解耦：液态启发更新负责主要的状态演化表示，轻量级上下文聚合模块提供互补的全局上下文。PLAN作为即插即用的通用骨干网络，可搭配紧凑随机模块处理随机FJSP，并替代异构图Transformer处理多面向动态FJSP。

Result: 在确定性、随机和多面向动态FJSP基准上，PLAN相比相应SOTA基线分别将平均makespan降低1.2%、1.4%和2.3%，其中某基准设置下改进达10.2%。平均推理延迟分别降低13.2%、31.7%和26.9%，最大实例上降低69.2%，参数仅需基线的22%-47%。

Conclusion: PLAN通过将液态神经网络的连续动态并行化，成功实现了参数高效与计算高效的统一，在多个FJSP变体上同时优于SOTA基线的调度质量和推理效率，证明了其作为通用轻量级骨干的有效性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches for flexible job shop scheduling (FJSP) heavily rely on attention-centric architectures to achieve state-of-the-art performance. However, these models suffer from excessive parameter counts and prohibitive inference latency as problem scales expand. While liquid neural networks (LNNs) offer a parameter-efficient alternative for modeling adaptive state evolution, their inherently sequential dynamics bottleneck computational efficiency. To resolve this trade-off, we propose PLAN (Parallel Liquid-inspired Approximation Network), a lightweight representation learning framework that reformulates continuous liquid-state dynamics into a discretized and parallelizable formulation. PLAN structurally decouples state evolution from context aggregation, where liquid-inspired updates handle the primary evolving state representation, and a lightweight context aggregation module provides complementary global context. Furthermore, PLAN acts as a versatile, plug-and-play backbone that generalizes to complex FJSP variants, pairing with a compact stochastic module for stochastic FJSP and replacing heavy heterogeneous graph transformers in multi-faceted dynamic FJSP. Extensive evaluations across deterministic, stochastic, and multi-faceted dynamic FJSP benchmarks show that PLAN reduces the average makespan by 1.2%, 1.4%, and 2.3%, respectively, compared with the corresponding state-of-the-art baselines, with the improvement reaching 10.2% in one benchmark setting. PLAN also reduces average inference latency by 13.2%, 31.7%, and 26.9%, respectively, with a maximum reduction of 69.2% on the largest instances, while using only 22$-$47% of the baseline parameters.

</details>


### [77] [Exploiting Separability in Multi-Scale Grey-Box Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03045)
*Joshua E. Hammond,Tyler A. Soderstrom,Brian A. Korgel,Michael Baldea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灰盒优化的双层贝叶斯优化（Bilevel BO）方法，将决策变量分为黑盒变量和白盒变量，通过外层BO优化黑盒变量、内层全局优化求解白盒子问题，从而精确满足白盒约束，无需惩罚函数或近似手段。在13个基准问题上显著降低了遗憾值，并减少了迭代次数和墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒优化问题中，决策变量自然地划分为黑盒变量（作为昂贵黑盒函数的输入）和白盒变量（由显式闭式方程控制，且依赖黑盒函数输出）。现有方法通常忽略这种可分性，或通过惩罚函数、机会约束、矩近似等方式处理约束，导致精度损失或计算开销大。

Method: 提出双层重构：外层使用贝叶斯优化（BO）仅针对黑盒变量优化标量目标，高斯过程代理定义在黑盒变量空间而非全部变量空间；内层通过全局优化求解白盒子问题。只要内层优化器收敛到可行点，白盒约束即可精确满足，无需惩罚函数、机会约束或矩近似。

Result: 在13个基准问题上，双层BO比现有方法获得更低的遗憾值，且所需迭代次数和墙钟时间更少。该优势对初始化集合大小、探索参数和内层求解器选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双层贝叶斯优化有效利用灰盒问题的可分性结构，在约束满足精确性和优化效率上均优于传统方法，且表现出较强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider grey-box optimization problems where the decision variables naturally partition into black-box variables (as arguments to an expensive black-box function) and white-box variables, governed by a set of explicit, closed-form equations that also depend on the output of the black-box function. We exploit this separability through a bilevel reformulation: an outer Bayesian optimization (BO) to optimize the scalar objective as a function of black-box variables alone, while an inner problem solves the white-box subproblem via global optimization. The Gaussian process surrogate used in BO is therefore defined rather than and white-box constraints are satisfied exactly whenever the inner optimizer converges to a feasible point---without penalty functions, chance constraints, or moment approximations. On a suite of 13 benchmark problems, bilevel BO achieves lower regret, with fewer iterations and wall clock time. This advantage is robust to initialization set size, exploration parameters, and inner-solver choice.

</details>


### [78] [Revisiting TD Target Aggregation under Uncertainty in Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03069)
*Lipeng Zu,Xiaonan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SADQ（Successor Rollout Aggregation Deep Q-Network），通过在Q-learning的时序差分（TD）目标形成过程中引入一步rollout预测来正则化下一状态动作值的聚合方式，从而缓解bootstrapping导致的过估计问题，在不改变底层学习框架的前提下提高训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQN中基于贪心最大化（max算子）的TD目标聚合对Q值估计噪声高度敏感：当Q值不确定时，最大化算子确定性地偏向最大估计值而不考虑其可靠性，通过bootstrapping不断放大误差，导致价值函数过估计。

Method: SADQ利用学习到的动力学模型进行一步rollout预测，用该预测来引导下一状态候选动作之间的比较，为Q-learning中的聚合步骤引入额外结构。具体地，采用混合Bellman更新（mixed Bellman update），在模型误差减小的条件下保持标准固定点，同时衰减不可靠的最大值。

Result: 理论分析表明SADQ以逐点方式（pointwise manner）减少bootstrap引起的过估计。实验上，SADQ在经典控制任务、真实世界向量环境以及Atari基准测试中，相较强DQN变体一致地改善了训练稳定性。

Conclusion: SADQ作为一种简单而对Q-learning的简易修改，通过在TD目标聚合中引入基于动力学模型的rollout引导，有效正则化了目标形成过程，在保持算法框架不变的前提下显著提升了训练稳定性，为缓解DQN过估计问题提供了一条实用且理论可保证的路径。

Abstract: Deep Q-Networks (DQNs) learn value functions through bootstrapped temporal-difference updates, where future returns are approximated using a greedy maximization over next-state action values. While effective, this aggregation rule is inherently sensitive to estimation noise: when Q-values are uncertain, the maximization operator deterministically favors the largest estimate, regardless of its reliability, leading to amplified errors through bootstrapping. In this work, we propose the \textbf{S}uccessor Rollout \textbf{A}ggregation \textbf{D}eep \textbf{Q}-Network (SADQ), a simple modification to Q-learning that regularizes how the TD target is formed. SADQ uses one-step rollout predictions from a learned dynamics model to guide the comparison among candidate next-state actions, introducing additional structure into the aggregation step without altering the underlying learning framework. The resulting mixed Bellman update attenuates unreliable maxima while preserving the standard fixed point under diminishing model error. We provide theoretical analysis showing that SADQ reduces bootstrap-induced overestimation in a pointwise manner. Empirically, SADQ consistently improves training stability across classical control tasks, real-world vector-based environments, and Atari benchmarks when compared to strong DQN variants.

</details>


### [79] [SynEnergy: Anomaly Semantic-Guided Diffusion for Synthetic Energy Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03087)
*Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ravikumar Gelli,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SynEnergy，一个两阶段扩散框架，用于生成保留异常事件的细粒度能源消耗数据。第一阶段通过异构图表征学习提取区域特定异常语义，第二阶段将异常语义注入扩散去噪过程生成真实序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度能源消耗数据对需求预测、需求响应规划和电网可靠性评估至关重要，但隐私和数据共享限制阻碍了数据获取。现有合成数据生成方法虽能复现整体分布和周期性模式，却常常平滑化或低估了由极端天气、基础设施故障和行为变化引发的异常事件。异常事件稀疏、在时空中局部化，并受地理邻近性和区域属性的异质依赖影响，因此保留这些异常具有挑战性。

Method: SynEnergy采用两阶段扩散框架：(1) 异构图异常语义学习（HG-ASL）阶段，通过联合建模城市区域间的空间和属性依赖，从稀疏残差结构中提取区域特定的异常语义；(2) 异常语义引导扩散（AS-Diff）阶段，将学习到的异常语义注入去噪过程，生成保留异常模式且真实的消费序列。该方法支持单个区域的可控生成，并可自然扩展到城市级规模。

Result: 在四个真实世界能源消耗数据集上，与11个通用和能源专属生成基线相比，SynEnergy将异常保留保真度平均提升12.21%，下游任务质量提升2.96%，同时保持与基线相当的总体生成保真度。

Conclusion: SynEnergy通过显式建模和学习异常语义并将其融入扩散生成过程，有效解决了合成能源数据中异常事件被平滑化的问题，实现了既逼真又保留异常模式的能源消耗数据生成。

Abstract: Fine-grained energy consumption data are essential for applications such as demand forecasting, demand response planning, and grid reliability assessment. However, access to such data is often restricted by privacy concerns and data-sharing constraints, motivating growing interest in synthetic energy data generation. Although existing methods can reproduce overall consumption distributions and recurring temporal patterns, they often smooth out or underrepresent anomalous events caused by extreme weather, infrastructure failures, and behavioral shifts. Preserving these events is challenging because they are sparse, localized in time and space, and shaped by heterogeneous dependencies across geographical proximity and regional attributes. To address these challenges, we propose SynEnergy, a two-stage diffusion-based framework for anomaly-preserving energy consumption data generation. The first stage, Heterogeneous Graph-based Anomaly Semantic Learning (HG-ASL), extracts region-specific anomaly semantics from sparse residual structures by jointly modeling spatial and attribute dependencies across urban regions. The second stage, Anomaly Semantic-guided Diffusion (AS-Diff), injects the learned anomaly semantics into the denoising process to generate realistic consumption sequences while preserving anomalous patterns. This design enables controllable generation for individual regions and scales naturally to city-wide settings. We evaluate SynEnergy on four real-world energy consumption datasets against 11 general-purpose and energy-specific generation baselines. Experimental results show that SynEnergy improves anomaly preservation fidelity by an average of 12.21% and downstream quality by 2.96%, while maintaining competitive overall generation fidelity compared to baselines.

</details>


### [80] [SMOPD: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03092)
*Wen Wang,Jiahua Bao,Tu Yongsiqi,Yihao Liu,Haotian Zhou,Haoxuan Ma,Mengyu Zhou,Wenkui Fan,Junwei He,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMOPD（Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation），一种两阶段训练方法，用于解决多奖励强化学习中稀疏奖励和稠密奖励粒度不均衡导致稀疏奖励优化信号不足的问题。该方法先通过奖励优先级配置训练多个奖励特化教师模型，再通过在线策略蒸馏将各奖励特化能力融合到单一学生策略中。实验表明在不同规模（1.5B、3B、7B）上均优于已有方法GDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GDPO方法通过在每个奖励维度聚合前分别做归一化来缓解多奖励信号相互掩蔽的问题，但其在奖励粒度差异显著时仍存在不足。具体而言，当模型同时接收稠密奖励（如0.1到1.0的细粒度分数）和稀疏奖励（如仅0或1的二元反馈）时，稀疏奖励提供的优化信号不足，导致其对应的能力无法得到有效强化。因此需要一种能够在不牺牲稠密奖励已学得能力的前提下，增强稀疏奖励优化信号的方法。

Method: SMOPD采用两阶段训练方法。阶段一（Specialize）：使用奖励优先级配置训练多个奖励特化教师模型，使得每个奖励维度都能在其信号可有效驱动优化的条件下被充分学习。阶段二（Merge）：利用在线策略蒸馏将各教师的奖励特化能力整合到一个学生策略中，同时保持任务层面的平衡优化。

Result: 在两类多奖励设置下进行了验证：互补奖励（工具调用准确性与格式）和冲突奖励（有益性与无害性）。实验结果显示SMOPD在1.5B、3B和7B三种骨干模型规模上均优于GDPO。

Conclusion: SMOPD通过奖励特化与在线蒸馏的联合策略，有效解决了多奖励强化学习中奖励粒度不均衡导致的信号掩蔽问题，验证了其在互补和冲突奖励场景下的通用有效性。

Abstract: We aim to improve model performance in multi-reward reinforcement learning training process. Existing Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO) has mitigated the issue of reward signals masking one another during direct scalarization by normalizing each reward dimension separately before aggregation. However, our experiments show that GDPO still struggles to balance reward signals with different granularities. Specifically, in some particular training tasks, the model may receive a dense reward that assigns fine-grained scores ranging from 0.1 to 1.0, together with a sparse reward that provides only binary feedback of either 0 or 1. In such cases, we find that the sparse reward may provide an insufficient optimization signal, preventing its corresponding capability from being effectively reinforced. Therefore, how can we strengthen the optimization signal from the sparse reward without sacrificing the capability already learned from the fine-grained reward? To overcome this limitation, we propose Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation (SMOPD), a two-stage training method for multi-reward optimization. Stage1-Specialize: SMOPD first employs reward-priority configurations to train multiple reward-specialized teachers, allowing each reward to be learned under conditions where its signal can effectively drive optimization. Stage2-Merge: SMOPD then utilizes online policy distillation to combine the reward-specialized capabilities of these teachers into a single student policy, while maintaining balanced task-level optimization. To validate our method, we conduct experiments on two multi-reward settings: complementary rewards(tool-calling accuracy and format) and conflicting rewards (helpful and harmless rewards). Based on above settings, SMOPD outperforms GDPO across 1.5B, 3B and 7B backbones.

</details>
