Motivation: 尽管图神经网络在众多下游图任务上达到最先进性能，但在聚类任务上其相对于传统描述性和推断性聚类算法的优势远未确定，尤其是在效率和恢复准确性方面存在争议。作者希望厘清这一问题，并探索将理论扎实的社区检测方法视为时间图粗粒度（池化）算子的可能性。

Method: 本文通过三个相互关联的视角构建分析框架：(1) 原理层面，通过共享的谱基础与随机块模型（SBM）中的可检测性阈值连接图学习与社区检测；(2) 原语层面，通过GPU加速的时间后端使谱聚类与多切片模块度优化在时间图上可高效计算；(3) 池化层面，将原理驱动的社区检测视为时间图上具有理论基础的粗粒度算子。

Result: 结果表明：(1) 在图属性缺失或较弱时，算法方法仍是合适工具，其瓶颈是可扩展性而非准确性；(2) 当结构、时间与属性信号对齐时，神经模型最具竞争力；(3) GPU加速原语使时间聚类具有可扩展性，为理论支撑的池化提供了一条可行路径，但社区粗粒度是否保留下游学习所需的动态信息仍是一个核心开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文表明在时间图聚类中，传统算法方法在属性信息不足时仍具优势，神经模型需在多种信号一致时方能展现优势；GPU加速的谱聚类和模块度优化原语为理论基础的粗粒度池化提供了可行路径，但社区粒度的粗粒度化对下游学习任务中动态信息保留的影响仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: This work focuses on the problem of learning on temporal graphs, with particular emphasis on the task of clustering: obtaining coarse-grained representations by aggregating information from nodes, edges, and temporal dynamics - a task related to pooling in machine learning on graphs, or community detection in network science. Although graph neural networks reach state-of-the-art performance across many downstream graph tasks, their advantage over established descriptive and inferential clustering algorithms is far less settled, especially under demands of efficiency and recovery accuracy. We frame this tension through three linked perspectives: principles, connecting graph learning and community detection through shared spectral foundations and detectability thresholds in stochastic block model regimes; primitives, making spectral clustering and multislice modularity optimization tractable through GPU-accelerated temporal backends; and pooling, viewing principled community detection as a theory-grounded coarse-graining operator for temporal graphs. Our results indicate that algorithmic methods remain the appropriate tool where attributes are absent or weak - scalability rather than accuracy being the binding obstacle - while neural models are most compelling when structural, temporal, and attribute signals align. By making temporal clustering scalable, GPU-accelerated primitives suggest a route toward theory-grounded pooling, while raising a central question: when does community-based coarse-graining preserve the dynamics needed for downstream learning tasks?

</details>


### [114] [To Describe or Construct Statistical Learning Models Using the Category-theoretical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03706)
*Congwei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述报告，面向初学者总结经典统计学习模型和著名算法，并从范畴论视角提供理解统计学习模型的框架，旨在吸引包括基础数学在内的其他领域研究者参与统计学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习是机器学习和人工智能的主流领域，已产生大量可应用于现实问题的结果。然而，作者希望吸引基础数学等领域的其他研究者参与统计学习相关研究，因此需要一份面向初学者的综述，并提供新的视角（范畴论）来帮助理解统计学习模型，以降低交叉学科研究的门槛。

Method: 本文主要采用综述方法，系统性地总结经典的统计学习模型和著名算法；同时引入范畴论（category theory）作为新的理论视角，用于理解和抽象统计学习模型的内部结构与关系。

Result: 本文通过对经典统计学习模型和算法的系统整理与总结，为初学者提供了入门参考；同时提出了从范畴论角度审视统计学习模型的新视角，为跨学科研究者提供了新的研究切入点。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于为统计学习领域提供了一份面向非专业人士的综述性报告，并通过范畴论视角开辟了理解统计学习模型的新途径，最终目标是促进基础数学领域研究者与该领域的交叉合作。

Abstract: Statistical learning is a fascinating field that has long been the mainstream of machine learning/artificial intelligence. A large number of results have been produced which can be widely applied to real-world problems. It also leads to many research topics and also stimulates new research. This report summarizes some classical statistical learning models and well-known algorithms, especially for amateurs, and provides a category-theoretic perspective on understanding statistical learning models. The aim is to attract researchers from other fields, including basic mathematics, to participate in the research related to statistical learning.

</details>


### [115] [Amortized Interventional Forecasting for Multivariate CIR Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03715)
*Andreas Sauter,Sumit Sourabh,Drona Kandhai,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIR-ACTIVA框架，将均值回归的CIR随机过程与摊销式因果效应估计相结合，用于估计多元信用违约互换（CDS）利差系统的多时域分布因果效应（即外部冲击下的系统响应），并通过新的因果多元CIR数据生成过程提供成对的观测与干预真值进行训练与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIR模型虽广泛用于描述短期利率、CDS利差等均值回归时间序列，但只能刻画序列间的相关性联动，无法回答"当一个序列受到外部冲击时系统如何响应"这一因果问题。观测条件分布会与历史联动相混淆，无法用于反事实场景下的应激测试。

Method: 提出两方面的贡献：(1) 一个摊销式分布因果效应估计模型，将轨迹视为带时间戳的观测，在不针对每个场景重新训练的情况下预测多时域、校准的冲击响应；(2) 一个因果多变量CIR数据生成过程，提供现实市场无法获得的成对观测与干预真值。在CDS利差上实例化并校准该框架。

Result: 在合成真值上验证了CIR-ACTIVA的有效性（独立于模拟器与现实的匹配度），并通过回测生成的轨迹与真实CDS数据对比评估其实践基础。相较于观测性基线和摊销式因果推断基线，CIR-ACTIVA在联合分布的因果选择性和时域分辨的校准性上均领先；当干预律随时间变化时仍保持选择性，且收益集中于短时域。

Conclusion: 该框架为耦合利差系统开启了一类观测性预测器无法回答的"what-if"查询，包括CDS应激测试等场景，具有明晰的实践价值。

Abstract: Mean-reverting dynamics are pervasive in finance, and the Cox--Ingersoll--Ross (CIR) process is a standard model for the time series they produce, from short rates to credit default swap (CDS) spreads. Yet CIR models capture only \emph{correlated} co-movement, not \emph{causal} influence between series, so they cannot answer the system's response when one series is externally shocked, which observational conditionals confound with historical co-movement. We make two contributions. First, an amortized model for distributional causal effect estimation that frames trajectories as time-stamped observations and predicts the calibrated multi-horizon shock response without retraining per scenario. Second, a causal multivariate CIR data-generating process that supplies the paired observational and interventional ground truth that real markets cannot. We instantiate and calibrate the framework on CDS spreads as a testbed. CIR-ACTIVA's validity is established on synthetic ground truth, independent of how well the simulator matches reality, while practical grounding is assessed by backtesting the generated traces against real CDS data. Against observational and amortized causal-inference baselines, CIR-ACTIVA leads on both causal selectivity in the joint distribution and horizon-resolved calibration, retaining its selectivity once the interventional law varies over the horizon, with gains concentrating at short horizons. This opens up a class of what-if queries on coupled spread systems, CDS stress testing among them, that observational forecasters cannot answer.

</details>


### [116] [UNVaMP: Neural Knowledge Tracing with Variational Regularization of Latent Knowledge Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03811)
*Carson J. Cook,Ahmed J. Zerouali,Anthony Schmidt,Reginald Ziedzor,Paul Lin,Luke G. Eglington*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UNVaMP架构，一种统一神经变分知识追踪方法，通过整合学生-题目交互与内部记忆生成动态潜在知识表征，兼顾预测准确性与可解释性。纯神经配置(UNVaMP-MLP)在四分之三数据集上取得最佳预测性能，混合配置(UNVaMP-MIRT)在损失少量性能的前提下提供可解释的知识状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪领域长期存在预测准确性与模型可解释性之间的权衡。现有方法要么追求纯神经网络的预测性能但难以解释，要么依赖可解释但表达能力受限的传统测量模型。此外，现有方法缺乏对知识状态估计波动性的显式控制机制、不确定性的量化手段，以及对异构交互特征输入的灵活支持。

Method: UNVaMP采用变分推断框架，将学生-题目交互与内部记忆整合以生成潜在知识表征。架构可配置为纯神经模型(UNVaMP-MLP)或混合模型(UNVaMP-MIRT，采用1PL MIRT测量函数)。该方法提供对学习轨迹平滑度的显式控制、知识状态不确定性的量化，以及支持异构学生-题目交互特征的灵活输入规范。

Result: 纯神经配置UNVaMP-MLP在四个数据集中的三个上取得最强预测性能；混合配置UNVaMP-MIRT仅略逊于MLP版本，表明可解释性的预测代价较小。实验数据集上的辅助输入引发UNVaMP-MIRT预测行为的结构化变化；模拟研究表明UNVaMP在受控测量条件下产生行为良好的知识状态估计。

Conclusion: UNVaMP在预测性能与可解释性之间取得了有效平衡，既适用于真实教育系统，又能从学生-题目交互中恢复潜在结构，为知识追踪提供了一种兼具实用性与理论完备性的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Unified Neural Variational Measurement of Proficiency (UNVaMP) architecture, a knowledge tracing method that integrates observed student-item interactions with internal memory to produce evolving latent representations of student knowledge. These representations support accurate predictions of future responses while enabling explicit control over the smoothness of estimated learning trajectories. UNVaMP can be configured as either a purely neural model or a hybrid model that predicts responses through an interpretable measurement function over the latent space. We show that a pure neural configuration (UNVaMP-MLP) achieves the strongest predictive performance among compared models on three out of four datasets. Meanwhile, a hybrid configuration (UNVaMP-MIRT, using a 1PL MIRT measurement function) lags only slightly behind UNVaMP-MLP, indicating that the predictive cost of interpretability is modest.
  Beyond predictive accuracy, UNVaMP provides the following: a principled mechanism for controlling volatility when estimating student latent variables, quantification of uncertainty over student knowledge state estimates, and flexible input specification that supports heterogeneous student-item interaction features. In addition, the hybrid UNVaMP-MIRT configuration generates interpretable moment-in-time student knowledge state estimates. Using an experimental dataset, we show that auxiliary inputs induce structured changes in the predictive behavior of UNVaMP-MIRT, consistent with sensitivity to underlying structure beyond response correctness. Furthermore, through a simulation study, we show that UNVaMP yields well-behaved knowledge state estimates under controlled measurement conditions. In total, these results indicate that UNVaMP is both useful for real-world education systems and capable of recovering underlying structure from student-item interactions.

</details>


### [117] [Resume Means Resume: A Machine-Checked Conformance Contract for Checkpoint, Interrupt, and Resume Semantics in Workflow Persistence Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03836)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RESUME CONTRACT，定义了持久化API上关于执行恢复的六项性质及派生义务，并通过TLA+模型进行穷举验证；随后构建确定性、无LLM的测试工具，对五个主流智能体工作流框架进行实证探测，发现它们在中断/崩溃恢复语义上存在系统性违规（如LangGraph在真实SIGKILL下重复执行已持久化工作、CrewAI重执行已完成副作用方法等），且并发消费场景下所有框架均违反consume-once。最后提出REMIT参考序列器，其Verus验证的恢复核心与发布可执行程序逐行一致，修复fork与validity单元，并通过共享存储中的opt-in门在读取路径修复跨进程单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化执行状态使运行可中断、崩溃可恢复的框架必须决定"恢复"对已发生副作用意味着什么，但现有五大智能体工作流框架对此给出了不同答案，且没有提供机器可检查的契约，行为甚至违反其自身声明的片段。

Method: ①形式化定义RESUME CONTRACT（六项性质：前缀延续、效应精确一次、fork确定性、检查点有效性、消费一次、恢复确定性，外加fork意图与活性义务）；②用TLA+建立参考语义并穷举检查（扩展到7.4百万状态不变）；③用39格故障矩阵分离各框架独立性需求，其中consume-once的消费子句独立于其他六项；④构建确定性、无LLM的测试工具，在固定版本上测量五个框架；⑤提出REMIT参考序列器，Verus验证恢复核心与发布可执行程序逐行一致。

Result: LangGraph 1.2.9持久记录第二个恢复值却从不查阅，静默持久化schema无效状态，真实SIGKILL后重执行已持久化工作（中断时精确一次，崩溃时至少一次，同一个API上）；CrewAI 1.15.2重执行已完成副作用方法，与其书面声明相悖；pydantic-graph 1.x在节点中途崩溃后无法恢复；任意两个被探测框架均无一致的符合性画像。consume-once在顺序执行下成立，但并发投递下失败：k个进程恢复同一暂停中断会触发被门控效应k次，40格中36格饱和度为1.0，且失败跨主机。REMIT修复了fork与validity单元，跨进程单元在读取路径修复，移植的opt-in门在共享存储中声明消费，先服务一个竞争进程并以拒绝其他进程的方式在节点执行前完成修复。

Conclusion: 现有智能体工作流框架在执行恢复语义上缺乏机器可检查契约且行为普遍违规；RESUME CONTRACT提供了正式的规范基础，REMIT验证了该契约可通过Verus验证的核心加共享存储消费声明门机制在参考实现中满足，从而修复fork、validity及跨进程consume-once单元。

Abstract: A framework that persists execution state so a run can be interrupted, survive a crash, and continue must decide what a resume means for effects that already fired. Five widely deployed agent workflow frameworks answer differently, none exposes a machine-checkable contract, and behavior violates even the fragments they state. The RESUME CONTRACT states six properties over the persistence API (prefix continuation, effect exactly-once, fork determinism, checkpoint validity, consume-once, recovery determinism), plus fork-intent and liveness obligations. A TLA+ model checks a reference semantics exhaustively, unchanged at scaled bounds (7.4 million states); a 39-cell fault matrix yields the separating models independence requires, and consume-once splits, its consumption clause independent of all six others. A deterministic, LLM-free harness measures them at pinned releases. LangGraph 1.2.9 durably records a second resume value and never consults it, persists schema-invalid state silently, and re-executes durably recorded work after a real SIGKILL: exactly-once across interrupts, at-least-once across crashes, on one API. CrewAI 1.15.2 re-executes completed effect-bearing methods against its written claim; pydantic-graph 1.x cannot resume after a mid-node crash; no two probed frameworks share a conformance profile. Consume-once holds sequentially and fails under concurrent delivery: k processes resuming one parked interrupt fire the gated effect k times, saturation 1.0 in 36 of 40 cells, and the failure crosses hosts. REMIT, a reference sequencer whose Verus-verified recovery core is line-identical to the shipped executable, repairs the fork and validity cells. The cross-process cell is repaired at the read path, and that repair ships: an opt-in gate claims consumption in the shared store, serving one racer and refusing the rest before any node executes.

</details>


### [118] [FedCritic-MIMO: Communication-Efficient Serverless Federated Critic Learning for Massive-MIMO Resource Control in Open and Disaggregated 6G RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03852)
*Amin Farajzadeh,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCritic-MIMO，一种面向开放可分解6G RAN的通信高效的serverless联邦多智能体强化学习框架。它通过干扰感知图上的对等共享critic参数交换（无需集中式训练或参数服务器），实现多小区massive-MIMO OFDMA场景下的用户调度、功率分配、波束成形和干扰协调，相比基线在性能-通信权衡上最优，并减少76%的critic通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G RAN走向开放和分解架构，控制器需要以独立可部署的cell级单元形式协作管理无线资源。传统集中式训练或参数服务器聚合方式面临通信开销大、可扩展性差、集中式轨迹收集成本高等问题。需要在无共享trainer、无集中式聚合的条件下实现多智能体协调，同时兼顾通信效率与学习性能。

Method: FedCritic-MIMO采用serverless联邦多智能体强化学习架构：各基站保留本地actor与个性化critic组件，仅在干扰感知图上对等交换共享critic参数；通过无线感知事件触发机制、自适应层wise top-k稀疏critic交换（带错误反馈）和均衡干扰感知融合实现协作。论文还建立了固定策略、冻结目标critic回归模型下均衡压缩对等critic递归的条件有限时间平稳性与共识保证。

Result: 在强干扰耦合的reuse-1仿真中，FedCritic-MIMO在所有基线（启发式、独立学习、集中式训练、通信消融）中取得最佳性能-通信权衡：最高held-out吞吐量、改善用户速率分布和平均SINR、提高QoS满意度、每传输比特干扰成本最低，并将critic通信开销相对于未压缩分布式critic交换降低76%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明serverless交换兼容共享critic参数即可协调RAN控制器，无需集中式轨迹收集或参数服务器聚合。FedCritic-MIMO为6G开放分解RAN中的AI原生资源控制提供了一种通信高效且有理论保证的分布式协作方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes FedCritic-MIMO, a communication-efficient serverless federated multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for AI-native resource control across independently deployable cell-level controllers in open and disaggregated 6G RANs. Controllers share no trainer, retain local actors and personalized critic components, and exchange only compatible shared critic parameters. FedCritic-MIMO targets reuse-$1$ multi-cell massive-MIMO OFDMA deployments, where RAN controllers jointly manage user scheduling, per-stream power allocation, beamforming, interference, and long-term QoS with limited inter-controller signaling. Each base station locally executes its actor without centralized training or actor federation, while critic knowledge is exchanged peer-to-peer over an interference-aware graph. It enables this collaboration through wireless-aware event triggering, adaptive layer-wise top-$k$ sparse critic exchange with error feedback, and balanced interference-aware fusion. We establish conditional finite-time stationarity and consensus guarantees for the balanced, compressed peer-to-peer critic recursion under a fixed-policy, frozen-target critic-regression model. In strongly interference-coupled reuse-$1$ simulations, FedCritic-MIMO achieves the best performance-communication tradeoff among heuristic, independent-learning, centralized-training, and communication-ablation baselines. It achieves the highest held-out throughput, improves user-rate distribution and mean SINR, increases QoS satisfaction, and attains the lowest interference cost per delivered bit among learning baselines. It reduces critic-communication overhead by $76\%$ relative to uncompressed distributed critic exchange. These results demonstrate that serverless exchange of compatible shared critic parameters can coordinate RAN controllers without centralized trajectory collection or parameter-server aggregation.

</details>


### [119] [Quantization Effects on Biomedical LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03854)
*Anton Rasmussen,Hong Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了Mistral-7B三种变体在PubMed RCT句子分类任务上的实现选择（提示模板、标签映射和评分规则）对分类校准和准确率的影响。关键发现是概率提取协议（均值vs求和token对数似然评分）主导了表观校准，且提示模板选择产生的准确率差异（7-24个百分点）与模型级效应相当甚至更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器语言模型用作分类器时，预测类别概率依赖一系列实现选择（提示模板、verbalizer、评分规则），但这些选择很少被视为实验变量，导致研究结果可能被实现细节所混淆，尤其在校准评估中。

Method: 对Mistral-7B的三种变体（Base、BioMistral、Instruct）在PubMed RCT句子分类（n=2000）上进行受控评估，涵盖FP16、INT8、INT4三种精度和四种答案文本提示模板，并比较求和与均值token对数似然两种评分规则，同时使用温度缩放进行校准后处理，以PubMedBERT微调模型作为参考。

Result: 求和vs均值token对数似然评分会反转模型的校准排名（BioMistral ECE从0.097升至0.289，Instruct从0.237降至0.096）；提示模板带来7-24个百分点的准确率差异；INT8量化对BioMistral和Instruct影响仅1-2个百分点但base模型在某些模板上达+4.2个百分点；温度缩放仅对求和评分有效。

Conclusion: 提示模板设计和评分归一化是评估解码器语言模型校准时的一阶实验决策，其影响可与模型级效应相提并论甚至更大，研究者在报告分类结果时必须将这些实现选择作为关键变量予以声明和控制。

Abstract: When decoder language models are used as classifiers, predicted class probabilities depend on implementation choices, including the prompt template, verbalizer (label-to-token mapping), and scoring rule, that are rarely treated as experimental variables. We present a controlled evaluation of three Mistral-7B variants (Base, BioMistral, and Instruct) on PubMed RCT sentence classification (n=2000) under FP16, INT8, and INT4 precision using four answer-text prompt templates. Our primary finding is that the probability extraction protocol dominates apparent calibration. Switching from summed to mean token log-likelihood scoring reverses the calibration ranking between models: BioMistral average expected calibration error increases from 0.097 to 0.289, whereas Instruct decreases from 0.237 to 0.096, while accuracy changes by less than 1 percentage point for the specialized models but 4-6 percentage points for the base model. Prompt template choice produces accuracy differences of 7-24 percentage points, comparable to or larger than model-level effects. On one template, BioMistral outperforms Instruct although the overall mean favors Instruct by only 1.3 percentage points. For BioMistral and Instruct, INT8 quantization changes accuracy and F1 by only 1-2 percentage points relative to FP16, whereas the base model shows larger INT8 effects on some templates (up to +4.2 percentage points). INT4 produces heterogeneous but non-catastrophic effects. Temperature scaling reduces expected calibration error under summed scoring for both models but only for that scoring rule. A fine-tuned PubMedBERT reference achieves 82.7% accuracy but uses about 176000 labeled training examples, precluding direct comparison. These results demonstrate that prompt template design and scoring normalization are first-order experimental decisions when evaluating decoder language model calibration.

</details>


### [120] [Bi-semantic Chemical Embedder for Joint Representation Learning of SMILES and Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03855)
*David Ming Segura,Jeremy Goumaz,Joshua W. Sin,Bojana Ranković,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CheMatE，一种基于ModernBERT的化学嵌入模型，通过两阶段训练（续训掩码语言建模+Matryoshka对比学习）统一分子结构与领域自然语言语义，在分子性质预测和科学语言理解任务上达到竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在NLP中表现卓越，SMILES文本表示也已成功将Transformer架构扩展到化学领域。然而，领域自适应预训练常导致模型对化学语法过拟合，灾难性地遗忘其基础语义能力。这一矛盾迫切需要一种方法能够在化学语义与通用语义之间取得平衡。

Method: CheMatE基于ModernBERT骨干网络，采用两阶段训练流程：（1）在构建的大规模SMILES注释长上下文科学文献语料库（来自FineWeb和ChemPile，分别含10.4B和11.5B token）上进行持续掩码语言建模（MLM）；（2）通过Matryoshka对比学习阶段，使用从原始训练语料算法生成的SMILES-文本对，结合多负排序损失（MNRL）进行训练。整个设计使模型暴露于富含SMILES的科学文献，实现双语义（bisemantic）理解。

Result: 在分子性质预测和科学语言理解等多项下游任务上，CheMatE相比专用化学模型和通用语言模型基线均取得具有竞争力的性能，验证了定制数据集与顺序训练策略相结合能够产生鲁棒且高度可迁移的表示。

Conclusion: 通过在单一文本框架内有效统一分子结构与上下文语义信号，CheMatE成功弥合了化学领域自适应与通用语义保持之间的鸿沟，展示了双语义表示学习在科学文本理解中的潜力。

Abstract: Transformer models have revolutionized natural language processing (NLP), and text-based molecular representations like SMILES have successfully extended these architectures to chemistry. However, domain-adaptive pre-training often causes models to overfit to chemical syntax, catastrophically forgetting their foundational semantic capabilities. To address this challenge, we introduce CheMatE, a chemistry-oriented embedding model that jointly captures molecular structure and domain-specific natural language within the same representation space. Built on a ModernBERT backbone, CheMatE learns bi-semantic representations through a two-stage training procedure: continued masked language modeling (MLM) followed by a Matryoshka contrastive learning stage via Multiple Negative Ranking Loss (MNRL). First, we train the model using MLM on a novel, large-scale corpus of SMILES-annotated, long-context scientific documents that were constructed and curated from FineWeb and ChemPile (comprising 10.4B and 11.5B tokens, respectively). Subsequently, the model undergoes contrastive learning using a synthetic dataset of SMILES-text pairs algorithmically derived from our original training corpus. This design exposes the model to SMILES-enriched scientific literature, enabling bi-semantic understanding. We evaluate CheMatE across a range of downstream tasks covering molecular property prediction and scientific language understanding. Our results demonstrate that coupling our custom-curated datasets with this sequential training strategy yields robust, highly transferable representations. By effectively unifying structural and contextual signals within a single text-based framework, CheMatE achieves competitive performance across both specialized chemistry models and general-purpose language model baselines.

</details>


### [121] [CRS-Triage: Confidence- and Reliability-Aware Selective Triage under Incomplete Clinical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03862)
*Guan Qiang,Yushen Chen,Tianlong Liu,David Rotenberg,Ethan H. Kim,Fang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRS-Triage，一种置信度与可靠性感知的选择性分诊方法，用于在EHR数据不完整、不可靠和不一致的情况下预测患者病情严重程度，并通过置信度阈值选择性决定是否由模型做出决策，同时在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集上验证了其强预测性能和良好的风险-覆盖率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊需要短时间内做出可靠决策，但电子健康记录（EHR）数据常存在不完整、不可靠和不一致的问题，现有ML模型依赖完整可靠的数据进行预测，难以应对这些挑战。

Method: CRS-Triage采用置信度与可靠性感知的选择性分诊策略：分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性，联合考虑两种模态间的一致性来估计每次预测的置信度；通过将置信度与预设阈值比较，选择性决定由模型决策还是延迟病例；通过惩罚欠分诊错误，偏好将患者分配到略高的病情等级以降低漏诊高风险患者的风险。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集上的实验表明，CRS-Triage实现了强预测性能，提供了更好的风险-覆盖率权衡，并且在EHR数据不完整、退化或模态间不一致时仍保持可靠。

Conclusion: CRS-Triage有效解决了不完整和不一致EHR数据下的急诊分诊问题，通过置信度感知的选择性决策和过诊偏好策略提升了预测的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Emergency triage requires reliable decisions within a short time period. However, the available electronic health record (EHR) data, including structured data and clinical text, are often incomplete, unreliable, and inconsistent. This makes machine learning (ML)-based triage prediction more challenging, as existing ML models typically rely on complete and reliable EHR data to accurately predict patients' acuity levels. To address this, we propose confidence- and reliability-aware selective triage (CRS-Triage) to predict patients' acuity levels with a confidence score. By comparing the confidence score with a predefined threshold, CRS-Triage can selectively determine whether the model should make the decision or defer the case. Specifically, CRS-Triage separately evaluates the reliability of structured data and clinical text and then jointly considers the consistency between the two modalities to estimate the confidence of each prediction. Moreover, to reduce the risk of missing high-acuity patients, namely under-triage, CRS-Triage prefers to assign patients slightly higher acuity levels, namely over-triage, by penalizing under-triage errors. Experiments on the MIMIC-IV-ED dataset show that CRS-Triage achieves strong predictive performance. It also provides a better risk-coverage trade-off and remains reliable when the available EHR data are incomplete, degraded, or inconsistent across modalities.

</details>


### [122] [GENESIS: Towards Explainable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03868)
*Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kumar Kolla,Vishak K Bhat,Harsh Vardhan Singh Chauhan,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GENESIS，一个可解释的混合因果发现框架，通过将图构建分解为可审计的决策点（三节点结构基序评分、与观测证据逐步整合、仅在统计证据不足时调用领域知识），实现100%的决策可追溯性，同时在各样本规模下优于纯统计方法并与SOTA的LLM辅助方法性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纯统计方法在小样本下缺乏分辨结构歧义的能力，而LLM辅助混合方法虽然通过语义推理提升了结构恢复效果，但其推理对单条边决策的影响不透明。现实应用中不存在ground-truth DAG，每条边（包含或排除）必须能被独立解释。作者将此需求形式化为"决策可追溯性"，即每条推断的边必须由可审计的统计证据、马尔可夫毯一致性或显式领域推理支持。现有混合方法均无法满足这一关键要求。

Method: GENESIS框架分为两个阶段：(1) 首先识别并评分三节点结构基序（链、叉、对撞结构），建立透明的结构先验；(2) 将这些先验与观测证据逐步整合以逐步细化图，仅在统计证据不足时才调用领域知识。通过设计，每条边的决策都由可审计的证据来源解析，确保决策可追溯性。

Result: 实验表明，GENESIS在所有设置下实现100%的决策可追溯性。在此额外约束下，GENESIS在大多数基准数据集和所有样本规模下，在结构汉明距离（SHD）指标上一致优于纯统计CD方法，同时达到与最先进的LLM辅助方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: GENESIS证明"可解释性"可以作为因果发现中的一等公民目标来实现：以可审计的决策追溯性为约束并不会牺牲性能，反而能在保持与LLM辅助方法可比性能的同时，超越纯统计方法，并满足实际应用中对每条边决策独立合理解释的刚性需求。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) from observational data faces two fundamental challenges. First, purely statistical methods often lack the power to resolve structural ambiguities in low-sample regimes. Second, although LLM-assisted hybrid approaches improve structure recovery through semantic reasoning, the influence of that reasoning on individual edge decisions remains largely opaque. Consequently, existing hybrid methods fail to satisfy a fundamental requirement: explaining why a particular edge is included or excluded in the learned directed acyclic graph (DAG). This is critical in real-world applications, where no ground-truth DAG exists and every structural decision must be independently justified. We formalize this requirement as decision traceability, requiring every inferred edge to be supported by auditable statistical evidence, Markov Blanket consistency, or explicit domain reasoning. We propose GENESIS, an explainable hybrid CD framework that decomposes graph construction into interpretable decision points. GENESIS first identifies and scores three-node structural motifs, including chains, forks, and colliders, to establish transparent structural priors, then progressively refines the graph by integrating these priors with observational evidence, invoking domain knowledge only when statistical evidence is insufficient. By design, every edge decision is resolved through an auditable source of evidence. Experiments show that GENESIS achieves 100% decision traceability across all settings, establishing explainability as a first-class objective in causal discovery. Despite this additional requirement, GENESIS consistently outperforms purely statistical CD methods on the majority of benchmark datasets across all sample regimes in terms of Structural Hamming Distance (SHD), while achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art LLM-assisted approaches.

</details>


### [123] [Enhancing VLM Reward Models Through Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03875)
*Pyrros Koussios,Chenhao Li,Xin Chen,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构感知微调（SAFT），一种无需真值监督的自监督方法，通过LoRA适配器利用任务内在的结构先验来正则化VLM的潜在空间，在线精炼噪声的视觉-语言模型奖励信号，从而加速策略收敛并提升对齐质量（EPIC距离）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计奖励函数是强化学习的主要瓶颈；虽然VLM可作为奖励模型减少人工干预，但其产生的奖励信号往往噪声大、不可靠，限制了直接部署的效用。

Method: SAFT通过LoRA适配器在VLM潜在空间中引入内在结构先验进行正则化，以自监督方式在线优化不完美的奖励信号，无需地面真值监督。

Result: 在多种基座模型能力下，SAFT一致地消除了奖励噪声，实现更快的策略收敛，并显著提升EPIC对齐距离，表明失败往往源于结构脆弱性而非语义误解。

Conclusion: SAFT以任务固有的结构归纳偏置替代大量人工偏好标注，为稳定文本条件强化学习提供可扩展路径，并凸显了将任务结构作为通用归纳偏置的价值。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL). Recent work uses large foundation Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models, computing text-observation similarity to bypass manual reward engineering. Although promising, these rewards are often noisy and unreliable, limiting their direct utility during deployment. We present Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT), a simple, self-supervised method that refines these imperfect reward signals online without access to ground-truth supervision. SAFT leverages intrinsic structural priors to regularize the VLM's latent space via LoRA adapters. We rigorously evaluate SAFT across a spectrum of base model capabilities to demonstrate its versatility. Our results show that SAFT consistently denoises the reward landscape, yielding faster policy convergence and substantially improved alignment (EPIC distance) relative to the underlying base model, suggesting that failures can often be attributed to structural brittleness rather than semantic misunderstanding. By replacing extensive human preference annotation with structural inductive biases inherent to the task, SAFT offers a scalable path for stabilizing text-conditioned RL and underscores the broader value of incorporating task structure as a general inductive bias.

</details>


### [124] [Operationally Feasible Synthetic Power-Grid Scenarios via Learning the AC-Operable Joint Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03878)
*Chenhan Xiao,Xinyu He,Haoran Li,Hanghang Tong,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可行性感知的分布学习框架，通过分层扩散模型直接学习可AC运行的电网场景联合分布（拓扑、支路参数、负荷曲线），替代事后优化/验证策略，显著提升生成场景的AC可行性与事故鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成电网生成方法虽通过事后验证、优化或物理感知生成提升了结构真实性和运行可行性，但生成的场景仍可能AC可行性低、鲁棒性差，限制了在下游电力系统研究中的实用价值。核心痛点在于"先生成后约束"范式无法从根源保证运行可行性。

Method: 提出可行性感知的分层扩散分布学习框架：将AC潮流收敛性与运行约束嵌入扩散模型的分布学习过程中（而非事后约束）；采用三层分层架构，依次生成（1）拓扑与母线属性，（2）基于生成结构的支路参数，（3）基于网络结构与电气特性的负荷曲线；通过高效扩散采样直接产生运行可行的场景。

Result: 在基准系统上的实验表明，该框架显著提升了运行可行性与事故鲁棒性，同时保持较强的统计保真度，并消除了基于优化的后处理需求。

Conclusion: 将可行性约束前置嵌入生成模型本身，而非依赖事后处理，是提升合成电网场景实用价值的有效途径；分层设计使高维生成任务工程化分解为可控的子问题，兼顾可行性与统计保真度。

Abstract: Synthetic power-grid scenarios are essential for planning, resilience assessment, contingency analysis, and data-driven power-system applications. Recent synthetic grid generation methods have improved structural realism and operational feasibility by incorporating engineering knowledge through post-generation validation, optimization, or physics-aware generation. However, generated scenarios may still exhibit low AC feasibility and robustness, limiting their practical value for downstream power-system studies. This paper proposes a feasibility-aware distribution-learning framework that learns the AC-operable joint distribution of network topology, branch electrical parameters, and time-varying load profiles. Instead of enforcing feasibility after generation, the proposed framework incorporates AC power-flow convergence and operational constraints into hierarchical diffusion-based distribution learning. This enables the generator itself to produce operationally feasible grid scenarios through efficient diffusion sampling. The hierarchical architecture decomposes the high-dimensional generation task into three engineering-motivated stages: topology and bus-attribute generation, branch-parameter generation conditioned on the generated structure, and load-profile generation conditioned on both network structure and electrical characteristics. Experiments on benchmark systems demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves operational feasibility and contingency robustness while maintaining strong statistical fidelity and eliminating optimization-based post-processing.

</details>


### [125] [Omega-S: A Functional Resilience Index for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03887)
*Alberto Acedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omega-S是一种无需旧数据、Fisher矩阵或旧权重副本的即插即用惩罚项，仅从权重矩阵计算，在Llama-3-8B的LoRA微调中显著缓解灾难性遗忘（保留率62.9%→84.1%）。作者实测发现其机制实际可简化为对节点度方差的惩罚，即非方阵中的行幅与方向对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在新数据上微调会退化其先前学习的知识，即灾难性遗忘问题。现有的缓解方法（如EWC）需要旧任务数据、Fisher矩阵或旧权重副本，开销大且实现复杂。作者希望提出一个仅依赖当前权重矩阵、零额外存储、开销极小的正则化惩罚项。

Method: Omega-S从权重矩阵的Tr(A^3)构建拓扑目标，在现有训练循环中仅需三行代码。作者通过弹性分析将复合项分解为四个因子，实测其中三个对权重变化几乎不敏感（弹性≤1e-4），实际生效的是度方差项（弹性9e-3），即惩罚节点度的方差——在方阵模块中表现为行幅，在非方阵中表现为方向对齐。实验采用LoRA微调Llama-3-8B从代码域到散文域，以HumanEval的pass@1为保留能力指标，跨10个种子与无正则化、tuned weight decay、tuned EWC对比。

Result: 在10个种子中，Omega-S在9/10上优于无正则化（0.173→0.238 pass@1，符号检验p=0.011），保留率62.9%→84.1%；10/10胜过调优的weight decay（p=0.002），8/10胜过调优的EWC（p=0.014）。相反，保对比度的构造在所有10个种子上保留效果更差。重复相同配置（同种子同硬件）的保留率标准差为0.104。

Conclusion: Omega-S提供了一种低成本、无需历史数据的正则化方法，能有效缓解低秩微调中的灾难性遗忘。该工作强调以实测而非断言方式报告机制，坦诚披露方法名实不符之处，并首次量化了低秩语言模型微调中种子级保留率的标准差（0.104），提醒社区所有种子配对比较都受此噪声界限约束。代码与完整负结果记录已公开。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a large language model on new data degrades what it previously learned. We present Omega-S, a drop-in penalty computed from the weight matrix alone: it needs no previous-task data, no Fisher matrix and no stored copy of the old weights. It is three lines in an existing training loop and adds under 4% to the cost of a step.
  Retention. On Llama-3-8B with LoRA, fine-tuned from code to prose and measured by HumanEval over ten seeds, Omega-S retains more of the original capability than no regularisation on 9 of 10 seeds (0.173 -> 0.238 absolute pass@1; sign test one-sided p=0.011, Wilcoxon p=0.006), as a retention ratio, 62.9% -> 84.1%. It also beats tuned weight decay on 10 of 10 seeds (p=0.002) and tuned EWC on 8 of 10 (p=0.014), every arm re-measured in the same session.
  Mechanism, measured rather than asserted. Omega-S is topological by construction, its objective built from Tr(A^3), but we measured which of its four factors actually moves and three do not: their elasticity with respect to the weights is at or below 1e-4, against 9e-3 for the degree-variance term. As implemented, the composite reduces to a penalty on the variance of node degrees, which means row magnitude in square modules and directional alignment in non-square ones. We report this because a method whose name promises one thing and whose gradient does another should say so. We also enumerate the open design choices, including a contrast-preserving construction that does what it was designed to do and makes retention worse on all ten seeds.
  Repeating an identical configuration, same seed and same hardware, gives a standard deviation of 0.104 in retention ratio. We have not found this quantified for low-rank fine-tuning of language models, and it bounds every seed-paired comparison in this literature, ours included.
  Code, per-seed results and the full record of negative results are available.

</details>


### [126] [Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer in LLM Families: A Closed-Form Linear Mapping for Prefill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03893)
*Taekyung Heo,Rasoul Shafipour,Ritchie Zhao,Maximilian Golub,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Ritika Borkar,Makesh Tarun Chandran,Pantea Zardoshti,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨模型KV缓存迁移方法，通过在线性回归映射器将源模型的KV缓存迁移到目标模型，跳过prefill阶段，在多个模型对中保留73-98%的独立prefill准确率，速度快2.7-25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中经常在不同规模的模型之间切换（成本级联、会话中切换、路由），但每次切换都迫使接收模型从头重新执行prefill，造成大量计算开销。作者希望利用跨模型KV缓存共享来跳过prefill，从而降低切换成本。

Method: 首先发现匹配KV对（KV头数及每头维度相同）上跨模型KV存在显著的线性结构。基于此设计闭式岭回归映射器，分三步：1）为每个目标层选择top-k最具有预测性的源层并拼接其KV；2）映射前去掉keys的RoPE，使拟合位置无关且可跨上下文长度复用；3）在小规模校准集（500条FineWeb-Edu序列，每条1024 token）上拟合岭回归。遇到线性映射性能退化时引入非线性MLP恢复性能。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六对模型上，线性映射器在四对上保留了接收模型独立prefill准确率的73-98%，但两对退化严重。非线性MLP在这两对上恢复了最多+37百分点的HellaSwag保留率。映射器运行速度比重新prefill快2.7-25倍，并且在多轮切换下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 跨模型KV缓存迁移在实践中是可行的，且其核心机制是一种简单的线性结构加上必要的非线性补充。该技术使跨模型切换无需重新prefill，显著降低推理成本。

Abstract: Production deployments often swap between different-sized models in a family for cost-quality cascading, mid-conversation switching, and routing, and each swap forces the receiver to repay the prefill from scratch. We propose cross-model KV cache transfer, where the receiver reuses the source's KV cache, skipping prefill. We find that cross-model KV has substantial linear structure across matched-KV pairs, where source and target share KV head count and per-head dimension. On Qwen3 14B->32B, one source layer explains 56% of variance in the target's keys and 32% in values, rising to 79% and 65% with multiple source layers. Building on this, we design a closed-form ridge mapper that operates per head and proceeds in three steps. First, for each target layer we select the top-k most predictive source layers and concatenate their KV as input. Second, we strip RoPE from the keys before mapping, so the fit is position-free and reusable across context lengths. Third, we fit ridge regression on a small calibration set of 500 FineWeb-Edu sequences of 1,024 tokens each. Surprisingly, across six pairs in three families, this linear mapper retains 73-98% of the receiver's standalone-prefill accuracy on four pairs, while two degrade sharply. A nonlinear MLP recovers up to +37 pp HellaSwag retention on the failures. The mapper runs 2.7-25x faster than re-prefill and remains stable across multi-turn handoff, making cross-model KV cache transfer practical.

</details>


### [127] [Sparse Weight Decomposition for Efficient Circuit Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03913)
*Chuanhao Yan,Xuhan Huang,Yawen Duan,Zhenfei Yin,Hang Zhao,Bryan Dai,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏权重分解（SWD），将预训练Transformer的线性投影权重矩阵分解为两个稀疏因子，其共享中间坐标作为可寻址的电路单元，无需训练替代网络即可支持电路提取工作流，在更少数据和更低计算成本下达到与强基线相当的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集预训练Transformer不易自然暴露可解释的电路提取单元。现有方法或学习辅助稀疏表示，或训练稀疏模型，带来了大量额外计算，并可能在所分析的表示与原始预训练模型之间引入保真度差距。

Method: SWD将每个预训练线性投影的权重矩阵重新参数化为两个稀疏因子矩阵，两因子共享的中间坐标作为独立可寻址的电路单元。该参数化表示无需训练单独的替代网络，即可支持评分、选择和消融等电路提取工作流。此外还提出零数据变体，可直接用于机制可解释性分析。

Result: 在单矩阵替换中，SWD仅用不到基线1%的数据即达到与Transcoder等强基线相当的保真度；在匹配替换保真度下，SWD在GPT-2、Qwen2.5和Qwen3.5-27B上以更少的活跃读写边和选定单元达到相同的电路充分性与必要性目标。SWD在全模型替换（微调非零因子值）下依然有效。

Conclusion: SWD提供了一种高效、高保真的电路提取方案，消除了单独训练替代模型的需求，显著降低数据与计算开销，同时支持零数据场景，扩展了机制可解释性分析的适用范围。

Abstract: Dense pretrained transformers do not naturally expose interpretable units for circuit extraction. Existing approaches obtain such units by learning auxiliary sparse representations or training sparse models, incurring substantial additional computation while potentially introducing a fidelity gap between the representation being analyzed and the original pretrained model. We propose Sparse Weight Decomposition (SWD), which reparameterizes pretrained linear projections by factorizing each weight matrix into two sparse factors whose shared intermediate coordinates serve as individually addressable circuit units. Without training a separate replacement network, this parametric representation supports the same scoring, selection, and ablation circuit extraction workflow used for methods that learn sparse features. Across single-matrix replacements, SWD matches the held-out fidelity achieved by Transcoder and other strong baselines while using less than 1% of the data that those baselines use to train their replacements. For matched replacement fidelity, SWD reaches the same circuit sufficiency and necessity targets with fewer active read/write edges and selected units across tasks on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3.5-27B. We further show that SWD remains effective for full-model replacement of all attention and MLP weight matrices after fine-tuning the nonzero factor values. Finally, SWD also features a zero-data variant, allowing broader use of mechanistic interpretability analysis (e.g., per-step analysis).

</details>


### [128] [PRISM: Powerful Time Series to Image (TS2I) Representations for Multivariate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03926)
*Mateusz Smendowski,Kamil Faber,Piotr Nawrocki,Nathalie Japkowicz,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一个即插即用的元工作流，用于系统地构建和评估基于图像表示的多变量时间序列异常检测。通过超过7000个实验证明，设计良好的PRISM配置可匹敌24个时间域基线模型，并在14个数据集的10个上取得最佳VUS-PR，平均提升41%。文章还揭示了通道化（channelization）这一关键设计维度，并提出新的统计方案MSM。此外，ImageNet预训练编码器可有效迁移至TSAD任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测（TSAD）在不同表示选择下性能差异显著，尤其在高维多变量场景中。虽然将时间序列转换为图像在预测和分类任务中已展现优势，但在TSAD中，如何将多变量高维序列映射为多通道图像以及视觉骨干网络是否能匹敌时间域基线模型仍是未解问题。

Method: 提出PRISM (Plug-and-play Representation for Image-based TSAD Meta-workflow)，一个系统化的元工作流，用于构建和评估图像表示的多变量TSAD方案。核心贡献包括：(1) PRISM工作流框架；(2) 识别并研究"通道化"这一关键设计维度（多通道图像通道维度的构建方式）；(3) 提出MSM（基于统计的方案），用于通道构建；(4) 评估ImageNet预训练编码器在TSAD上的迁移效果（含冻结与微调两种策略）。

Result: 在超过7000个实验的评估中，设计良好的PRISM配置与24个时间域基线竞争，在14个数据集中的10个上取得最佳VUS-PR，平均改进41%。MSM方案比基于PCA的替代方案提升11-27%。ImageNet预训练编码器有效迁移，冻结编码器保持92%的微调性能，同时训练速度提升1.8倍。

Conclusion: 基于图像的表示方法在多变量TSAD中可达到与时间域基线相当甚至更优的性能。通道化是影响性能的关键且此前被忽视的设计维度。预训练视觉编码器（如ImageNet）具备良好的迁移能力，尤其冻结编码器在性能与效率之间提供了良好平衡。代码已开源。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins applications in predictive maintenance, finance, and cloud computing, however performance remains sensitive to representation choices, especially in multivariate settings. While transforming time series into images has shown success in forecasting and classification, it remains unclear how multivariate, high-dimensional series should be mapped to multi-channel images and whether vision backbones can match time-domain baselines in TSAD. We introduce PRISM, a plug-and-play meta-workflow enabling systematic construction and evaluation of image-based representations for multivariate TSAD. Our evaluation spanning over 7,000 experiments shows that well-designed PRISM configurations are competitive with 24 time-domain baselines, achieving the best VUS-PR on 10 of 14 datasets, with an average improvement of 41% over the best competing method on those datasets. Further, we identify channelization - how the channel dimension of multi-channel images is constructed - as a critical and previously understudied design dimension, and introduce MSM, a novel statistics-based scheme achieving 11-27% gains over PCA-based alternatives. Finally, ImageNet-pretrained encoders transfer effectively to TSAD, with frozen encoders retaining 92% of fine-tuned performance while training 1.8 times faster. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Smendowski/PRISM.

</details>


### [129] [A Physics-Flavored Transformer Network for Parametrizing Contraction Dynamics of Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03927)
*Mattias Luber,Timo Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理风味神经网络（PFNN），将拉伸指数物理模型集成到CNN-Transformer架构中，用于从力-时间曲线中自动提取骨骼肌组织的动力学表型参数。通过混合训练范式（合成数据预训练+真实数据无监督自对齐），该模型能够在高通量场景下实现多种收缩表型（包括杜氏肌营养不良模型）的高保真参数化，弥合了理想生物物理学与嘈杂体外数据之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化骨骼肌组织（ESMs）的功能表征通常依赖峰值力等简单指标，丢弃了关键的动力学信息。虽然机理模型能捕捉这些动力学特性，但其数学复杂性阻碍了可扩展应用和广泛采用。因此需要一种既能提取物理意义参数又具备可扩展性的自动化方法。

Method: 提出物理风味神经网络（PFNN），将拉伸指数型物理模型集成到CNN-Transformer架构中，直接从力-时间曲线提取物理意义参数。采用混合训练范式：先在合成数据上发展"物理直觉"，再通过无监督自对齐在未标注的真实数据上进行微调，以应对标注生物数据稀缺的问题。

Result: 实验表明，该物理风味方法能在多种不同收缩表型和细胞系（包括杜氏肌营养不良模型）上实现高保真参数化，证明了其可扩展性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该可扩展、自改进的流水线弥合了理想生物物理模型与嘈杂体外数据之间的差距，为高通量生物物理研究提供了鲁棒工具，有望推动ESM功能表征从简单指标向动力学层面全面自动化方向发展。

Abstract: Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues (ESMs) have become a key structure for biomedical disease modeling and pharmacological screening, yet their functional characterization often relies on simplistic metrics like peak force, discarding critical kinetic information. This is partially due to the high level of mathematical complexity which mechanistic models introduce to capture these dynamics. Hence, exactly the complexity prevents scalable application and widespread adaptation in the field. Here we present a Physics-Flavored Neural Network (PFNN) that automates the kinetic phenotyping of ESMs. Our architecture integrates a stretched-exponential physical model into a CNN-Transformer, enabling the extraction of physically meaningful parameters directly from force-time profiles. To address the scarcity of labeled biological data, we employ a hybrid training paradigm: the model develops a "physical intuition" on synthetic data before undergoing unsupervised self-alignment on unlabeled real-world measurements. Our results demonstrate that this physics-flavored approach achieves high-fidelity parameterization across diverse contractile phenotypes and cell lines, including Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy models. Our scalable, self-improving pipeline bridges the gap between idealized biophysics and noisy \emph{in vitro} data, providing a robust tool for high-throughput biophysical research.

</details>


### [130] [Latent Reward Registers for Diffusion Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03929)
*Yuanshen Guan,Zipeng Feng,Zhiwei Xiong,Peiqin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Reward Registers机制，通过在冻结的DiT中预置可学习的无位置寄存器token，直接从中间噪声潜变量估计最终奖励，提供贯穿整个去噪过程的密集可微奖励信号，并支持两种对齐策略：RG-OPD（训练）和RGS（推理），在效率与性能上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与人类偏好对齐通常依赖在最终生成样本上评估的稀疏终端奖励，在多步去噪过程中存在严重的时间信用分配挑战——中间步骤无法获得有效反馈，导致对齐效率低下。

Method: 在冻结的Diffusion Transformer输入序列中预置可学习的、无位置的寄存器token作为独立读出机制，直接从中间噪声潜变量估计终端偏好；基于此设计两种策略：训练阶段采用RG-OPD沿在线策略轨迹蒸馏奖励引导更新，避免标准策略梯度的昂贵rollout；推理阶段采用RGS通过幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导采样轨迹，无需参数更新。

Result: 在高噪声水平下寄存器达到评估的潜奖励模型中最高的配对准确率；RG-OPD在超越在线强化学习基线的同时将GPU小时数降低最多33倍；RGS在免训练方法中创下新SOTA，同时提升对齐与感知指标。

Conclusion: Latent Reward Registers提供了一种高效、无需修改生成器参数的密集奖励估计方案，在训练和推理两个层面都能显著提升扩散模型的对齐效果与计算效率。

Abstract: Aligning diffusion models with human preferences usually relies on a sparse terminal reward evaluated on the final generated samples, presenting a severe temporal credit-assignment challenge across the multi-step denoising process. We propose Latent Reward Registers, a mechanism that estimates terminal preference directly from intermediate noisy latents by prepending learnable, position-free register tokens to the input sequence of a frozen Diffusion Transformer (DiT). This independent readout mechanism extracts latent reward evidence without altering the generator's hidden states or velocity field. The resulting dense, differentiable reward signal throughout the full denoising process facilitates two alignment strategies. For training, Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD) distills reward-guided updates along on-policy trajectories, bypassing the computationally expensive rollouts of standard policy gradients. For inference, Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS) steers trajectories via magnitude-matched reward gradients without parameter updates. Empirically, at high noise levels (u = 0.8), the registers reach the highest pairwise accuracy among the evaluated latent reward models. Furthermore, RG-OPD outperforms online reinforcement learning baselines while reducing GPU hours by up to 33x, and RGS establishes a new state-of-the-art among training-free methods, strictly enhancing both alignment and perceptual metrics. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Guanys-dar/latent-reward-register

</details>


### [131] [Muon Meets Mamba: Spectral Optimization for State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03941)
*Arslan Battalov,Karim Kramin,Alexander Markotenko,Sofia Sinitsina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控实验比较Muon与AdamW优化器在Mamba-2 130M状态空间模型上的表现，发现Muon的收益具有局部性：仅对输出投影使用Muon优于对输入投影或两者同时使用，且优势主要体现在token效率上，与条件数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在Transformer模型上表现出色，但其在状态空间模型（如Mamba）上的行为尚未被充分研究。作者希望通过受控实验填补这一空白，探究Muon在非Transformer架构中的适用性以及其收益的具体来源。

Method: 在Mamba-2 130M模型上，采用严格受控的实验协议，仅改变使用Muon训练的权重组（输出投影、输入投影、两者同时），与AdamW进行对比。在两种语料库和两种token预算下进行训练，并从token效率、训练长度和条件数等多个维度分析收益来源。

Result: 发现Muon的收益是局部化的：仅在输出投影上使用Muon效果最好，优于在输入投影或同时在两者上使用。该优势主要体现在token效率上，在两种语料和两种token预算下均成立，且在超过计算最优训练点后依然持续。Muon虽能降低其训练投影的条件数，但条件数并不能解释性能提升——条件数更低的输入投影反而没有带来帮助。

Conclusion: Muon在状态空间模型上的收益不来自一般的条件数改善，而是与特定权重组（输出投影）相关，具有局部性和token效率优势。该发现对Muon在非Transformer架构中的适用性以及其工作机制提供了新的理解。

Abstract: Muon is a recent optimizer that orthogonalizes the update to each weight matrix with a Newton-Schulz iteration, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm. Almost all the evidence for it comes from Transformer models, and its behavior on state-space models is largely unreported. We compare Muon with AdamW on Mamba-2 130M under a controlled protocol that varies only which weight groups are trained with Muon. The benefit is localized. Muon on the output projection alone beats Muon on the input projection or on both. The advantage is mainly one of token efficiency. It holds on two corpora and two token budgets, and persists when training continues well past the compute-optimal point. Conditioning does not explain the gain. Muon lowers the condition number of whichever projection it trains, but the better-conditioned input projection is not the one that helps.

</details>


### [132] [Assessment of Conditional Diffusion Model for Synthetic Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03990)
*Seyed Kahaki,Shijie Li,Weijie Chen,Nicholas Petrick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对合成组织病理学图像质量评估的局限性，提出使用基于数字病理预训练基础模型的改进FID和IS指标，并辅以precision-recall指标。实验表明病理专用指标具有更好的判别力，改进的IS与下游分割性能相关性显著更高（r=0.6096 vs 0.0708），且数据多样性比视觉保真度对下游任务性能的影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成组织病理学图像生成可能解决计算病理学中数据稀缺的问题，但现有评估方法（如FID和IS）依赖ImageNet预训练的特征提取器，可能与病理图像域不匹配，无法充分评估合成数据的医学应用质量。因此需要探索更合适的评估指标。

Method: 1) 提出采用在数字病理数据集上预训练的基础模型来改进FID和IS指标；2) 补充precision-recall指标的额外质量评估；3) 使用条件去噪扩散模型及两步训练方法，在四个基准数据集上生成具有系统性变化质量特征的合成数据集；4) 测量合成数据质量指标与下游核分割性能（AJI+和Dice系数）之间的相关性。

Result: 病理特异性指标具有更好的判别力。改进的Inception Score与AJI+相关性更高（r=0.6096, p=0.0122），而原始IS相关性极低（r=0.0708, p=0.7944）。此外，增加生成训练数据的多样性比提高单张生成图像的视觉保真度与分割模型性能具有更高的正相关性。

Conclusion: 传统评估指标在组织病理学合成图像评估中存在局限，基于病理域预训练模型的改进指标能更有效地反映合成数据质量与下游任务性能的关系。同时，数据多样性对于下游模型性能的重要性超过单张图像的视觉保真度，这为合成数据生成策略提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation has emerged as an approach that may address data scarcity in computational pathology, yet current evaluation methodologies may not fully assess synthetic data quality for medical applications. This work investigates and addresses limitations in existing evaluation metrics, investigating an approach for assessing synthetic histopathology image quality through domain-specific metrics and downstream task validation. We show that conventional synthetic data evaluation metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) may have limitations when applied to histopathology images due to their reliance on ImageNet-pretrained feature extractors. To address these limitations, we propose for consideration modified FID and IS approaches utilizing foundation models pretrained on digital pathology datasets, supplemented by precision-recall based metrics as part of an additional quality assessment. Using conditional denoising diffusion models trained on four benchmark datasets, with a two-step training approach, we generated synthetic datasets with systematically varied quality characteristics. We also measured the correlation between the synthetic data quality metrics with downstream nuclei segmentation performance using common metrics including the aggregated Jaccard index (AJI+) and the Dice coefficient. The study results suggest that pathology-specific metrics may provide improved discriminative power. Specifically, the modified Inception Score indicates higher correlation with downstream task performance (r=0.6096 with AJI+, p=0.0122), compared to the original IS (r=0.0708, p=0.7944). Our observations indicate that increasing the variety of generated training data has a higher positive correlation with segmentation model performance than improving the visual fidelity of individual generated images.

</details>


### [133] [Test-Time Scaling in Reasoning LLMs: Inference Regimes, Evaluation, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04001)
*Mohsen Hariri,Weicong Chen,Nahal Shahini,Vikash Singh,Kai Ye,Amirhossein Samandar,Debargha Ganguly,Sreehari Sankar,Yanyan Zhang,Shouren Wang,Jerry Peng,Biyao Zhang,Michael Hinczewski,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地将大规模语言模型的"测试时扩展"（test-time scaling）形式化为带预算的推理过程，沿三条轴线展开：统计结构（单轨迹序列扩展、叶级扩展、前缀级扩展）、评估原则（端到端系统性能与候选库诊断分离，并引入评估profile）、以及可复现性要求（精确重放vs分布级可复现）。同时发布了超过20亿条完整推理轨迹的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"测试时扩展"这一术语涵盖了多种结构迥异的推理算法——单轨迹延展深思、采样后投票/验证聚合、对未完成部分状态进行搜索。这些算法在统计结构、计算核算和失败模式上均有本质差异。将不同算法混为一谈地当作单一标量"预算"来对待，或在不报告推理协议的情况下仅报告准确率，导致研究结果难以跨研究比较。作者希望建立一套系统的、可比较的测试时扩展框架。

Method: 作者从三个维度构建系统性框架：(1) 将测试时扩展形式化为自回归模型隐式前缀树上的预算推理，区分三种结构形态：单轨迹顺序扩展、叶级扩展（带终端归约）、前缀级扩展；(2) 将被评估对象视为完整的推理系统，提出分离端到端系统性能与候选库诊断的评估原则，引入评估profile，其坐标和简单函数可恢复或界定常见的重复采样指标，并要求协议匹配的计算与不确定性报告；(3) 明确推理协议的可复现性要求，区分精确重放与分布级可复现，识别各类型所需的工件。此外还按模型侧和接口机制组织了开源权重推理生态系统，在广泛知识、符号推理和竞赛数学基准上验证以上原则，并组装超过20亿条完整推理轨迹以供发布。

Result: 论文产出包括：一套区分三种结构形态的测试时扩展形式化框架；一个评估profile工具及其与常用重复采样指标间的数学关系；协议匹配的计算与不确定性报告规范；可复现性分类学（精确重放vs分布级可复现）；对开源权重推理生态系统的系统化组织；以及在多类基准上的应用验证和超过20亿条推理轨迹的数据集。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的各类算法不应被视作可互换的单一预算消耗过程，需要根据其统计结构、计算核算和失败模式加以区分。通过形式化框架、系统化评估原则和可复现性规范，可以为不同推理协议提供更公平、更可比的评估基础，从而推动该领域研究的科学化发展。

Abstract: Large language models can solve substantially harder reasoning problems with more inference-time compute. The term "test-time scaling," however, now covers diverse inference algorithms that extend deliberation along a single trajectory, sample completed candidates and aggregate them through voting or verification, or search over unfinished partial states. These algorithms differ in their statistical structure, compute accounting, and failure modes. Treating these procedures as interchangeable under a single scalar "budget," or reporting accuracy without the inference protocol that produced it, makes results difficult to compare across studies. We develop a systematic account of test-time scaling along three axes. First, we formalize test-time scaling as budgeted inference over the implicit prefix tree of an autoregressive model and distinguish three structural regimes: single-trajectory sequential scaling, leaf-level scaling with terminal reduction, and prefix-level scaling. Second, we treat the evaluated object as the entire inference system and develop evaluation principles that separate end-to-end system performance from candidate-bank diagnostics. We introduce an evaluation profile whose coordinates and simple functionals recover or bound common repeated-sampling metrics, and prescribe protocol-matched reporting of compute and uncertainty. Third, we specify reproducibility requirements for inference protocols, distinguishing exact replay from distributional reproducibility and identifying the artifacts needed to support each. We also organize the open-weight reasoning ecosystem by model-side and interface mechanisms, apply these principles to broad-knowledge, symbolic-reasoning, and competition-mathematics benchmarks, and assemble over 2 billion full reasoning traces for release with progressively richer verifier and token-level signals.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Particle trapping in vortex crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02779)
*Jean-Régis Angilella,S. Ravichandran*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维无粘和有粘涡旋晶体中惯性粒子的运动，探讨了粒子长期被困的条件。发现存在先前分析未发现的捕陷点，粒子轨迹可能趋近于可用渐近方法描述的极限环。在有粘性情况下，新发现的固定点会暂时存在；中等雷诺数下环形合并形成环形涡层可困住粒子，而高雷诺数下逐对合并导致新涡晶中粒子聚集于固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解惯性粒子在涡旋晶体（正多边形顶点排列的涡旋）中的长期捕获条件，是对此前分析的补充与深化，揭示粘性如何影响粒子捕陷行为。

Method: 通过分析无粘和有粘涡旋晶体中惯性粒子的运动方程，研究固定点的存在性，利用渐近方法描述粒子轨迹趋向的极限环，并考察粘性涡旋合并后的粒子动力学转变。

Result: 发现了先前分析中未识别的额外捕陷点；中等雷诺数下环形合并形成环形涡层可困住粒子于原点为中心的圆盘内；高雷诺数下逐对合并形成较小涡晶，粒子聚集于新涡晶的固定点。

Conclusion: 惯性粒子可在涡旋晶体中被长期捕陷，捕陷机制取决于涡晶结构（有无中心涡旋）及雷诺数（粘性程度），不同雷诺数区间对应不同的粒子聚集模式。

Abstract: We study the motion of inertial particles in two-dimensional inviscid and viscous vortex crystals, where vortices are placed at the tips of a regular polygon, and determine the conditions under which long-term trapping of particles can occur. In crystals with a central vortex, we find that trapping points in addition to those found in a previous analysis may exist finding, furthermore, that particle trajectories may approach a limit-cycle which can be described by means of asymptotic methods. We show that these newly discovered fixed points persist temporarily in the presence of viscosity, and study the different transitions following viscous vortex mergers. We find that for moderate Reynolds numbers, the annular merger of the vortex crystal form an annular vortex layer that can keep particles trapped within a disk centered at the origin. For larger Reynolds numbers, pairwise vortex merger leads to crystals with a smaller number of vortices, with particles clustering at the fixed points of the newly formed vortex crystal.

</details>


### [135] [Flame Dynamics of Air-Diluted Methanol spray Combustion in Confined Swirling Vitiated Hot Coflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03027)
*Zafar Alam,Bharat Bhatia,Ashoke De*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究利用旋流热共流改善甲醇喷雾的燃料-空气混合与火焰稳定性，采用欧拉-拉格朗日多相方法和改进的FGM燃烧模型，系统研究旋流数（0.2-3.0）对火焰稳定性和自着火行为的影响。结果显示提升角随旋流数增大先升高后降低（临界旋流数为1.0），高旋流数使火焰从尖锐柱状转变为均匀弥散的燃烧区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为设计低排放、旋流稳定的燃烧器，需要确保燃料-空气混合充分并维持火焰稳定。本研究聚焦于旋流热共流下甲醇喷雾的火焰动力学特性，以深入理解旋流强度对火焰稳定性和自着火的影响机理。

Method: 采用欧拉-拉格朗日多相模拟方法：连续气相由欧拉框架求解，离散液滴相由拉格朗日框架追踪；气相反应采用改进的火焰面生成流形（FGM）模型以实现准确且高效的燃烧模拟。选取旋流数SN=0.2、0.6、1.0、1.4、2.0和3.0六个工况，结合颗粒统计、火焰指数、本征正交分解（POD）及平均气相流场分布对火焰动力学进行综合分析。

Result: 随着旋流数从中低值上升，火焰提升角高度增加，但在达到临界旋流数（SN=1.0）后开始下降。高旋流数使时均火焰结构从尖锐柱状转变为均匀分布的燃烧区域，火焰更加紧凑且弥散。旋流强度对火焰形态、分布和动态特性均有显著影响。

Conclusion: 旋流数在火焰稳定和结构演化中起关键作用，临界旋流数（SN=1.0）是火焰提升角变化的转折点。高旋流数有利于形成更紧凑、分散均匀的火焰，对低排放旋流燃烧器的设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This study employs swirling hot coflow to ensure improved fuel-air mixture and stable flame, which are essential for designing low-emission, swirl-stabilized combustors. The present study introduces a swirling confined hot coflow around an air-diluted methanol spray. We have used the Eulerian-Lagrangian approach for multiphase simulation, resolving the dispersed liquid phase via the Lagrangian framework while addressing the continuous gas phase through the Eulerian framework. The Modified Flamelet Generated Manifold (FGM) model facilitates accurate and computationally efficient simulation of gas-phase reactions. The swirl numbers (SN), which are 0.2, 0.6, 1.0, 1.4, 2.0, and 3.0, are employed in this study to evaluate their impact on flame stability and auto-ignition. A higher lift-off height is observed as the swirl number rises from lower to moderate (SN = 0.2, 0.6, and 1.0). It decreases after the lift-off height reaches the critical swirl number (SN=1.0). These large swirl numbers also cause the time-averaged flame structure to change from a sharp columnar flame to an evenly spread combustion region. It also produces a more compact and widely dispersed flame for higher swirl numbers. Particle statistics, Flame Index, proper orthogonal decomposition (POD), and the mean gas-phase flow field distribution are used to study these impacts on flame dynamics in detail.

</details>


### [136] [Statistical Analysis of Droplet Size Distributions in Liquid-Jet-in-Crossflow Atomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03030)
*Tom Johny,Bharat Bhatia,Zafar Alam,Ashoke De*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过已验证的VOF-LPT耦合框架对横向气流中的液体射流（LJICF）进行数值模拟，系统分析了动量通量比、韦伯数、横流压力等参数对液滴尺寸分布和雾化特性的影响，发现更高的动量通量比和韦伯数能产生更细更均匀的液滴，横流压力虽促进细化但降低下游液滴密度，并对对数正态和Rosin-Rammler分布模型的适用性进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向气流中液体射流的雾化机理复杂，液滴尺寸分布直接影响燃烧效率和排放性能。现有研究对多种流动参数（动量通量比、韦伯数、横流压力等）对下游液滴尺寸分布和均匀性的综合影响缺乏系统性理解，需要精确的数值工具和统计方法深入量化这些关键参数的作用。

Method: 采用经过验证的可压缩VOF-LPT耦合框架进行数值模拟，同时捕捉初次雾化和二次雾化过程。通过计算Sauter平均直径（SMD）和标准差（STD）评估下游液滴尺寸特征，并利用概率密度函数和累积分布函数分析离散液滴尺寸分布，最后使用对数正态分布和Rosin-Rammler分布对液滴尺寸趋势进行建模对比。

Result: 增强的破碎条件下液滴分布向更细更均匀的方向转变；更高的动量通量比和韦伯数产生更细更均匀的液滴；增加横流压力促进细小液滴形成但因受限喷雾羽流、延迟粒子转化和减少滞留时间而降低下游液滴密度；对数正态分布能较好地捕捉偏斜度和尾部行为，Rosin-Rammler分布能准确表示中等和较大液滴，但两种模型分别对尖锐峰值和最小液滴尺寸的再现存在局限。

Conclusion: 气动力和不稳定性在高效雾化中起关键作用，动量通量比和韦伯数是决定液滴细化程度与均匀性的核心参数；横流压力对液滴细化与液滴密度存在权衡效应；对数正态和Rosin-Rammler分布作为近似模型各有优劣，需结合具体应用场景选择合适分布函数描述液滴尺寸谱。

Abstract: This study investigates the droplet size distribution (DSD) and atomization characteristics of a liquid jet injected into a crossflow (LJICF) under varied momentum flux ratios, Weber numbers, and flow conditions. Numerical simulations are performed using the validated compressible Volume of Fluid-Lagrangian Particle Tracking (VOF-LPT) coupled framework to capture both the primary and secondary atomization processes. Key parameters, including momentum flux ratio, Weber number, crossflow pressure, and velocity, were analyzed to assess their impact on droplet size characteristics, including Sauter mean diameter (SMD) and standard deviation (STD) in the downstream region. The discrete size distribution of droplets comprising probability density and cumulative distribution reveals a shift toward finer, more uniform droplets under enhanced breakup conditions. The findings emphasize the critical role of aerodynamic forces and instabilities in driving efficient atomization, with higher momentum flux ratios and Weber numbers leading to finer and more uniform droplets. Increased crossflow pressure promotes finer droplet formation but is found to reduce droplet density in the downstream domain due to a confined spray plume, delayed particle conversion, and reduced droplet residence time. The log-normal and Rosin-Rammler distributions effectively capture droplet size trends, with the former closely representing the skewness and tail behavior and the latter accurately representing intermediate and larger droplets. However, both have limitations in replicating sharp peaks and the smallest droplet sizes, respectively.

</details>


### [137] [Construction of flexible wing models by combined manufacturing of additive and subtractive processes for transonic wind tunnel testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03074)
*Natsuki Tsushima,Kensuke Soneda,Kenichi Saitoh,Kazuyuki Nakakita*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合金属增材制造与减材加工的系统性制造方法，用于构建跨声速风洞试验用柔性机翼模型，并通过多种测试验证了其几何精度、结构特性和气动弹性行为的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的柔性机翼模型制造依赖手工抛光等技术，精度受制于操作者技能且可重复性差，难以满足跨声速风洞试验对模型几何精度与结构一致性的严格要求。

Method: 采用金属增材制造与减材加工相结合的工艺路径制造两个柔性机翼模型，并通过三维扫描、静态载荷测试、地面振动测试、有限元分析及跨声速风洞试验进行系统评估。

Result: 制造模型精确再现了设计几何与结构特性，平均表面粗糙度低于1.0 μm，平均表面偏差低于0.3 mm；独立制造的模型颤振行为高度一致，实测颤振频率分别为157.0 Hz和158.0 Hz。

Conclusion: 组合增材与减材制造方法能够高效、可重复地生产柔性机翼模型，为可靠的气动弹性风洞实验提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: This study presents a systematic manufacturing approach that combines metal additive manufacturing and subtractive machining to construct flexible wing models for transonic wind tunnel testing. Two wing designs were fabricated and evaluated in terms of geometric accuracy, structural characteristics, and aeroelastic behavior. Three-dimensional scanning, static load tests, ground vibration tests, finite element analyses, and transonic wind tunnel tests were conducted. The fabricated models reproduced the designed geometries and structural properties with good accuracy. The average surface roughness was below 1.0 micrometer, and the average surface deviation was below 0.3 mm, improving geometric precision over previous skill-dependent polishing methods. Independently manufactured models also exhibited highly consistent flutter behavior, with measured flutter frequencies of 157.0 and 158.0 Hz. The results demonstrate that the combined additive and subtractive manufacturing approach enables efficient and reproducible production of flexible wing models for reliable aeroelastic wind tunnel experiments.

</details>


### [138] [Modelisation of chaotic systems with a latent Stochastic Differential Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03438)
*Ismaël Zighed,Nicolas Thome,Patrick Gallinari,Taraneh Sayadi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机微分方程（SDE）的概率非侵入式降阶模型（ROM），用于混沌动力系统建模。通过非线性自编码器将高维流场映射到低维潜空间，并在该空间内用SDE显式描述时间演化：漂移项捕捉可预测的动力学分量，扩散项吸收状态依赖的随机性。该方法生成的新混沌流轨迹在局部和全局上与真实转移核一致，即使轨迹与训练集完全不同，仍能保持底层统计量和流形结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SDE在科学机器学习中主要用作不确定性量化或分布匹配的算法工具，很少被真正用于建模宏观非线性物理系统的随机过程。将高维非线性动力学投影到低维流形会引入不可约简的不确定性，加上混沌吸引子和湍流固有的多可能未来，使得传统确定性降阶模型难以准确捕捉混沌动力学。因此需要用SDE在潜空间中显式建模混沌系统的演化。

Method: 采用非线性自编码器将流场编码为低维表示，在潜空间中用SDE显式控制时间演化。其中漂移项由学习得到，用于捕捉动力学的可预测分量；扩散项用于吸收状态依赖的随机性，从而统一建模确定性动力学与混沌固有的不可预测性。

Result: 该概率框架成功传播了高度非线性的状态，为混沌区域的确定性方法提供了稳健的替代方案。生成的混沌流轨迹在局部和全局上与DNS数据的真实转移核一致，同时生成的新轨迹与训练集不同但保持底层统计量和流形特性，验证了模型的强生成性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于SDE的概率降阶模型是一种有效的混沌动力系统建模方法，能够生成统计一致的新轨迹，展现出强大的生成能力和鲁棒性，为混沌流模拟提供了除传统确定性方法之外的新范式。

Abstract: Stochastic Differential Equations (SDEs) have become a cornerstone of scientific machine learning, though they are predominantly utilized as algorithmic tools for uncertainty quantification or distribution matching. In contrast, leveraging SDEs fundamentally to model macroscopic, nonlinear physics as stochastic processes remains largely unexplored. This work introduces a probabilistic, non-intrusive reduced-order model (ROM) for chaotic dynamical systems. We argue that projecting high-dimensional nonlinear dynamics onto a low-dimensional manifold introduces irreducible uncertainty, compounded by the chaotic attractors and multi-admissible futures inherent to turbulent flows. Consequently, a chaotic system governed by a partial differential equation can be effectively modeled by an SDE in a suitable latent space. To this end, a nonlinear autoencoder is employed to map the flow field into a low-dimensional representation, within which the temporal evolution is explicitly governed by an SDE. The predictable component of the dynamics is captured by a learned drift term, while state-dependent stochasticity is absorbed by a diffusion term. We demonstrate that this probabilistic framework successfully propagates highly nonlinear states, offering a robust alternative to traditional deterministic methodologies for chaotic regimes. Ultimately, our model generates new chaotic flow trajectories that remain locally and globally consistent with the true transition kernel learned from Direct Numerical Simulation (DNS) data. Even though these generated trajectories are unique and distinct from the training set, they preserve the underlying statistics and manifolds, validating the strong generative performance and robustness of our methodology.

</details>


### [139] [A windy sea surface with Stokes waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03657)
*Nikhil Yewale,Anil Kumar,Vinod Kadari,Ratul Dasgupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文通过求解驱动的耗散非线性势流方程，研究了风致重力-毛细表面波从光滑到波纹状态的转变，将其映射到雷诺数-波能相空间中，识别出转变带，并通过亚谐波稳定性分析揭示了波纹在载波交替面上增强的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测风致重力-毛细表面波从光滑到波纹状态的转变是非线性波动力学中长期悬而未决的问题，理解这一转变对于界面图案形成和海洋遥感等应用具有重要意义。

Method: 求解驱动-耗散的非线性势流方程，将波长4-13 cm的波浪映射到雷诺数-波能相空间；识别波陡度随雷诺数非单调变化的转变带；采用亚谐波（不）稳定性分析研究最快增长模态的演化。

Result: 识别出一个将光滑与波纹波态分隔的转变带，其中波陡度对雷诺数呈现非单调依赖关系；亚谐波稳定性分析表明最快增长模态会在载波的交替面上增强波纹结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解风驱动重力-毛细波从光滑到波纹态的转变提供了新的相空间框架，所揭示的交替面波纹增强机制对界面图案形成和海洋遥感研究具有广泛启示意义。

Abstract: Predicting the transition of wind-forced, gravito-capillary surface waves from smooth to corrugated states remains a longstanding problem in nonlinear wave mechanics. Solving driven-dissipative, nonlinear potential flow equations we map these waves ($4-13$ cm) onto a Reynolds number -- wave energy phase-space. We identify a transition band separating smooth from corrugated wave states - the wave steepness exhibits a non-monotonic dependence on Reynolds number within this. Subharmonic (in)stability analysis reveals that the fastest-growing mode intensifies corrugations on alternate faces of the carrier wave, offering insights with broad implications for interfacial pattern-formation and oceanic remote-sensing.

</details>

### [140] [The Fractional Half Dirac Operator Over Sobolev Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02871)
*Dejenie A. Lakew*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界光滑域上半个量级Dirac算子及其幂的边值问题，证明其解属于半个量级Hilbert空间，该空间可正交分解为域积分解与边界积分解两部分之和，并引入不同符号区分Sobolev空间中子空间与函数的正交和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立分数阶Dirac算子边值问题解的分解理论，通过Hilbert空间的正交分解结构来揭示域内部分与边界部分对解的独立贡献，从而深化对分数阶Dirac算子边值问题解空间结构的理解。

Method: 针对有界光滑区域上的半个量级Dirac算子及其幂的边值问题，将解构造为广义函数u，属于半个量级Hilbert空间。该空间被证明是两个正交子空间的直和：一个由域内半分数Dirac算子值通过域积分产生，另一个由解的边界迹值通过边界积分产生。文中使用两种不同的符号分别表示子空间层面的正交和与正交子空间中函数层面的正交和。

Result: 证明了该边值问题的解u存在于半个量级Hilbert空间中，并可将该空间正交分解为域积分部分的解空间与边界积分部分的解空间之和，即解由域内分片算子作用结果与边界迹值积分结果两部分正交叠加而成。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了半个量级Dirac算子边值问题解的正交分解定理，揭示了域内数据和边界数据在半个量级Sobolev空间中对解的独立且互补的贡献机制，为分数阶Dirac算子边值问题的进一步理论分析提供了结构基础。

Abstract: Let a bounded and smooth domain in the n dimensional Euclidean space be given and consider a boundary value problem of the half order Dirac operator and its power, with f be the value of the half derivative of the solution in the domain and, g be the trace value of the solution over the boundary. We will show that the solution will be a generalized function u in the half order Hilbert space which is the orthogonal sum of the solution that emerged as a domain integral from the domain value of the fractional half Dirac operator and the one that is the boundary integrals that emerges from the boundary or trace value of the solution. We use two different symbols to designate orthogonal sums for subspaces and functions in orthogonal subspaces of the Sobolev space.

</details>


### [141] [Generic Recovery of Permittivity and Permeability in Anisotropic Maxwell Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02900)
*Antonio Cocan,Maarten V. de Hoop,Joonas Ilmavirta,Matti Lassas,Anthony Várilly-Alvarado*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性非手性电磁介质的逆问题，证明Fresnel曲面在已知各向同性磁导率时唯一确定介电张量，并在一般情形下利用仿射几何不变量理论证明Fresnel多项式映射为双有理，从而建立真实Fresnel曲面在规约变换意义下对(ε,μ)的通用唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电磁波传播的Fresnel曲面（Maxwell方程组特征簇）恢复均匀各向异性介质的本构张量是经典逆问题。已有工作多限于各向同性或特定对称情形，一般各向异性情形下唯一性尚不明确，且存在自然规约对称性导致的非唯一性，需要严格的数学框架来刻画这一自由度。

Method: 首先对已知μ=I的情形直接证明唯一性及Fresnel多项式可约性与ε特征值重数之间的关系；对一般正定对称(ε,μ)，分析Fresnel多项式的不可约性，识别其不变的规约对称性，然后运用代数几何中的仿射几何不变量理论（GIT）构造参数空间在该规约作用下的仿射商，并证明Fresnel多项式映射由此商到其像诱导的映射是双有理的。

Result: （1）μ=I时，Fresnel曲面唯一确定ε，且多项式可约当且仅当ε有重特征值；（2）一般情形下Fresnel多项式在复数域上通常是不可约的；（3）通过GIT构造仿射商并证明Fresnel映射双有理，从而除一个真实代数例外集外，真实Fresnel曲面在规约等价意义下唯一确定(ε,μ)。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了各向异性电磁介质逆问题的通用唯一性，并将仿射几何不变量理论首次应用于PDE逆问题中的规约自由表述，为代数几何方法在逆散射与介质参数反演中的运用提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study the inverse problem of recovering the constitutive tensors of a homogeneous anisotropic electromagnetic medium without magnetoelectric coupling (non-chiral) from its Fresnel surface, the characteristic variety of Maxwell's equations governing electromagnetic wave propagation. For known isotropic permeability, normalized to $μ= I$, we prove that the Fresnel surface uniquely determines the permittivity tensor $\varepsilon$, and that the associated Fresnel polynomial is reducible precisely when $\varepsilon$ has a repeated eigenvalue. For general, positive-definite symmetric tensors $(\varepsilon,μ)$, we prove that the Fresnel polynomial is generically irreducible over $\mathbb{C}$ and we identify the natural gauge symmetry under which it is invariant. Using geometric invariant theory, a powerful tool of modern algebraic geometry, we construct an affine quotient of the parameter space by this gauge action and prove that the induced Fresnel-polynomial map is birational onto its image. We deduce that, outside a proper real algebraic exceptional set, the real Fresnel surface determines $(\varepsilon,μ)$ up to gauge. This establishes generic uniqueness for the inverse problem and, to our knowledge, provides a new application of affine geometric invariant theory to gauge freedom in a PDE inverse problem.

</details>


### [142] [Stability of Electrical Impedance Tomography with Anisotropies and its Application to the Deep Caldeón Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03053)
*Tianhao Hu,Bangti Jin,Yiran Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了电阻抗断层成像（EIT）中各向异性电导率在共形类中的新条件Lipschitz稳定性结果（涵盖二维和多维情形），并将该稳定性理论用于解释深度Calderón方法的鲁棒性，通过数值实验支持理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度Calderón方法作为一种基于深度学习的EIT图像重建技术，虽已展现出良好的经验效果，但缺乏理论基础；同时EIT中各向异性电导率重建的稳定性问题尚待系统研究。

Method: 首先建立各向异性EIT在共形类中恢复电导率的新条件Lipschitz稳定性结果（覆盖二维与多维情形）；然后利用该稳定性理论分析深度Calderón方法的鲁棒性，重点关注训练数据的合理选择；最后在二维情形下进行数值实验以验证理论分析。

Result: 给出了各向异性EIT在共形类中的条件Lipschitz稳定性理论，并将该理论与深度Calderón方法的鲁棒性建立联系，提供了二维数值实验结果作为理论补充。

Conclusion: 所建立的稳定性理论为深度Calderón方法提供了理论基础，阐明了训练数据选择与算法鲁棒性之间的关系，二维数值结果验证了理论分析的正确性。

Abstract: In this work, we establish new conditional Lipschitz stability results for electrical impedance tomography (EIT) with anisotropies, of recovering the conductivity in a conformal class of a known anisotropic conductivity in both two- and multi-dimensional cases. Then we employ the stability theory to understand the property of the deep Calderón method, one deep learning-based technique for image reconstruction in EIT that has shown promising empirical results, but still lacks theoretical underpinnings. Specifically, we relate the stability theory to the robustness of the method with the proper choice of the training data, and present numerical results in two-dimension to complement the theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [143] [A note on relativistic kinetic Schauder estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03126)
*Weinan Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了相对论输运（Lorentzian传输）线性动能方程中仅依赖动量方向的Schauder估计的反例，证明不能将完整的Lorentzian Hölder控制替换为仅依赖动量的Hölder控制。关键在于利用非线性动量-速度映射与固定光滑扩散/漂移系数之间的结构兼容性，通过共轭变换将扩散项变为Laplacian并消去所有一阶项，从而对单一固定算子实现阻碍，而非通过频率依赖的系数族。进一步，在零外力情况下该失败依然存在，通过构造一致正稳态解，其隐藏的空间振荡编码在有界零阶系数中，且该系数的动量Hölder半范趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于文献[DW26]中的启发，旨在探究线性动能方程（相对论输运）中Schauder估计的充分条件。具体而言，作者希望验证是否可以将[ HSTT ]中使用的完整Lorentzian Hölder正则性条件简化为仅依赖于动量方向的Hölder条件。构建反例以否定这一可能性，从而揭示该问题中正则性条件的本质边界。

Method: 作者利用动量-速度映射（非线性映射）与一对固定的光滑扩散和漂移系数之间的结构兼容性构造反例。关键技术步骤是通过共轭变换将扩散算子转化为Laplacian，同时使所有诱导的一阶项精确抵消。这样，阻碍现象由单一固定算子实现（该算子在每个有界动量集上一致椭圆），而非通过随频率变化的系数族。此外，通过构造均匀正的稳态解（零外部强迫），文中将空间振荡编码在有界零阶系数中，并证明该系数的动量Hölder半范趋于零。

Result: 成功构造了动量型Schauder估计失败的显式反例：即使算子在每个有界动量集上一致椭圆，仅依赖动量的Hölder系数控制也不足以保证空间方向的Schauder正则性。在零外力条件下，该失败依然成立——存在均匀正稳态解，其空间振荡由有界零阶系数承载，而该系数的动量Hölder半范趋于零。由此证明了[ HSTT ]中完整的Lorentzian Hölder控制在一般情况下不可被动量型Hölder控制替代。

Conclusion: 本文否定了动量型Hölder估计在相对论输运动能方程中的一般有效性，明确了[ HSTT ]中完整Lorentzian Hölder条件的必要性。该结果清晰地划定了动能方程Schauder理论中可简化条件的边界，为后续研究提供了重要的反例参考。

Abstract: In this note, motivated by \cite{DW26}, we construct counterexamples to momentum-only Schauder estimates for linear kinetic equations with relativistic transport. The construction exploits a structural compatibility between the nonlinear momentum-to-velocity map and a single fixed pair of smooth diffusion and drift coefficients: after conjugation, the diffusion becomes the Laplacian and all induced first-order terms cancel. Thus the obstruction is realized for one fixed operator, uniformly elliptic on every bounded momentum set, rather than through a frequency-dependent family of coefficients. We further show that the failure persists with zero external forcing by constructing uniformly positive stationary solutions for which the hidden spatial oscillation is encoded in a bounded zeroth-order coefficient whose momentum Hölder seminorm tends to zero. Consequently, the full Lorentzian Hölder control used in \cite{HSTT} cannot in general be replaced by momentum-only Hölder control.

</details>


### [144] [Strichartz estimates for quasi-periodic functions on long time intervals: An arithmetic approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03194)
*Kotaro Inami*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维薛定谔方程具有拟周期初值数据的长时Strichartz估计。对于具有代数频率比的双频数据，观察到线性薛定谔演化的行为依赖于该比的代数次数，并基于此改进长时区间上的Strichartz估计，同时证明了端点的L^4 Strichartz估计。证明使用了Roth型丢番图不等式和Parsell-Vinogradov系统的Vinogradov型均值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟周期初值下的薛定谔方程具有重要的物理和数学意义，长时Strichartz估计对于理解色散方程长时间的演化行为至关重要。作者注意到频率比的代数次数会影响线性演化的行为，这一观察为改进已有的Strichartz估计提供了新的思路。

Method: 1) 将拟周期初值数据的结构分解为多频形式；2) 利用Roth型丢番图不等式处理代数频率比的丢番图逼近性质；3) 运用Parsell-Vinogradov系统和Vinogradov型均值估计来处理高维指数和；4) 结合上述工具对长时区间上的Strichartz估计进行逐频段分析。

Result: 1) 基于频率比代数次数对演化行为影响的观察，改进了长时区间上的Strichartz估计；2) 证明了端点的L^4 Strichartz估计。

Conclusion: 频率比的代数结构显著影响拟周期初值下薛定谔方程的长时色散行为，Roth型丢番图不等式与Vinogradov型均值估计是处理此类问题的有效工具，所得估计在长时区间上优于以往结果。

Abstract: We study long-time Strichartz estimates for the one-dimensional Schrödinger equation with quasi-periodic initial data. For two-frequency data with an algebraic frequency ratio, we observe that the behavior of the linear Schrödinger evolution changes depending on the algebraic degree of the ratio. Making use of this observation, we improve the Strichartz estimates on long time intervals. We also prove an endpoint $L^4$ Strichartz estimate. Our proofs use Roth-type Diophantine inequalities and Vinogradov-type mean value estimates for the Parsell--Vinogradov systems.

</details>


### [145] [Concentration, Local Uniqueness, and Morse Index of Multi-bubble Solutions for a Critical Exponential Biharmonic Choquard Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03321)
*Wenjing Chen,Shengbing Deng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究四维有界域上具非局部指数非线性项的Navier双调和方程，通过约化变分法证明C^1-稳定临界点可构造m泡正解，并给出解的局部唯一性、线性化算子非退化性及莫尔斯指数的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维情形下，带Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev非局部项的指数型双调和方程在临界指数约束下具有变分结构，但集中解的构造与唯一性尚不清楚，特别是在尺度参数趋于零的临界四维情形下，约化Hessian的尺度方向的退化性是关键难点。

Method: 采用有限维约化方法：首先利用Navier Green函数及其正则部分构造微扰的泡型近似解，将问题约化到由每个泡的集中点和尺度组成的有限维参数空间；然后证明约化泛函为F_m（含对数K、正则部分H及格林函数G的双线性项）的微扰，从而将临界点稳定性与解的存在性关联；最后通过精确分析约化Hessian（含尺度方向的8π²b_α²|log ε|⁻¹量级正定块）和线性化算子非退化性，给出唯一性与指标公式。

Result: C^1-稳定的F_m临界点产生正m泡解，尺度为ε⁻¹量级，非线性源集中收敛到M_αδ_{ξ_i^*}；非退化临界点给出局部唯一性；线性化算子在H²∩H₀¹上非退化，且指标ind(u_ε)=m+ind(-D²F_m(ξ^*))；约化Hessian的尺度块为正，等于8π²b_α²|log ε|⁻¹I_m+o(|log ε|⁻¹)。

Conclusion: 该文完整建立了四维非局部Navier问题m泡解的变分结构：稳定临界点对应解的存在性，非退化性对应唯一性，并给出了Morsec指标与其约化有限维泛函指标之间的精确关系，说明四维临界容量控制尺度方向且尺度块正定，结果本质上推广了经典指数型双调和方程的相关理论。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^4$ be a bounded domain of class $C^6$, let $0<α<4$, and let $K\in C^4(\overlineΩ)$ be positive. We study the Navier problem \[ Δ^2u=\varepsilon^{8-α}K(x)e^{u(x)} \left(\int_Ω\frac{K(y)e^{u(y)}}{|x-y|^α}\,dy\right), \qquad u=Δu=0\quad\text{on }\partialΩ. \] Let $G$ be the Navier Green function, let $H$ be its regular part, and put $M_α=8π^2(8-α)$. The concentration points are governed by \[ \mathcal F_m(\boldsymbolξ) =\sum_{i=1}^m \left[\log K(ξ_i)+\frac{M_α}{2}H(ξ_i,ξ_i)\right] +M_α\sum_{i<j}G(ξ_i,ξ_j). \] Every $C^1$-stable critical point of $\mathcal F_m$ produces a positive $m$-bubble solution whose scales are of order $\varepsilon^{-1}$ and whose nonlinear source converges to $M_α\sum_iδ_{ξ_i^*}$. If the critical point is nondegenerate, the corresponding $m$-bubble solution is locally unique, modulo permutations, in a fixed scaled modulation neighborhood. The linearized operator is nondegenerate on $H^2(Ω)\cap H_0^1(Ω)$, and \[ \operatorname{ind}(u_\varepsilon) =m+\operatorname{ind}\!\left(-D^2\mathcal F_m(\boldsymbolξ^*)\right). \] A critical four-dimensional capacity controls the scale directions. The dilation block of the reduced Hessian is positive and equals $8π^2b_α^2|\log\varepsilon|^{-1}I_m+o(|\log\varepsilon|^{-1})$, where $b_α=(8-α)/2$.

</details>


### [146] [Stable blowup for the harmonic map heat flow into perturbed spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03332)
*Alexander Wittenstein*

Main category: math.AP

Abstract: We prove stable self-similar blowup for the harmonic map heat flow in dimensions 3 to 6 for target manifolds which are slightly perturbed versions of the round sphere. Method: 在分布（distribution）意义下表述时变麦克斯韦系统，对介电常数和磁导率施加显式的迹和正则性假设；推导时间不连续与空间界面处的跳变条件；利用这些条件推导时间分裂、反射和透射产生的波矢量广义Snell定律；将振幅方程组织为有限维线性系统，并给出斜入射的具体示例。

Result: 成功推导了时间与空间界面共存时的跳变条件，建立了广义Snell定律和振幅线性系统，并通过斜入射实例进行了验证。该方法不依赖光滑场假设，且允许材料参数在远离界面处不必保持恒定。

Abstract: We study the time-dependent Maxwell system, formulated in the sense of distributions, for electromagnetic waves propagating through media with temporal and spatial material interfaces. Under explicit trace and regularity assumptions on the permittivity and permeability, we derive the jump conditions produced by temporal discontinuities and by subsequent spatial interfaces. These conditions are used to obtain generalized Snell laws for the wave vectors generated by temporal splitting, reflection, and transmission. We also derive the associated amplitude equations and organize them as a finite-dimensional linear system for a space-time slab geometry. Finally, we provide an explicit oblique-incidence example. Our approach does not impose a smooth-field ansatz and allows material parameters that need not be constant away from the interfaces.

</details>


### [149] [Remarks on the antimaximum principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03460)
*Vladimir Bobkov*
