TL;DR: 本文围绕p-Laplacian模型问题中的反极大值原理（AMP）进行了系统性研究，包括AMP的均匀性、端点λ_f的下半连续性、端点界限、扩展AMP（负解不存在性）、反比较原理以及将源函数正则性从Lebesgue空间放宽到Morrey空间等若干方面，并提出了相关开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反极大值原理（AMP）是偏微分方程理论中的基本性质，但对于p-Laplacian问题（p≠2时）其适用性、均匀性、端点的连续性质及源函数正则性要求等关键问题尚未被充分理解，作者旨在对这些方面进行系统性的梳理与讨论。

Method: 本文采用分析论证与讨论相结合的方式，针对具体模型问题识别使AMP均匀成立的一类源函数，通过变分方法和比较原理等技术手段分析λ_f的映射性质（如下半连续性）及上下界，同时探讨了扩展AMP的适用范围、反比较原理的成立条件，并将源函数的正则性要求从Lebesgue空间扩展至Morrey空间进行研究。

Result: 作者确定了使AMP均匀成立的一类源函数，证明了f↦λ_f映射的下半连续性，给出了λ_f的界限估计，确认了扩展AMP（大λ下负解不存在）的成立情况，研究了反比较原理，并成功将结果推广到Morrey空间中的源函数。部分结果（如涉及更精细正则性的结论）仅在p=2的线性情形下给出。

Conclusion: 本文对p-Laplacian Dirichlet问题的反极大值原理进行了多维度的深入分析，丰富了AMP的理论框架，同时通过列出的开放问题为该领域后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We present several observations on the antimaximum principle (AMP) for the model problem $-Δ_p u = λ|u|^{p-2} u + f$ in a bounded smooth domain $Ω$, subject to the zero Dirichlet boundary conditions, and where the source function $f$ is nontrivial, nonnegative, and sufficiently regular. Denote by $λ_f$ the endpoint of validity of the AMP, so that every solution of the problem is negative in $Ω$ for any $λ\in (λ_1,λ_f)$. Our discussion covers the following aspects: identification of a class of sources over which the AMP is uniform, lower semicontinuity of the map $f \mapsto λ_f$, bounds on $λ_f$, the nonexistence of negative solutions for sufficiently large $λ$ (extended AMP), the anticomparison principle, and the weakening of the source regularity from the Lebesgue to Morrey spaces. Some of the results are stated only in the linear case $p=2$. As a part of the discussion, we provide a few related open problems.

</details>

Method: 通过构造齐次（homogeneous）的秩一凸函数，利用高维度m的几何特性，证明这些函数在定义域内任何点都不满足拟凸性条件，从而得到反例。

Result: 当m充分大时，成功构造出齐次且处处秩一凸但处处非拟凸的被积函数，证明了秩一凸不蕴含拟凸性在适当的高维情形下可以处处失效。

Conclusion: 该工作为秩一凸与拟凸的等价性问题提供了更强的高维反例，加深了对向量变分法中凸性层级结构的理解。

Abstract: We construct homogeneous rank-one convex integrands $F\colon \mathbb{R}^{2\times m} \to \mathbb R$ that are nowhere quasiconvex when $m$ is large.

TL;DR: 本文构造了二维不可压缩欧拉方程中新的均匀旋转涡斑解族，由单个外涡斑和多个内部界面组成，其中m≥2个高度集中的内部分量按正m边形顶点排列，整体顺时针刚性旋转，且当集中参数趋于零时内部件同时向原点坍缩，外边界收敛到单位圆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前没有解析构造过多个集中内部分量包含在共同外涡斑中并同时向中心坍缩的去奇异化机制，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于轮廓动力学公式，通过对称性约简得到两个非线性边界方程，采用合适的奇异重缩并利用带连续参数的隐函数定理在对称自适应Hölder空间中完成证明。

Result: 成功构造了满足上述几何结构的均匀旋转涡斑解族；涡度在测度意义下收敛到Rankine涡叠加中心处环量为-m的点涡。

Conclusion: 这是首次从解析上构造出多个集中内部分量同时向中心坍缩的去奇异化机制，为涡斑与点涡相互作用理论提供了新的构造性结果。

Abstract: We construct new families of uniformly rotating vortex-patch solutions of the two-dimensional incompressible Euler equations consisting of a simply connected outer patch and multiple interior interfaces, which can be interpreted geometrically as holes. More precisely, each solution consists of a single outer vortex patch enclosing $\mathbf m\geq2$ identical, highly concentrated inner components arranged at the vertices of a regular $\mathbf m$-gon; the entire configuration rotates rigidly in the clockwise direction.
  As the concentration parameter tends to zero, the inner components shrink and collapse simultaneously toward the origin, while the outer boundary converges to the unit circle. The corresponding vorticities converge, in the sense of measures, to a Rankine vortex supplemented by a point vortex of circulation $-\mathbf m$ at its center.
  The proof is based on a contour-dynamics formulation, a symmetry reduction to two nonlinear boundary equations, and a suitable singular rescaling. We then apply an implicit function theorem with a continuous parameter in symmetry-adapted Hölder spaces. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first analytical construction of a desingularization regime in which several concentrated inner components are contained in a common outer patch and collapse simultaneously toward its center.

</details>


### [152] [Brinkman's term for the steady-state Navier-Stokes equations with prescribed flux rate or pressure drop in perforated pipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03672)

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于理解在几何上存在多尺度扰动（微小颗粒）的管道中流体的宏观行为。通过均质化方法揭示微观结构对宏观有效方程的影响（即Brinkman项的涌现），并且在没有对小数据进行任何限制的前提下建立解的渐近形态，这是该领域的重要理论挑战。

Method: 采用两个关键方法：（1）适用均质化理论中的能量方法，分析Navier-Stokes方程组（带混合边界条件）在ε→0时的渐近行为；（2）在通量率给定时，通过基于稳态Euler方程Bernoulli定律的矛盾的论证来建立所需的一致估计，从而克服数据大小不受限制带来的困难。

Result: 当ε→0时，系统解的渐近行为被成功刻画，所得的宏观有效方程在原有Navier-Stokes（或Stokes）结构基础上额外增加了Brinkman项。在给出流量率的问题中，通过Bernoulli定律的矛盾的论证成功获得了所必需的均匀界，且不需要对数据大小施加任何限制。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在含有稀疏均匀分布小颗粒的扭曲管道中，流体的宏观运动方程需修正为带有Brinkman阻尼项的形式；同时表明即使数据任意大，在通量率给定的情况下仍可通过Euler方程Bernoulli定律的矛盾论证获得必要的先验估计，从而保证了均质化结果的严格性。

Abstract: The steady motion of a viscous incompressible fluid in a distorted pipe, containing several small particles of diameter $\eps^3$ and mutual distance $\eps$, is modeled through the Navier-Stokes equations with mixed boundary conditions. Apart from inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions on the particles, these involve the Bernoulli pressure and the tangential velocity on the inlet and outlet of the tube, while either the transversal flux rate or the pressure drop is prescribed along the pipe. Applying the energy method in homogenization theory, we study the asymptotic behavior of the solutions to these systems as $\eps \to 0$, without any restriction on the magnitude of the data, and show that the effective equations display an additional Brinkman term. An important feature of the present work concerns the required uniform bounds, which are achieved (in the case of the prescribed flux problem) by a contradiction argument based on Bernoulli's law for solutions of the stationary Euler equations.

</details>

Method: 采用Liouville变换将问题简化，将伴随方程的解通过从无穷远出发的Volterra积分方程构造为正精确解，绕过了线性传播子的显式表示。利用该伴随解推导能量解的爆破判据和寿命上界，并通过限制阻尼系数原函数至多对数增长来获得完整的移位Glassey临界指数范围。

Conclusion: 该工作为带时间依赖阻尼和质量的半线性波动方程建立了统一的爆破理论框架，不依赖于线性传播子的显式可解性，显著推广了已有结果的应用范围。

Abstract: We consider semilinear wave equations with time-dependent damping and mass and a derivative-type nonlinearity. By applying a Liouville transformation and solving a Volterra integral equation posed from infinity, we construct a positive exact solution to the adjoint equation without using an explicit representation of the linear propagator. This solution is used to derive a blow-up criterion for energy solutions with finite propagation, together with an upper bound for the lifespan. If the primitive of the damping coefficient grows at most logarithmically, we obtain the full shifted Glassey range, including the critical exponent, and the corresponding polynomial and exponential lifespan estimates. The result applies independently of the sign of the scale-invariant discriminant and therefore includes the mass-dominant regime. It also covers non-integrable oscillatory perturbations of the damping coefficient when their contributions are canceled by the corresponding mass terms.

</details>

Abstract: We consider polyconvex functionals defined on vector valued functions, the model case being the graph area functional, and we analyze the relaxed energy with respect to the strict convergence in $BV$. In several cases where the relaxed area is known, by exploiting a continuity result by Reshetnyak we are able to find an explicit formula for wide classes of integrands with linear growth in the minors of the gradient. We preliminarily extend to the $BV$ setting a continuity property observed by Acerbi--Dal Maso in the Sobolev case. Finally, partial results concerning the relaxation of the vortex map with respect to the $L^1$ convergence are obtained.

*Jie Jiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究全抛物Keller–Segel系统在有界光滑凸区域上的全局存在性与一致有界性，证明了对任意τ>0，μ>Nχ/4即可保证全局存在与一致有界，且该系数显式充分条件与τ无关、不涉及嵌入或极大正则性常数，是首个在任意空间维数下对所有τ>0均成立的系数显式有界性判据。证明基于新的辅助比较函数Y_τ，通过闭式标量抛物不等式、抛物压缩论证、Hölder–Sobolev插值与加权极大L^p正则性等技术完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Keller–Segel系统是经典的趋化模型，其解的全局存在性与有界性依赖于系数条件。已有结果通常依赖嵌入常数或极大正则性常数，且条件随τ变化，缺乏对所有τ>0统一、系数显式的有界性判据。本文旨在给出一个不依赖τ、不涉及隐式常数的充分条件。

Method: 构造新的辅助比较函数Y_τ=u+(χτ/2)|∇v|²-(τ-1)Δv，证明其对任意τ>0满足闭式标量抛物不等式。当τ≥1时直接获得u的点态比较与显式界；当0<τ<1时先获得v的一致上界，再通过z=e^{-χv/2}的抛物压缩论证得到v的一致Hölder界，继而用Hölder–Sobolev插值与加权极大L^p正则性获得u的L^p界（p充分大），最后由标准抛物光滑化完成论证。

Result: 对任意固定τ>0，条件μ>Nχ/4保证全抛物Keller–Segel系统在任意空间维数下有界光滑凸区域上的全局存在性和一致时间有界性。该条件是系数显式的（仅涉及N、χ、μ），独立于τ，且不依赖任何嵌入常数或极大正则性常数，是首个在所有τ>0下保持不变的系数显式有界性判据。

Conclusion: 本文建立了全抛物Keller–Segel系统在所有τ>0下统一适用的系数显式有界性判据μ>Nχ/4，填补了该领域的一个空白。核心创新在于引入与τ相关的辅助比较函数Y_τ，巧妙地将不同τ范围的情形统一到同一框架下处理，并成功规避了传统方法对嵌入常数的依赖。

Abstract: We study the fully parabolic Keller--Segel system
  \[
  u_t=Δu-χ\nabla\!\cdot(u\nabla v)+λu-μu^2,
  \qquad
  τv_t=Δv-v+u
  \]
  in a bounded smooth convex domain. For every fixed $τ>0$, we prove that
  \[
  μ>\frac{Nχ}{4}
  \] guarantees global existence and uniform-in-time boundedness. This coefficient-explicit sufficient condition is independent of $τ$ and involves no embedding or maximal-regularity constants. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first coefficient-explicit boundedness criterion that remains unchanged for all $τ>0$ in arbitrary space dimension. 
  The proof is built on a new auxiliary comparison function
  \[
  Y_τ
  =u+\frac{χτ}{2}|\nabla v|^2-(τ-1)Δv,
  \]
  which satisfies a closed scalar parabolic inequality for every $τ>0$. When $τ\ge1$, this inequality yields a direct pointwise comparison and an explicit bound for $u$. When $0<τ<1$, it instead provides a uniform upper bound for $v$. Applying a parabolic squeezing argument to the transform $z=e^{-χv/2}$ then yields a uniform Hölder bound for $v$. Hölder--Sobolev interpolation and weighted maximal $L^p$-regularity subsequently give an $L^p$-bound for $u$ with sufficiently large $p$, and standard parabolic smoothing closes the argument.

</details>


### [156] [Approximate traveling waves for the focusing NLS with an external potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03915)
*Luke Baker,Xuemei Li,Jason Murphy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对带快速衰减外势扰动的三维聚焦三次非线性薛定谔方程(NLS)，构造了近似行波解，并证明了在高于基态阈值的任意质量-能量下，存在前向全局、H^1有界且不散射的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带外势扰动的NLS方程中非线性束缚态的存在性及其动力学行为，特别是超越未扰动方程基态阈值后是否仍存在非散射的有界解，这关乎色散方程解的长期行为分类。

Method: 通过构造近似行波解的方法，利用快速衰减外势作为扰动，在三维聚焦三次NLS的框架下建立满足质量-能量高于未扰动基态阈值的非散射解。

Result: 证明了存在前向全局、H^1有界且不散射的解，其质量-能量可以任意高于未扰动方程的地态阈值。

Conclusion: 该结果表明外势扰动能够诱导超越未扰动基态阈值的新型束缚态动力学，扩展了对NLS非散射解存在区域的理解。

Abstract: We construct approximate traveling wave solutions for the $3d$ focusing cubic NLS perturbed by a rapidly decaying external potential. As an application, we demonstrate that there exists a forward-global, $H^1$ bounded, nonscattering solution at any mass-energy higher than the ground state threshold for the unperturbed equation.

</details>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Analysis of inverse stochastic resonance: Effects of neural excitability and timescale separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03454)
*Marius E. Yamakou,Torben Krüger,Hermann Schulz-Baldes*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 该论文对双稳FitzHugh-Nagumo神经元中由加性噪声诱导的逆随机共振（ISR）进行了系统分析，通过余维二分支识别出双稳区域，利用几何最小作用方法计算准势垒，并建立简化的亚稳态两态马尔可夫模型，提出了ISR的符号判据及最小噪声幅度的半定量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经兴奋性和时间尺度分离如何调控噪声诱导的放电活动调制，尤其是逆随机共振（ISR）现象——即噪声增加反而抑制放电的机制。此前的数值观测显示有限时间模拟中极限环盆地占据可能依赖初始条件，作者希望从理论上澄清这一依赖是否为渐近性质，并建立从内在神经参数到噪声诱导尖峰抑制的机制性联系。

Method: 采用余维二分支分析（codimension-two bifurcation analysis）识别双稳楔形区域；利用有限时间蒙特卡洛模拟观测极限环盆地占据；通过几何最小作用方法（gMAM）计算退化噪声下的准势垒；构造简化的亚稳态两态马尔可夫近似推导弱噪声下极限环盆地占据概率的解析公式，并据此给出真实ISR的符号判据和最小噪声幅度的半定量预测。

Result: 证明了随机系统存在唯一的不变概率测度，因此长时间放电统计不依赖于初始盆地（有限时间的初始依赖性仅是稀有跃迁未完全解析的非渐近现象）。准势差将双稳楔形划分成两个逃逸主导区域；在负有效指数区间，只有当极限环准势超过不动点准势时才出现真实的ISR最小值。所推导的弱噪声公式和符号判据与数值模拟吻合。

Conclusion: 论文建立了"逃逸平衡"机制，将内在神经元参数（兴奋性和时间尺度分离）与渐近噪声诱导尖峰抑制联系起来，为ISR的预测和调控提供了理论框架。这深化了对噪声在神经系统中非单调作用的理解，表明ISR源自极限环与不动点之间的准势竞争而非初始条件的残留效应。

Abstract: We analyze inverse stochastic resonance (ISR) in a bistable FitzHugh--Nagumo neuron driven by additive noise in the voltage variable, focusing on how neural excitability and timescale separation regulate the noise-induced modulation of spiking activity. A codimension-two bifurcation analysis identifies a narrow bistable region in which a stable fixed point and a stable limit cycle coexist, separated by an unstable periodic orbit. Finite-time Monte Carlo simulations show that the occupation of the limit-cycle basin may appear to depend on the initial basin when rare transitions are not fully resolved. We prove that this dependence is not asymptotic: the stochastic system admits a unique invariant probability measure, so long-time firing statistics are independent of the initial basin of attraction. The parameter dependence of ISR is characterized by quasi-potential barriers computed with a geometric minimum action method for the degenerate noise. The difference between the limit-cycle and fixed-point quasi-potentials partitions the bistable wedge into two escape-dominated regimes. A reduced metastable two-state Markov approximation yields a weak-noise formula for the limit-cycle basin occupation probability, a sign criterion for genuine ISR, and a semiquantitative prediction of the ISR-minimizing noise amplitude. In the fitted regime with a negative effective exponent, a genuine ISR minimum occurs only when the limit-cycle quasi-potential exceeds that of the fixed point. These results provide an escape-balance mechanism linking intrinsic neuronal parameters to asymptotic noise-induced spike suppression.

</details>


### [158] [Exact Low-Dimensional Reduction Theory for Populations of Stuart--Landau Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04000)
*Kai Tokunaga*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Stuart-Landau振荡器种群的精确低维约化理论，涵盖两类耦合情形，分别可精确约化为三维和七维系统，能够捕捉相振子模型无法描述的、振幅起关键作用的动力学行为（如聚类、混沌等复杂非平衡动力学）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低维约化理论主要针对相振子种群，无法描述振幅起关键作用的集体动力学。作者旨在发展一种精确的低维约化框架，以涵盖更广泛的集体动力学现象（如聚类），并捕捉无法由相振子种群或其约化模型描述的复杂行为。

Method: 针对Stuart-Landau振荡器种群，区分两种耦合情形：(1) 耦合仅进入Stuart-Landau方程的系数；(2) 耦合还进入这些系数之外的其他项。在适当假设下，分别将系统精确约化为三维和七维系统。所考虑的额外耦合项类别足够广泛，可涵盖聚类等丰富的集体动力学。

Result: 该约化方法成功捕捉了振幅起本质作用的动力学，这类动力学无法由相振子种群或其低维约化描述。此外，该框架被证明是捕捉复杂非平衡动力学（如混沌）的有力工具。

Conclusion: 该精确低维约化理论为Stuart-Landau振荡器种群提供了统一且通用的约化框架，弥补了现有相振子约化理论的不足，并为研究振幅相关集体动力学及混沌等复杂非平衡现象提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: This paper develops an exact low-dimensional reduction theory for populations of Stuart-Landau oscillators. The theory covers two cases: in one case, coupling enters only through the coefficients of the Stuart-Landau equations, and in the other, coupling also enters through terms outside those coefficients. Under suitable assumptions, the two cases can be reduced exactly to three and seven-dimensional systems, respectively. The class of additional coupling terms for which the reduction applies is sufficiently general to allow a broad range of collective dynamics, such as clustering. In particular, the present reduction is shown to capture dynamics in which amplitude plays an essential role and which cannot be described by populations of phase oscillators or their low-dimensional reductions. The proposed framework is also shown to be a powerful tool for capturing complex nonequilibrium dynamics such as chaos.

</details>
