### [159] [Universal crossovers in weakly-monitored quantum critical states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02716)
*Abhishek Kumar,Rushikesh A. Patil,Andreas W. W. Ludwig,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了1D量子Ising哈密顿量的临界和三临界基态在弱能量测量和弱自旋测量（无后选择）后的系综，通过有限尺寸重整化群交叉分析揭示了测量作为相关微扰的普适性质，发现临界Ising模型直接流向投影测量不动点（面积率纠缠），而三临界Ising模型则流向弱测量不动点（对数纠缠）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子测量对临界多体系统基态的影响是量子信息与凝聚态物理交叉领域的重要问题。临界系统对微扰高度敏感，测量作为一种非幺正扰动可能显著改变其普适性质。此前对于测量如何作为相关微扰影响临界和多重临界Ising基态的重整化群流结构尚不清楚。

Method: 采用有限尺寸重整化群（RG）交叉分析方法，分别对临界和三临界1D量子Ising哈密顿量的基态施加弱能量测量和弱自旋测量（无后选择），通过纠缠有效中心荷、有效Affleck-Ludwig边界熵以及测量平均关联函数矩的多分形签名来表征测量后系综的普适性质。

Result: 两种情况下均发现由Born规则随机性主导的"测量主导"或"测量改变"不动点。临界Ising模型从弱测量直接RG流向投影测量不动点，表现出面积率纠缠；三临界Ising模型则存在弱测量不动点，表现出对数纠缠。测量诱导的固有随机性可产生丰富而复杂的普适长距离标度行为。

Conclusion: 该研究澄清了弱测量多重临界Ising基态的重整化群流结构，表明测量诱导随机性本身可以生成复杂且丰富的普适长程标度行为，并通过受控解析RG和数值有限尺寸RG交叉分析得以验证。

Abstract: We study post-measurement ensembles of ground states of tricritical and critical 1D quantum Ising Hamiltonians subjected, respectively, to weak energy and spin measurements without post-selection. These measurements act as relevant perturbations about the unmeasured critical ground states. Using finite-size renormalization group (RG) crossover analyses, we characterize their universal properties through the entanglement effective central charge, effective Affleck-Ludwig boundary entropy, and signatures of multifractality from moments of measurement-averaged correlation functions. In both cases, we find evidence for "measurement-dominated" or "measurement-altered" fixed points governed by the underlying Born-rule randomness. For critical Ising, we find a direct RG flow to a projective-measurement fixed point with area-law entanglement, whereas for the tricritical Ising model, we find evidence for a weak-measurement fixed point with logarithmic entanglement. These results clarify the RG-flow structure of weakly measured multicritical Ising ground states and show how intrinsic measurement-induced randomness can generate complex and rich universal long-distance scaling behavior in the post-measurement ensembles, accessible to controlled analytical RG and numerical finite-size RG crossover analyses.

</details>


### [160] [Two dimensional inhomogeneous classical systems at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02903)
*Jean-Marie Stéphan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维经典晶格模型在缓慢非均匀变形下的临界行为，证明这类系统在大尺度上可由弯曲时空中的共形场论（CFT）描述，并解析识别出潜在的时空度量。以Ising模型和带畴壁边界条件的六顶点模型为例，通过流体力学方法求解自由情形，并在有相互作用时确定了分离临界涨落区与有序冻结相的"北极曲线"（arctic curve）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非均匀（不均匀）变形的晶格模型如何保持临界行为，并揭示其在弯曲空间中对应的CFT结构，从而将均匀体系中的共形不变性推广到非均匀情形，为研究受限几何与非均匀系统中的临界现象提供理论框架。

Method: 结合共形场论与流体力学方法：缓慢变化的形变保持临界性，从而在全局上等效于弯曲时空中的CFT；通过识别底层空间度量来建立对应关系。对自由情形的六顶点模型采用流体力学严格求解；对包含相互作用的体系，通过分析确定arctic curve以刻画临界涨落区与冻结相的边界。

Result: 发现非均匀变形的二维临界晶格模型可由弯曲时空中的CFT描述，并成功识别了对应的空间度量。在自由六顶点模型中通过流体力学获得精确解；对相互作用情形，推导出了arctic curve，精确界定了临界涨落区域与有序（冻结）相的几何边界。

Conclusion: 缓慢非均匀的变形虽然破坏了均匀性，但在保持临界行为的前提下，系统整体可由弯曲时空中的CFT刻画。这一框架为理解和计算非均匀临界现象（如arctic curve）提供了统一且可解析求解的途径，并适用于Ising模型与六顶点模型等经典体系。

Abstract: We study simple inhomogeneous deformations of known two-dimensional classical lattice models described by conformal field theory (CFT) at large distances. The deformations are chosen to vary slowly at lattice scales, while preserving critical behavior. Globally, we find that such systems are described by a CFT in curved space, and identify the underlying space metric. Our two examples are the Ising model and the six vertex model with domain wall boundary conditions. In the latter boundaries are also inhomogeneous, which complicates the analysis. We nevertheless solve the free case using hydrodynamics. In the presence of interactions we determine the arctic curve which separates a critical fluctuating region from an ordered (frozen) phase.

</details>


### [161] [Fractal deconfinement and confinement in Sierpinski ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02741)
*James Walkling,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究Sierpinski垫片（Sierpinski gasket）这一分形结构上的六顶点模型，通过递归计算配分函数和相关函数，揭示了包含多种不同行为的丰富相图。冰模型表现出熵性的电荷禁闭，而一个特定的四顶点极限则展现出分形退禁闭现象，连接退禁闭电荷的弦本身是一个统计分形，分形维数约为1.3。最后提出将其构造成人工自旋冰以实现实验研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六顶点模型在常规二维晶格上已被广泛研究，但在分形结构上的行为尚不清楚。由于维度（Hausdorff维数）对这类模型的长波长行为至关重要，作者希望研究四配位分形（Sierpinski垫片）上的六顶点模型，探索维数对相关性与约束现象的影响。

Method: 在Sierpinski垫片这一Hausdorff维数为log_2 (3)的四配位层次分形上研究六顶点模型。通过递归方法计算配分函数和关联函数，系统地扫描顶点权重参数空间，绘制相图。等权重点对应冰模型（ice model）。

Result: 获得了包含许多不同区域的丰富相图。冰模型表现出熵驱动的电荷禁闭（entropic charge confinement）；而在一个特定四顶点极限中观察到分形退禁闭（fractal deconfinement），连接退禁闭电荷对的弦本身是一个统计分形，其分形维数d_l=log_2(5/2)≈1.3。此外还提出了人工自旋冰的实验构想来验证这些现象。

Conclusion: Sierpinski垫片上的六顶点模型展现出维度效应导致的独特物理现象，包括冰模型的熵禁闭和特定极限下的分形退禁闭，这些现象有别于传统二维晶格上的行为。所提出的人工自旋冰装置为实验验证这些丰富现象提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We study the six-vertex model on the Sierpinski gasket, a four-coordinated hierarchical fractal with Hausdorff dimension $d_f=\log_23$. Given the importance of dimensionality for the long-wavelength behavior of such models, we specifically consider correlations and confinement as a function of the vertex weights, with the equal-weight point corresponding to the ice model. We calculate the partition function and correlators recursively to obtain a rich phase diagram hosting many different regimes. While the ice model shows entropic charge confinement, a particular four-vertex limit exhibits fractal deconfinement, with the string joining the deconfined charges itself a statistical fractal with fractal dimension $d_l=\log_2(5/2)\approx 1.3$. Finally, we propose a setup as an artificial spin ice to enable experimental study of the rich phenomenology of Sierpinski ice and its generalisations.

</details>


### [162] [Exact Resonances Are Not Sufficient for Phonon Energy Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03180)
*Wei Lin,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在多声子共振条件下，精确共振匹配、非零相互作用系数和网络连通性并不能保证持续的声子能量扩散。对称性驱动的平衡关系使精确共振碰撞电流达到非热零通量态，导致从单个共振集到连通网络的动力学停滞。完整的能量扩散实际上依赖于准共振，且热力学极限与弱非线性极限不可交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多声子共振条件是声子输运和晶格热化的动力学理论的基础，但现有理论仅从共振匹配、相互作用系数和非零网络连通性等运动学角度判断能量扩散的可能性，忽略了动力学层面上的限制因素。作者希望证明这些运动学判据并不充分，从而纠正对声子能量扩散机制的理解。

Method: 作者通过理论分析证明，对称性强制的平衡关系会驱动精确共振碰撞电流达到非热零通量状态，从而导致从单个共振集到连通网络的动力学停滞（kinetic arrest）。同时通过分析热力学极限与弱非线性极限的顺序效应，展示了在不同极限顺序下热化行为的本质差异，并通过高阶交叉（higher-order crossovers）刻画热化时间的发散行为。

Result: 发现精确共振的三要素（共振匹配、非零系数、网络连通）仅在运动学上成立，但不足以作为动力学上的扩散判据。完整能量扩散需要准共振参与；热力学极限先取时恢复主导动力学行为，而在固定有限尺寸下，当非线性趋近于零时热化时间通过高阶交叉而发散。

Conclusion: 精确共振的存在性和连通性只是运动学判据，而非充分的动力学判据。声子能量扩散的真实机制依赖于准共振，且热力学极限与弱非线性极限的不可交换性表明不同极限下的动力学行为存在本质差异。

Abstract: Multi-phonon resonance conditions underpin kinetic theories of phonon transport and lattice thermalization. We show that exact resonance matching, nonzero interaction coefficients, and network connectivity do not guarantee persistent energy diffusion. Symmetry-enforced balance relations drive exact-resonant collision currents to nonthermal zero-flux states, producing kinetic arrest from individual resonant sets to connected networks. Complete energy spreading is sustained by quasi-resonances. The thermodynamic and weak-nonlinearity limits do not commute: the leading kinetic behavior is recovered in the former, whereas at fixed finite size the thermalization time diverges through higher-order crossovers as the nonlinearity vanishes. Exact-resonance existence and connectivity are therefore kinematic, not sufficient dynamical, criteria for phonon energy diffusion.

</details>


### [163] [Two-Parameter Ansatz for the Violation of Eigenstate Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02744)
*Kohei Ohgane,Lev Vidmar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双参数ansatz来描述ETH（本征态热化假说）违反的连续过渡，将ETH破坏统一并区分为两种机制：消退遍历性（fading ergodicity）和陷阱遍历性（trapped ergodicity）。后者描述有限系统中ETH被违反但在热力学极限下恢复的情景。作者在带无序和线性势的spin-1/2 J1-J2链中验证该框架，发现观测到的ETH违反与陷阱遍历性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETH是理解孤立多体系统量子热化与遍历性的主流框架，但目前缺乏统一理论来描述ETH违反如何从常规ETH逐步过渡到完全破坏的连续过程。

Method: 提出一个双参数ansatz来刻画ETH违反，区分"消退遍历性"（已知的遍历性破坏路径）和"陷阱遍历性"（有限系统中ETH违反但在热力学极限下恢复的新场景），并在含无序和线性势的spin-1/2 J1-J2链中进行数值验证。

Result: 在无序和线性势两种情况下，观测到的ETH违反均与陷阱遍历性（trapped ergodicity）机制一致，而非传统的消退遍历性机制。

Conclusion: 该双参数框架为理解ETH违反的连续过渡提供了统一的理论工具，并揭示了一种新的物理场景——陷阱遍历性，即有限尺寸系统中的ETH破坏可能在热力学极限下被恢复。

Abstract: The eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) provides the prevailing framework for understanding quantum thermalization and ergodicity in isolated many-body systems. Yet, no general theory describes the continuous onset of ETH violation between the conventional ETH and its complete breakdown. Here, we introduce a two-parameter ansatz for the ETH violation that unifies and distinguishes two mechanisms: fading ergodicity and trapped ergodicity. While fading ergodicity captures the established route to ergodicity breaking, trapped ergodicity describes a distinct scenario in which ETH is violated in finite systems but restored in the thermodynamic limit. We test this framework in the spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ chains with on-site disorder and linear potential. In both cases, we find that the observed ETH violation is consistent with trapped ergodicity.

</details>


### [164] [Fibonacci number systems and the localization criterion in the many-body Aubry-André model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03458)
*Balázs Hetényi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了分数斐波那契数制系统，它插值于用于自然数的斐波那契数制和用于实数的无理基φ数制之间，并将其应用于多体无相互作用Aubry-André模型的局域化相图解释。对于有限系统尺寸，将粒子密度写入分数斐波那契数制可得到局域化判据；在热力学极限下，判据保留但粒子密度以基φ数制表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立一种统一的数制框架，将斐波那契数制与无理基φ数制联系起来，并利用这一数制体系为Aubry-André模型的局域化-退局域化转变提供新的数学解释工具。

Method: 作者扩展了传统斐波那契数制，引入分数斐波那契数制，使其在有限系统尺寸下描述粒子密度以获得局域化判据；在热力学极限下，粒子密度自然地过渡到无理基φ数制的表示，并讨论了这一极限过程的性质。

Result: 在有限系统中获得了一个基于分数斐波那契数制表示的局域化判据；该判据在热力学极限下依然成立，只是粒子密度改用基φ数制表示。论文还分析了热力学极限的物理本质。

Conclusion: 分数斐波那契数制成功连接了两种已知的数制系统，并为Aubry-André模型的局域化转变提供了一个新的数论视角，且证明了该判据在热力学极限下保持一致性。

Abstract: We introduce an extension of the Fibonacci number system, we call the fractional Fibonacci number system, which interpolates between the Fibonacci number system (used for natural numbers) and the irrational base-$φ$ number system, which can be used to represent real numbers. The number system finds its use in interpreting the localization phase diagram of the many-body non-interacting Aubry-André model. For finite system sizes a localization criterion can be obtained if the particle density is written in the fractional Fibonacci number system. In the thermodynamic limit, the criterion remains, but in this case the particle density is expressed in base-$φ$. The nature of the thermodynamic limit is also discussed.

</details>


### [165] [Discrepancy between the H-Function and Entropy: Insights into the rigorously established Boltzmann equation for hard-sphere gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03355)
*Li-Xiang Cen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过探究H函数与熵之间演化差异的根源，证明了在Boltzmann-Grad极限下严格推导的硬球气体Boltzmann方程中，H函数不能作为有效的时间箭头判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H函数与熵在演化行为上存在差异，需要厘清H函数作为时间箭头判据的有效性，以解决统计力学中关于不可逆性的基础性争论。

Method: 对硬球气体模型在Boltzmann-Grad极限下严格推导的Boltzmann方程进行考察，对比分析H函数与熵的演化性质，追溯二者演化差异的起源。

Result: 揭示了H函数与熵演化差异的根源，并证明H函数在严格推导的Boltzmann方程中不能作为有效的时间箭头判据。

Conclusion: H函数在Boltzmann-Grad极限下的硬球气体Boltzmann方程中不具备时间箭头判据的有效性，这与熵在该框架下的表现形成对比。

Abstract: By investigating the origin of the evolutionary discrepancy between the H-function and entropy, we elucidate that the H-function fails to serve as a valid arrow-of-time criterion in the rigorously derived Boltzmann equation for the hard-sphere gas model under the Boltzmann-Grad limit.

</details>


### [166] [The intermediate scattering function of an interacting adlayer as a characteristic function: a closed-form theory of Ising lattice-gas surface diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03398)
*S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用中间散射函数（ISF）作为特征函数的双重角色（零时刻值为静态结构因子），将Ising格子气类型的侧向相互作用引入表面扩散。ISF在动量传递、时间、覆盖度和温度下均以闭式形式给出，并由此得到完整的线性响应层级。单个相关参数同时固定平衡结构和相互作用重正化的跳跃率，该形式在一维通道中精确成立，并通过配对近似推广到表面。闭式形式并非假设，而是从主方程投影到密度后丢掉记忆核的产物。KMC验证了约1%的跳跃率精度和1-2%的初始集体速率精度。文章给出四个不相关的非临界指纹，可闭式反演以获得尝试频率、静态势垒和相互作用，并应用于Na/Cu(111)和H/Pt(111)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将侧向相互作用引入表面扩散的研究，使得ISF能够闭式计算，从而同时描述平衡结构和相互作用重正化的动力学行为，弥补以往理论中静态结构因子与动力学扩散之间缺乏统一框架的空白。

Method: 将ISF视为特征函数（CF），利用其零时刻值为静态结构因子的性质，在Ising格子气场下推导闭式ISF。通过将主方程投影到密度上获得精确的运动方程，其振幅恒等式成立、初始速率满足求和规则，并丢弃记忆核得到闭式形式。采用最近邻相关的配对近似处理表面，结构因子的动量依赖由集体（随机相位）处理给出。利用KMC验证重正化跳跃率和初始集体速率。

Result: 得到ISF的闭式解，涵盖动量传递、时间、覆盖度和温度；单个相关参数同时确定静态结构与重正化跳跃率。KMC验证跳跃率精度优于1%，初始集体速率精度1-2%。识别出四个非临界指纹（准弹性振幅、线宽、Arrhenius斜率、线宽对拟合时间范围的依赖），并可直接闭式反演出尝试频率、静态势垒和相互作用。de Gennes窄化从类比升格为定理。应用分析于Na/Cu(111)和H/Pt(111)。

Conclusion: 该方法为带侧向相互作用的表面扩散提供了精确且可闭式求解的理论框架，不仅统一了静态与动力学描述，还提供了通过实验可观测的指纹量反演微观参数的途径，并通过KMC验证了其准确性。

Abstract: The intermediate scattering function (ISF) measured by helium spin-echo is a characteristic function (CF): the Fourier transform of the distribution of adsorbate displacements, whose value at zero time is the static structure factor. We use this double role to bring lateral interactions of Ising lattice-gas type into surface diffusion. The ISF then follows in closed form in momentum transfer, time, coverage and temperature, and with it the whole linear-response hierarchy. A single correlation parameter fixes both the equilibrium structure and the interaction-renormalized hop rate; it is exact along a one-dimensional channel, and is carried onto the surface as the nearest-neighbour correlation of a pair approximation, the momentum dependence of the structure factor then coming from a collective (random-phase) treatment. The closed form is not postulated. Projecting the master equation onto the density gives an exact equation of motion whose amplitude is an identity and whose initial rate obeys a sum rule, so that the quasi-elastic weight is the layer's own structure factor and de Gennes narrowing becomes a theorem rather than an analogy; the closed form is what remains when the memory kernel of that equation is dropped. Kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) on the same Hamiltonian reproduces the dressed hop rate to better than about one per cent and the initial collective rate to $1$--$2\%$. Four correlated, non-critical fingerprints of the interaction follow --- in the quasi-elastic amplitude, its linewidth, the Arrhenius slope, and the dependence of the fitted linewidth on the range of times over which the decay is fitted. They invert in closed form for the attempt frequency, the static barrier and the interaction. Applications to Na/Cu(111) and H/Pt(111) are analysed and discussed.

</details>


### [167] [Lower bounds on entropy production from dynamical correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03619)
*Paul Raux,Alexander M. Maier,Udo Seifert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对部分观测和粗粒化系统中熵产生率的推断问题，推导了基于粗粒化可观测量两点时间关联函数不对称性的两个熵产生下界：一个适用于任意相关时间延迟的非平衡稳态，另一个适用于时间相关过程且需要延迟趋于零的极限。这两个下界对潜藏的马尔可夫动力学或过阻尼朗之万方程耦合系统均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机热力学中，熵产生是关键性质，但在部分观测或粗粒化系统中其推断具有挑战性，通常依赖于已证明的下界。作者旨在推导新的、基于实验可测量的两点时间关联函数不对称性的熵产生下界，以扩展现有推断方法的适用范围。

Method: 作者从粗粒化状态可观测量（coarse-grained state observables）的实验可获取的时间关联函数出发，利用其不对称性构造熵产生率的下界。针对两类动力学（马尔可夫动力学和耦合过阻尼朗之万方程）以及两类过程（非平衡稳态和时间依赖过程）分别推导了相应的下界形式，并讨论了这些下界的优化策略及其可能的紧致性（tightness）。

Result: 得到了两个版本的熵产生下界：1）对于非平衡稳态，下界在任意时间延迟下均成立；2）对于时间依赖过程，下界仅在延迟趋于零的极限下成立。这些下界对遵循潜藏马尔可夫动力学或耦合过阻尼朗之万方程的任何系统均成立，并通过两类动力学示例验证了下界的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为部分观测系统的熵产生率推断提供了一种新的、基于时间关联函数不对称性的下界方法，适用于非平衡稳态和时间依赖过程两类场景，对任意粗粒化状态可观测量和广泛动力学类型均具有普适性，并可通过优化进一步提升下界的紧致性。

Abstract: Entropy production is a key property in stochastic thermodynamics. For partially observed and coarse-grained systems, its inference is challenging and typically rests on proven lower bounds. We derive two versions of such bounds based on the asymmetry of experimentally accessible two-time correlation functions of coarse-grained state observables. For non-equilibrium steady states, the bound is valid for arbitrary correlation lag. For time-dependent processes, it requires the limit of vanishing lag. These bounds hold true for any system that follows either a Markovian dynamics or a coupled set of overdamped Langevin equations on some underlying, unobservable level of description. We illustrate the bounds for both types of dynamics and discuss their optimization and potential tightness.

</details>


### [168] [Kappa distributions as asymptotic marginals of exponential family ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03960)
*Sergio Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于Physica A 660 130370(2025)中关于驱动经典系统中超统计行为的最新结果，证明了当N→∞时，总动能服从逆伽马分布的N粒子系统必然产生κ分布的单粒子速度。该发现为κ分布提供了非广义统计力学之外的物理解释，并提出了一种利用逆伽马系综通过蒙特卡罗Metropolis模拟生成κ速度的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索κ分布在非广义统计力学框架之外的物理解理基础，并将超统计行为的最新理论成果（针对恒定微正则热容系统）应用到具体粒子系统中，从而为κ分布的本质提供新见解。

Method: 利用超统计行为理论，将N粒子系统的总动能设定为逆伽马分布，在N→∞极限下推导单粒子速度分布，并通过逆伽马系综进行蒙特卡罗Metropolis模拟生成κ速度。

Result: 在N→∞极限下，逆伽马总动能分布的系统的单粒子速度严格趋向于κ分布；同时提出了一种基于逆伽马系综的蒙特卡罗Metropolis方法用于实际生成κ分布速度。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了逆伽马分布、超统计行为与κ分布之间的联系，为κ分布提供了独立于非广延统计力学的理论基础，并给出了实用的数值模拟生成方案。

Abstract: Recently (Physica A 660 130370, 2025), emergence of superstatistical behavior in driven classical systems has been shown for systems with constant microcanonical heat capacity. As an application of this result, in this work we show that a system of $N$ particles with inverse gamma distribution of total kinetic energies must have kappa-distributed single-particle velocities in the limit $N \rightarrow \infty$. Our results provide insight into the nature of kappa distributions outside the theory of nonextensive statistical mechanics, while also bringing forward a practical method for the generation of kappa velocities via Monte Carlo Metropolis simulation in the inverse gamma ensemble.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [A Dimension-Two Counterexample to the Separable Jacobian Conjecture in Characteristic Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02634)
*Romy Mondello*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在特征2的代数闭域上构造了一个二维反例F=(P,Q)，其Jacobian行列式为1（即étale），但不是自同构，且其泛次数为3（与特征互素），从而反驳了特征2情形的可分Jacobian猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobian猜想在特征2情形下一直悬而未决，特别是"可分Jacobian猜想"（即若Jacobian为1且扩张可分，则F为自同构）在二维情形是否成立此前未知。本文旨在通过构造一个具体的多项式自同态，展示该猜想在特征2下不成立。

Method: 给定F=(P,Q)，其中P=x+x^2y+x^4+x^6y^2，Q=y+x^5+x^6y+x^7y^2+x^8y^3。作者通过以下步骤证明：(1) 直接验证Jacobian行列式为1；(2) 利用三个不同点(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)映射到同一像这一事实辅助分析；(3) 通过隐式三次恢复、在实际目标域上的不可约性论证、以及函数域嵌入与几何泛纤维之间的桥梁，证明[k(x,y):k(P,Q)]=3且扩张可分；(4) 给出显式图表示证明F是étale；(5) 从Irit Huq-Kuruvilla的三变量映射的坐标置换形式推导出F。附录中还记录了Lean形式化验证和Harpocrite replay的精确范围。

Result: 成功构造了二维特征2下Jacobian为1但非自同构的多项式映射F，其泛次数为3（与特征2互素），扩张可分，从而给出可分Jacobian猜想的二维反例。同时给出了F是étale的显式图论证明，并关联到Irit Huq-Kuruvilla的三变量映射。

Conclusion: 在特征2的二维情形下，Jacobian为1且泛扩张可分并不能保证F为自同构，分可Jacobian猜想在特征2下不成立。这为Jacobian猜想在不同特征下的行为提供了新的边界认识。

Abstract: Let k be the algebraic closure of F_2. We study the polynomial endomorphism F=(P,Q) of the affine plane, where P=x+x^2 y+x^4+x^6 y^2 and Q=y+x^5+x^6 y+x^7 y^2+x^8 y^3. Its Jacobian determinant is 1, while the three distinct points (0,1), (1,0), and (1,1) have the same image. We prove that [k(x,y):k(P,Q)]=3 and that the extension is separable. Thus the generic degree is prime to the characteristic although F is not an automorphism, giving a dimension-two counterexample to the separable Jacobian conjecture in characteristic two. The proof uses explicit recovery from a hidden cubic, irreducibility over the actual target field, and a bridge between function-field embeddings and the geometric generic fiber. We also give an explicit graph presentation proving that F is etale and derive the map from a coordinate-permuted form of a three-variable map of Irit Huq-Kuruvilla. An appendix records the precise scope and evidence boundaries of a Lean formalization and an independent Harmonic Aristotle replay.

</details>


### [170] [Hulls, linear equivalence, and weighted superelliptic codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02850)
*Jurgen Mezinaj,Tanush Shaska*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究代数几何码的交码与并码，将\"主理想交构造一维高码\"的已知结果转化为一种度量。对任意除子G和A，精确计算了C_L(D,G)∩C_L(D,A)，表示为meet码加一个过剩项ε(G,A)，该过剩项恰好在meet非特殊时为零，否则它判定K−G∧A线性等价于某个有效除子。对超椭圆曲线y^n=f(x)，利用加权平面模型将高（hull）转化为格点计数，确定了ε"盲区"的显式度数区间，并证明在高仅依赖于仿射点数的条件下，最大高在完全分裂点集上达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明G∧A为主理想时（即一维高情形）可以构造代数几何码，本文的动机是将这一构造推广为度量工具：对任意除子精确刻画交码的结构，并利用高（hull）的性质来探测线性等价关系、区分同类的除子类，最终在超椭圆曲线上给出高分布的完全刻画。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 对任意除子G,A精确计算C_L(D,G)∩C_L(D,A)，将其分解为meet码与过剩项ε(G,A)，后者为H^1(O(G∧A))的商与子商；(2) 证明ε的消失等价于meet非特殊，并在度数为零时等价于Picard群中某单类的消失，将构造线性等价转化为检测线性等价；(3) 对超椭圆曲线y^n=f(x)，利用加权射影平面P^2_{(1,n/c,d/c)}（c=gcd(n,d)）的加权平面模型，将次数s加权形式的码C_s与sD_∞上的码等同，从而把高化为格点计数；(4) 给出ε盲区的显式度数区间，并在此区间内计算dim Hull(C_s)=cμ(s)−nδ+1−g_X；(5) 通过对有限域上总分裂亏格二曲线的算例展示高谱线单独区分同度数同类的曲线。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 交码的精确分解公式，过剩项ε(G,A)刻画了meet非特殊性的偏离；(2) 当0≤deg(G∧A)≤2g_X−2时，高维数至多为g_X+1，即高只在ε"盲区"才可能大；(3) 在素数域上，在关于(n,d,q)的显式不等式条件下，整个族上高的最大值是ℓ(⌊M/2⌋D_∞)，且在完全分裂点集上唯一达到；(4) 两个亏格二曲线（F_7与F_11上的总分裂曲线）具有相同的除子类与相同度数组，但被高谱线单独区分。

Conclusion: 本文将交码的代数结构（meet/join、过剩项）与高维数联系起来，表明高不仅是一种码论不变量，更是检测线性等价的工具：高能够在meet与join的对称性盲区之外区分除子类，给出比除子类更精细的区分能力。对超椭圆曲线情形获得了完全显式的高分布描述和最大值刻画。

Abstract: The containment of the code of the meet $G\wedge A$ in the hull and the identity $G\vee A-D=K-G\wedge A$ exchanging meet and join are known; imposing that $G\wedge A$ be principal constructs algebraic geometry codes with one-dimensional hull. We turn that construction into a measurement. For arbitrary divisors $G$ and $A$ we compute $C_L(D,G)\cap C_L(D,A)$ exactly: it is the code of the meet together with an excess $\varepsilon(G,A)$, canonically their quotient and a subquotient of $H^1(\mathcal O(G\wedge A))$. So $\varepsilon$ vanishes exactly when the meet is non-special; otherwise it certifies that $K-G\wedge A$ is linearly equivalent to an effective divisor, at degree zero the vanishing of a single class in the Picard group: the hull detects a linear equivalence rather than being built from one. For superelliptic curves $y^n=f(x)$ both sides can be computed: their weighted plane models in $\mathbb P^2_{(1,n/c,d/c)}$, $c=\gcd(n,d)$, identify codes $C_s$ of weighted forms of degree $s$ with those of $sD_\infty$ and turn hulls into lattice counts. The range on which $\varepsilon$ is blind is an explicit interval of degrees, where $\dim\operatorname{Hull}(C_s)=cμ(s)-nδ+1-g_X$, $μ(s)=\min\{s,M-s\}$, depends only on its affine-point count. Outside it the meet and join are invariant under $s\mapsto M-s$ while $\varepsilon$ is not, so every asymmetry of the hull profile is excess and the threshold in $s$ refines the divisor class: two totally split curves of genus two, over $\mathbb F_7$ and over $\mathbb F_{11}$, present the same class at the same pair of degrees and are separated by the profile alone. If $0\leq°(G\wedge A)\leq2g_X-2$ the hull is at most $g_X+1$, so it is large only where it is blind, and over a prime field, under an explicit inequality on $(n,d,q)$, its maximum over the family is $\ell(\lfloor M/2\rfloor D_\infty)$, attained exactly on the totally split locus.

</details>


### [171] [Orbits and Fields of Definition for Graded Keller Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02863)
*Kyle Kistner,Tanush Shaska*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文根据权向量的签名对分层Keller映射进行分类，并研究哪些双曲权重支持反例以及非空轨迹的分层结构。作者构造了两族反例：循环锥族填充K(3,(1,-1,-1))，对角族给出K(3,(1,-p,-p))非空。所有成员的几何单值群都是全度的交错群或对称群，可解情形仅出现在一般次数3。在一般次数12时，归一化四次循环种子构成Q上的光滑平面三次曲线，证明相关轨道空间包含非平凡代数族，确立了真正的模空间而非孤立例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分层Keller猜想的一个算术/代数几何方向：对二元多项式自同构（Keller映射）的Jacobi行列式问题进行分类，特别关注双曲权重向量下反例的存在性及其模结构，以深化对Jacobian猜想反例的理解。

Method: 通过权向量的签名（符号模式）对Keller映射进行分类；构造循环锥族（在一般次数N≥6时填充K(3,(1,-1,-1))）和对角族（给出K(3,(1,-p,-p))）；利用几何单值群分析衡量映射的代数结构；通过归一化四次循环种子构造光滑平面三次曲线上的点对应到不同的分级等价轨道。

Result: 循环锥族在一般纤维次数N≥6时填充K(3,(1,-1,-1))，包括Q(√-15)上的6叶成员和Q上的33叶成员；对角族对每个p≥2给出K(3,(1,-p,-p))非空，具有det JF=-p和一般纤维次数2p-1；所有成员的几何单值群是全度的A或S；可解情形仅在一般次数3出现；一般次数12时，四次循环种子的轨迹构成Q上的光滑平面三次曲线，产生非平凡模空间。

Conclusion: 本文不仅构造了新的Keller映射反例族，更重要的是证明了相关轨道空间包含非孤立的代数族，即存在真实的模空间结构。这从模空间角度深化了对Jacobian猜想及分层Keller猜想的研究，表明双曲权重下存在丰富的反例结构。

Abstract: We classify graded Keller maps by the signature of their weight vectors and address which hyperbolic weights support counterexamples and how the nonempty loci are stratified. We construct two families. Cyclic cones populate \(K(3,(1,-1,-1))\) at every composite generic fibre degree \(N\geq 6\), including a six-sheeted member over \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-15})\) and a 33-sheeted member over \(\mathbb{Q}\). A diagonal family gives \(K(3,(1,-p,-p))\neq\varnothing\) for every \(p\geq2\), with \(\det JF_p=-p\) and generic fibre degree \(2p-1\). The geometric monodromy of every member of both families is alternating or symmetric of full degree; hence the solvable case occurs only in generic degree three. At generic degree twelve, normalized quartic cyclic seeds form a smooth plane cubic over \(\mathbb{Q}\), whose points yield pairwise distinct graded-equivalence orbits. Thus the relevant orbit space contains a nonconstant algebraic family, establishing genuine moduli rather than isolated examples."

</details>


### [172] [Murphy's law in non-abelian Hodge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02875)
*Gregorio Baldi,Yeuk Hay Joshua Lam*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了非整性 ℚ-变分Hodge结构（ℚVHS）的显式例子，利用Kabaya和Maskit的Fenchel-Nielsen型参数化理论描述相对特征簇的Teichmüller分量；同时探讨了这类特征簇上 𝒪_{K,S}-整点相关的丢番图结果，并给出Beauville经典定理（椭圆曲线族）的新证明；最后收集了非整性ℚVHS的Hodge轨迹的"病态行为"，包括Cattani–Deligne–Kaplan定理和André–Oort猜想的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变分Hodge结构理论（如CDK定理、André–Oort猜想）主要针对整性（ℤVHS）情形，而对于更一般的非整性ℚVHS，其结构和性质尚缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在构造显式的非整性ℚVHS例子，探索其Hodge轨迹的病理行为，从而澄清ℚVHS与ℤVHS之间的本质差异。

Method: 1) 利用Kabaya和Maskit发展的Fenchel-Nielsen型坐标参数化来构造相对特征簇的Teichmüller分量，从而得到非整性ℚVHS的显式构造；2) 结合丢番图方法研究这些特征簇上的𝒪_{K,S}-整点；3) 通过对这些例子的系统分析，收集非整性ℚVHS中Hodge轨迹的病理现象（如CDK定理和André–Oort猜想的失效）。

Result: 成功构造了非整性ℚVHS的显式例子；给出了关于此类特征簇整点的若干丢番图结果；提供了Beauville定理的新证明；并发现非整性ℚVHS中CDK定理与André–Oort猜想均可能失效，表明"凡能出错之处必然出错"。

Conclusion: 本文工作揭示了非整性ℚVHS与整性ℤVHS在结构上的根本差异。整性假设（ℤVHS）对变分Hodge结构理论的诸多关键定理至关重要：一旦去掉这一假设，不仅构造出反例，而且经典结果（如CDK定理、André–Oort猜想）的系统性失效表明整性条件在本质上不可弱化。

Abstract: We construct explicit examples of non-integral variations of $\mathbb{Q}$-Hodge structures. Our approach leverages Fenchel--Nielsen-type parameterizations, due to Kabaya and Maskit, of the Teichmüller component of relative character varieties. Additionally, we discuss various Diophantine results concerning the $\mathcal{O}_{K,S}$-integral points of such character varieties, and give a new proof of Beauville's classical theorem on families of elliptic curves. We conclude by collecting \emph{pathological behaviors} of the Hodge locus of non-integral $\mathbb{Q}$VHS, in particular the failure of the Cattani--Deligne--Kaplan theorem and the André--Oort conjecture; our results indicate that for $\mathbb{Q}$VHS which are not $\mathbb{Z}$VHS, what can go wrong must go wrong.

</details>


### [173] [Unifying singular value decompositions of tensors via aligned orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03202)
*Alvaro Ribot*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基对齐双正交（bato）张量，这类张量可表示为若干临界秩一近似的和，其因子为其展开的奇异向量。证明了通用bato分解的可辨识性、截断的临界低bato秩近似性质，计算了bato张量集的维数，并将其Zariski闭包的不可约分量与最大部分拉丁超矩形的同痕类联系起来。许多重要张量（如行列式、矩阵乘法张量）都是bato张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接张量的Tucker分解与CP分解之间的关系，深入研究一类特殊的、具有良好代数与几何结构的张量（bato张量），并建立其与组合结构（拉丁超矩形）之间的联系。

Method: 从代数几何和多重线性代数的角度出发，定义基对齐双正交（bato）张量；分析其Tucker分解与CP分解的对应关系；利用代数几何工具证明通用分解的可辨识性；通过计算维数和Zariski闭包来刻画bato张量集的几何结构，并将其与拉丁超矩形的同痕类建立对应。

Result: 证明了通用bato分解是可辨识的；截断保持临界低bato秩近似性质；计算了bato张量集的维数；识别出Zariski闭包不可约分量与最大部分拉丁超矩形同痕类的一一对应关系。

Conclusion: bato张量是一类结构丰富且重要的张量类，兼具可辨识性这一计算优势和清晰的代数几何刻画，其理论框架统一了多种重要张量（行列式、矩阵乘法张量等），为张量分解理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study basis-aligned two-orthogonal (bato) tensors, which can be written as a sum of critical rank-one approximations whose factors are singular vectors of their flattenings. As such, bato tensors admit a Tucker decomposition and a canonical polyadic decomposition that are related to each other. We prove that generic bato decompositions are identifiable and that their truncations are critical low-bato-rank approximations. We also compute the dimension of the set of bato tensors, and identify the irreducible components of its Zariski closure with isotopy classes of maximal partial Latin hyperrectangles. Many important tensors are bato, such as determinants, matrix multiplication tensors, and other structure tensors of algebras.

</details>


### [174] [Unirationality is the same thing as Rational Connectedness in Characteristic Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03255)
*Stephen Maguire*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特征零的域上，光滑射影簇的单有理性、有理连通性和有理链连通性是等价的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何中，单有理性（unirationality）、有理连通性（rational connectedness）和有理链连通性（rational chain connectedness）是三个重要的有理性概念，此前已知它们之间存在蕴含关系，但逆方向是否成立（特别是在一般射影簇上）一直未完全解决。本文旨在证明这三个概念在特征零的光滑射影簇上完全一致。

Method: 本文使用MRC纤维化（Maximal Rationally Connected fibration）方法。具体地，构造一个簇MU(X)以及两个有理映射π: X→MU(X)和λ: MU(X)→MRC(X)，使得λ∘π=ν（其中ν是X到MRC(X)的MRC纤维化映射）。关键性质是：π的极一般纤维是单有理的，而λ的极一般纤维是有理连通但非单有理的。随后通过归纳论证证明MU(X)与MRC(X)双有理等价。

Result: 证明了对于特征零域上的光滑射影簇，单有理性、有理连通性与有理链连通性三者等价。具体而言，通过构造的中间簇MU(X)及归纳论证，证明了MU(X)与MRC(X)双有理等价，从而得出这三个概念的一致性。

Conclusion: 在特征零的光滑射影簇范畴中，单有理性、有理连通性和有理链连通性是三个等价的概念。这一结果统一并完善了代数几何中有理性理论的基本框架。

Abstract: In this paper we prove that unirationality, rational connectedness and rational chain connectedness coincide for smooth projective varieties over a field $ k $ of characteristic zero. Our approach uses the MRC fibration to show that if $ X $ is a smooth projective variety, then there exists a variety $ \operatorname{MU}(X) $, together with rational maps $ π: X \dashrightarrow \operatorname{MU}(X) $ and $ λ: \operatorname{MU}(X) \dashrightarrow \operatorname{MRC}(X) $, such that i) if $ ν: X \dashrightarrow \operatorname{MRC}(X) $, then $ λ\circ π= ν$ on the appropriate domains; ii) the very general fibres of $ π$ are unirational; iii) the very general fibres of $ λ$ are rationally connected but not unirational. We then apply an induction argument to show that $ \operatorname{MU}(X) $ is birationally equivalent to $ \operatorname{MRC}(X) $.

</details>


### [175] [A klt generalized pair with infinitely generated canonical ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03258)
*Jihao Liu,Yanze Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个定义在复数域上的射影klt广义对，其广义对数典范环不是有限生成的，以此否定或补全广义对理论中的有限生成性质。论文成果由生成式AI（GPT-5.6-sol-ultra、Fable 5和Danus系统）辅助完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义对（generalized pairs）是双有理几何中近年来发展的重要工具，广义对数典范环的有限生成性是其中的核心问题之一。构造非有限生成的例子对于理解广义对的奇点和环结构的边界具有重要理论意义。

Method: 通过在复数域上构造一个射影klt广义对，并分析其广义对数典范环的生成性质，证明该环不是有限生成的。方法涉及广义对数典范环的代数结构分析。

Result: 成功构造了一个射影klt广义对，其广义对数典范环不是有限生成的，给出了该性质的反例。

Conclusion: 广义对数典范环在klt广义对的情形下不总是有限生成的，这一发现对广义对理论中有限生成性的适用范围提出了新的边界条件。此外，本成果借助生成式AI技术完成，展现了AI在代数几何研究中辅助证明构造的潜力。

Abstract: We construct a projective klt generalized pair over the complex numbers whose generalized log canonical ring is not finitely generated. The main result of this paper is obtained by generative AI, particularly GPT-5.6-sol-ultra, Fable 5, and the Danus system.

</details>


### [176] [On Fano indices of weighted projective spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03434)
*Haidong Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了n维具有典范奇点的权射影空间的Fano指标被(S_n-1)(2S_n-3)所界定（其中S_n为Sylvester序列），肯定回答了Chengxi Wang关于权射影空间及Picard数为一的Q-因子拓扑Fano簇的猜想，并研究了4维权射影空间Fano指标的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chengxi Wang提出了关于权射影空间Fano指标上界的猜想，作者希望通过Sylvester序列给出该猜想的肯定回答，并进一步探索Fano指标的分布规律。

Method: 利用Sylvester序列的递推性质，对n维具有典范奇点的权射影空间的Fano指标进行上界估计；同时通过计算和分析4维权射影空间Fano指标的分布，并与低维情形进行比较。

Result: 证明了Fano指标的上界为(S_n-1)(2S_n-3)，肯定回答了Chengxi Wang的猜想；揭示了4维情形的Fano指标分布与3维及以下终端Calabi-Yau簇指标分布的一致性现象。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅证明了Wang的猜想，还揭示了权射影空间Fano指标的分布与终端Calabi-Yau簇指标分布之间的深层联系，并推测这种一致性在4维及所有维度中持续成立。

Abstract: The Sylvester sequence is defined recursively by $s_1=2$ and $s_i=s_{1}\cdots s_{i-1}+1$. In this paper, we prove that the Fano index of an $n$-dimensional well-formed weighted projective space with canonical singularities is bounded above by \[ (s_n-1)(2s_n-3). \] This gives an affirmative answer to a conjecture of Chengxi Wang for weighted projective spaces and $\mathbb Q$-factorial toric Fano varieties with Picard number one.
  We also investigate the distribution of Fano indices among $4$-dimensional weighted projective spaces. As the distribution of Fano indices of weighted projective spaces coincides with that of indices of terminal Calabi--Yau varieties in dimension $n\leq 3$, we expect this coincidence to persist also in dimension 4, and more generally, in all dimensions.

</details>


### [177] [Singularities of Amoeba Contours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03601)
*Mounir Nisse*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了计算变形虫轮廓（amoeba contours）奇点的显式实代数方程，可区分对数临界轨迹的退化与不同临界抬升的重合。对于平面曲线，该方法提供了检测节点、尖点和多分支的实用系统。此外，论文证明最大稀疏性并不能使轮廓奇点数量最大化，即使在格三角形和平行四边形情形下也是如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形虫轮廓的奇点结构与其所关联的代数曲线的临界行为密切相关，但此前缺乏系统性的代数计算工具来区分不同类型的奇点来源（退化与抬升重合），且对稀疏性与轮廓奇点数量之间的关系理解不足。

Method: 推导用于计算变形虫轮廓奇点的显式实代数方程，通过判别对数临界轨迹的退化与临界抬升的偶然重合来分类奇点；在平面曲线情形下将其转化为检测节点、尖点及多分支的实用代数系统，并通过格三角形和平行四边形的实例进行验证。

Result: 得到了计算轮廓奇点的显式实代数方程，可系统性地识别平面曲线的节点、尖点和多分支；同时构造反例表明最大稀疏性并不最大化轮廓奇点数量。

Conclusion: 显式代数方程提供了一种实用的奇点检测工具；稀疏性与轮廓奇点最大化之间的关系比之前认为的更微妙，需要更精细的刻画。

Abstract: We give explicit real-algebraic equations for computing the singularities of amoeba contours, separating degeneracies of the logarithmic critical locus from coincidences of distinct critical lifts. For plane curves, this yields practical systems detecting nodes, cusps, and multiple branches. We also show that maximal sparsity does not maximize contour singularities, even for lattice triangles and parallelograms.

</details>


### [178] [Cusps and nodes of Amoeba Contours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03613)
*Mounir Nisse*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为光滑平面曲线的阿米巴轮廓奇点建立了新的牛顿多边形界。尖点估计将Lang-Shapiro-Shustin的4次界从首项系数8改善到4，同时保留牛顿多边形的归一化面积、边界格点数和方向宽度；还获得了横向s-节点的新的重数敏感界，带有自然衰减因子1/C(s,2)。证明结合了对数高斯映射、归一化纤维积、分歧理论和饱和非对角交截方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑平面曲线的阿米巴轮廓奇点（尖点和多重点）的牛顿多边形界是复分析几何中的重要问题。作者希望改进已有的Lang-Shapiro-Shustin尖点界，并首次系统研究多重点的重数敏感界。

Method: 综合运用对数高斯映射、归一化纤维积、分歧理论以及饱和非对角交截方案等多种工具，分别揭示尖点和多重点的不同几何机制。

Result: 将尖点界的首项系数从8降至4，同时不损失任何牛顿多边形不变量；建立了横向s-节点的重数敏感界，衰减因子为1/C(s,2)，该因子在组合学上是自然的。

Conclusion: 作者给出了更精细的奇点界，证明了尖点与多重点受不同的几何机制支配，并为未来在阿米巴与热带几何方向的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We establish new Newton-polygon bounds for the singularities of amoeba contours of smooth plane curves. Our cusp estimate refines the degree-four bound of Lang--Shapiro--Shustin, reducing its leading coefficient from $8$ to $4$ while retaining the normalized area, boundary lattice points, and directional widths of the Newton polygon. We also obtain a new multiplicity-sensitive bound for transverse $s$-nodes, with the natural decay factor $1/\binom{s}{2}$. The proofs combine logarithmic Gauss maps, normalized fiber products, ramification theory, and saturated off-diagonal intersection schemes, revealing the distinct geometric mechanisms governing cusps and multiple nodes.

</details>


### [179] [The period-index conjecture is false](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03684)
*Alexander Perry*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了在任意不可数、特征为0的代数闭域k上，满足d≥3维的簇，其Brauer类因Hodge理论原因违反周期-指数猜想。当d=3时，构造在k可数时也成立，特别地，周期-指数猜想在代数闭域&#771;Q上失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期-指数猜想（period-index conjecture）是数论与代数几何中关于Brauer群的重要猜想。作者的目标是构造反例，以表明该猜想在一般情况下不成立，且反例来源于Hodge理论方面的结构原因，而非其他数论因素。

Method: 在特征0的代数闭域上，通过构造维数d≥3的簇及其上的Brauer类，利用Hodge理论（如Hodge结构、循环覆叠或相关代数几何工具）来产生周期与指数之间的差异，从而构造出违反周期-指数猜想的反例。

Result: 成功构造了d≥3维簇上的Brauer类违反周期-指数猜想；在d=3时，构造不依赖k的不可数性，特别证明了该猜想在&#771;Q（代数闭有理数域）上失败。

Conclusion: 周期-指数猜想在一般特征0代数闭域上的高维簇中不成立，其失败可由Hodge理论原因解释；在三维情形中，甚至在&#771;Q上也存在反例。

Abstract: For any uncountable algebraically closed field $k$ of characteristic $0$ and any $d \geq 3$, we construct a variety over $k$ of dimension $d$ with a Brauer class which violates the period-index conjecture for Hodge-theoretic reasons. When $d = 3$, our construction works even without the assumption that $k$ is uncountable; in particular, the period-index conjecture fails over $\overline{\mathbf{Q}}$.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Stochastic Saddle Avoidance Beyond Unit Excitation and Smoothness: A Pathwise Lyapunov-Perron Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03001)
*Junwen Qiu,Bohao Ma,Andre Milzarek,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖于单元激励（UE）假设的抽象几乎必然避让定理，用于随机递归的鞍点避让分析，并通过路径依赖的变量替换和路径wise Lyapunov-Perron证明策略，将其应用于随机镜像下降、随机重排和近端随机梯度方法，证明其收敛到局部极小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UE假设（随机误差在每个方向上有一致正分量）虽然能直接排除严格鞍点的收敛，但过于简化了实际噪声结构。在过参数化/插值模型中噪声可能消失，在有限和问题中噪声可能位于低维数据相关子空间，UE在这些常见场景下不成立。因此需要一种不依赖UE的鞍点避让理论。

Method: 提出一个不依赖UE的抽象几乎必然避让定理，用可验证的路径条件替代UE类要求。采用路径依赖的变量替换（path-dependent change of variables）结合路径wise Lyapunov-Perron证明策略，规避了传统确定性分析中中心稳定流形论证因随机采样映射不共享固定点而无法直接应用的问题。

Result: 获得了随机镜像下降（包括SGD）和随机重排（random reshuffling）的严格鞍点避让结果；对非光滑复合目标，证明了近端型随机梯度方法的避让结果。结合迭代收敛保证，建立了收敛到原始目标函数局部极小值的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了无UE假设下随机鞍点避让理论的空白，通过路径条件这一更贴合实际噪声结构的方法，将鞍点避让理论扩展到过参数化、有限和、无放回采样等更实际的优化场景。

Abstract: Unit excitation (UE) is a common assumption in stochastic saddle avoidance: the stochastic error must have a uniformly positive component along every direction, in expectation. This condition gives a direct way to rule out convergence to strict saddles, but it also oversimplifies the actual noise structure, and does not match many stochastic optimization regimes. In overparameterized or interpolation models, the noise may vanish near stationarity. In finite-sum problems, the stochastic gradient noise may lie in a low-dimensional, data-dependent subspace. In these (common) scenarios, UE is naturally not satisfied. In this paper, we prove an abstract almost sure avoidance theorem for stochastic recursions without UE. The theorem replaces UE-type requirements by verifiable pathwise conditions. In applications, these conditions follow, e.g., from local smoothness and finite-moment assumptions under standard i.i.d. sampling, or from the finite-sum structure under without-replacement sampling. Since the stochastically sampled maps generally do not share a fixed point, the celebrated center-stable manifold argument used in deterministic analyses is not directly applicable. Instead, we use a path-dependent change of variables together with a pathwise Lyapunov--Perron-based proof strategy. As applications, we obtain strict saddle avoidance for stochastic mirror descent (including SGD) and for random reshuffling. For nonsmooth composite objectives, we prove avoidance results for a proximal-type stochastic gradient method. Combining these insights with suitable iterate convergence guarantees, this allows establishing convergence to local minimizers of the original objective function.

</details>


### [181] [Oracle-Based Distributionally Robust Optimization under Optimal Transport Ambiguity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03075)
*Guixian Chen,Salar Fattahi,Soroosh Shafiee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对最优传输模糊集下的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）问题，提出将内层最坏情况期望问题精确约简为标量预算分配任务，从而绕过传统单层凸规划重构带来的大量辅助变量和锥约束。该算法基于分布最佳响应框架计算近似原始-对偶解，并针对对偶DRO证明了最小不利分布的存在性及支撑规模上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRO通过将minimax问题重构为单层凸规划来求解，虽然理论上可处理，但引入大量辅助变量和苛刻的锥约束，实际扩展性差。因此需要一种更高效的算法来替代这种大规模提升（lifted）重构方法。

Method: 1) 将内层最坏情况期望问题精确约简为一维标量预算分配任务；2) 设计基于oracle的分布最佳响应（best-response）框架，直接计算近似的原始-对偶解；3) 提出后处理方案，恢复最多N+1个支撑点的最优最坏情况分布；4) 将对偶DRO约简为凸规划，提取具有至多min{N+n+1, KN}个支撑原子的最小不利分布。

Result: 证明了在原始DRO中可恢复支撑至多N+1点的最优最坏情况分布，在对偶DRO中最小不利分布支撑至多min{N+n+1, KN}个原子。数值实验表明，所提方法显著优于基于重构的最先进求解器。

Conclusion: 通过将内部最坏情况期望问题约简为标量预算分配，提出了一种避免大规模提升重构的高效算法，并在原始和对偶DRO两个层面都提供了理论支撑与实用求解方案，在扩展性和计算效率上均有显著提升。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) with optimal transport ambiguity sets is traditionally solved by reformulating the minimax problem into a single-level convex program. While theoretically tractable, these reformulations introduce numerous auxiliary variables and demanding conic constraints that scale poorly in practice. In this paper, we address this challenge by reducing the inner worst-case expectation problem exactly to a scalar budget allocation task. This structural insight yields an efficient algorithm that bypasses large lifted reformulations, alongside a fast post-processing scheme to recover an optimal worst-case distribution supported on at most $N+1$ points, where $N$ denotes the sample size. We embed this procedure within an oracle-based distributional best-response framework to directly compute an approximate primal-dual solution to the overall DRO problem. Furthermore, we extend our analysis to the dual DRO formulation, proving the existence of a least-favorable distribution supported on at most $\min\{N+n+1, KN\}$ atoms, where $n$ and $K$ denote the decision dimension and number of loss components, respectively, and provide an efficient convex programming reduction to extract it from the solution of the primal DRO. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art reformulation-based solvers.

</details>


### [182] [Optimal Nonergodic Primal-Dual Complexity of Efficient Inexact Parameter-Free Augmented Lagrangian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03170)
*Arnesh Sujanani,Saeed Ghadimi,Henry Wołkowicz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为线性约束凸复合优化问题提出了三种非精确增广拉格朗日（AL）方法，以可验证的近似KKT解为目标，在凸情形下达到最优的O(ε⁻¹)原始-对偶复杂度，在强凸情形下达到近最优的O(ε⁻¹/²log(ε⁻¹))复杂度，并通过大量数值实验验证了相比近端增广拉格朗日（PAL）方法5至50倍的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸优化算法（如近端增广拉格朗日PAL方法）在原始-对偶复杂度上存在次优性（如O(ε⁻⁴/³)、O(ε⁻⁷/⁴)、O(ε⁻²)以及O(ε⁻¹log(ε⁻¹))），且多数基于目标值误差而非可验证的KKT条件。本文旨在设计能以可验证的近似KKT解为收敛判据、同时达到最优复杂度且无需调参的非精确增广拉格朗日算法。

Method: 针对目标为光滑凸函数与非光滑闭真凸函数（定义域紧致）之和的线性约束凸复合优化问题，本文提出了三种非精确增广拉格朗日方案。在凸情形下，三种方案均达到最优复杂度O(ε⁻¹)，其中两个变体为参数无关（parameter-free）；在强凸情形下达到近最优复杂度O(ε⁻¹/²log(ε⁻¹))，同样有两个参数无关变体。方法还提供非遍历收敛保证，其中一种变体具有更强的最后迭代（last-iterate）收敛保证。

Result: 理论方面，三种非精确AL方案在凸情形下实现了最优原始-对偶复杂度O(ε⁻¹)，优于PAL方法的O(ε⁻¹log(ε⁻¹))以及此前AL方法O(ε⁻⁴/³)至O(ε⁻²)的界；强凸情形实现近最优O(ε⁻¹/²log(ε⁻¹))。数值实验在六个重要问题类别（弹性网最小二乘回归、组稀疏Huber化SVM、量子半定规划等）上验证了相对PAL方法5至50倍的显著加速。

Conclusion: 本文提出的非精确增广拉格朗日方法在理论上达到了凸情形下的最优复杂度和强凸情形下的近最优复杂度，同时具备参数无关特性与可验证KKT精度保证，并在实践中展现出对PAL方法的大幅计算优势，为线性约束凸复合优化提供了一种高效、稳健且易于部署的求解框架。

Abstract: We develop inexact augmented Lagrangian (AL) methods for linearly constrained convex composite optimization problems whose objective is the sum of a smooth convex function and a possibly nonsmooth closed proper convex function with compact domain. Unlike primal accuracy guarantees based on objective-value error, our methods target verifiable approximate KKT solutions. In the convex setting, we propose three inexact AL schemes with optimal primal-dual complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1})$, improving prior AL bounds such as $\mathcal O(ε^{-4/3})$, $\mathcal O(ε^{-7/4})$, and $\mathcal O(ε^{-2})$, and improving the $\mathcal O(ε^{-1}\log(ε^{-1}))$ guarantees of proximal augmented Lagrangian (PAL) methods. Two of the three convex variants are parameter-free. We also establish non-ergodic convergence guarantees, including a stronger last-iterate guarantee for one variant. In the strongly convex setting, our methods achieve near-optimal complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1/2}\log(ε^{-1}))$, with two parameter-free variants. Numerical experiments on six important problem classes, including elastic-net least-squares regression, group-sparse Huberized support vector machines, and a quantum semidefinite program (SDP), demonstrate substantial computational advantages of our inexact AL methods over a representative PAL method, with speedups frequently ranging from $5$ to $50$ times.

</details>


### [183] [Optimal Deterministic Oracle Complexity for Weakly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03246)
*Jiajin Li,Siyu Pan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了ρ-弱凸且G-Lipschitz函数寻找ε-驻点的确定性一阶算法的最坏情况复杂度下界，证明需要Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)次查询，与现有上界匹配，建立了最优确定性oracle复杂度，并揭示了光滑非凸与光滑弱凸优化之间的本质复杂度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱凸非光滑优化问题在机器学习中广泛出现（如鲁棒PCA、相位恢复等），但其确定性一阶算法的oracle复杂度下界此前未知。本文旨在确立这一下界，并理解非光滑几何本身（而非随机性）所带来的额外复杂度开销。

Method: 通过构造一个特定的ρ-弱凸、G-Lipschitz函数族（当Δ≤G²/ρ时），证明任何确定性一阶算法（每次查询可获取函数值和完整次微分）至少需要Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)次查询才能找到ε-驻点（以Moreau包络的梯度度量）。

Result: 证明了确定性一阶算法的下界为Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)，与已知最优的确定性和随机一阶算法上界在常数因子内匹配，从而确立了该问题的最优确定性oracle复杂度。该结果揭示了光滑非凸优化（Θ(ε⁻²)复杂度）与光滑弱凸优化之间本质的ε⁻²额外因子差异。

Conclusion: 非光滑弱凸优化的额外复杂度因子源于非光滑几何本身（而非随机性或算法设计缺陷），这给出了两类优化问题之间的基本复杂性分离，为后续研究提供了理论基石。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of finding $ε$-stationary points of $ρ$-weakly convex and $G$-Lipschitz functions, where stationarity is measured by the gradient of the Moreau envelope. We consider a first-order oracle that returns both the function value and the full subdifferential at every query point. We prove that every deterministic first-order algorithm requires $ Ω({ρG^2Δ}/{ε^4})$ oracle queries whenever $Δ\leq {G^2}/ρ$, where $f(\bz)-\inf f \leq Δ$. This lower bound matches the best known deterministic and stochastic first-order upper bounds, up to universal constants, and establishes the optimal deterministic oracle complexity. The result reveals a fundamental complexity separation between smooth nonconvex and nonsmooth weakly convex optimization. While smooth nonconvex minimization admits a $Θ(ε^{-2})$ oracle complexity, nonsmooth weakly convex optimization incurs an intrinsic additional $ε^{-2}$ factor arising from nonsmooth geometry rather than stochasticity.

</details>


### [184] [Joint-Range Inequalities for Nonconvex QCQPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03318)
*Liding Xu,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸二次约束二次规划（QCQP），提出一种"先投影再提升"（project-then-lift）的割平面方法，基于两条基础有效不等式构造联合范围（joint-range）不等式，并给出了非凸情形下投影集凸包的闭式描述以及凸情形下的半定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCQP是广泛存在的非凸优化问题，其松弛质量直接影响求解效率。现有的混合整数舍入（MIR）不等式能处理"混合"项，但缺乏对非凸二次结构更深入的刻画。受MIR启发，作者希望通过两条基础不等式的联合范围来构造更强的新型割平面，以改善松弛紧度。

Method: 采用project-then-lift方法：首先将两条基础有效不等式对应的两行松弛投影到二维集合中；进而分析两条不等式中二次函数的联合取值范围——对于非凸联合范围给出投影集的闭式凸包描述，对凸联合范围给出半定表示；由此导出新的联合范围不等式族，并提升（lift）回扩展QCQP公式。进一步提出更灵活的割线混合联合范围不等式（secant mixed-joint-range inequalities），以更好利用非凸联合范围。该方法通过控制提升不等式的支撑集来保持稀疏性。

Result: 在初步几何实验中，联合范围不等式相比通过重构-线性化技术（RLT）构造的投影松弛，能够显著减小该投影松弛的面积，验证了其紧致化效果。

Conclusion: 联合范围不等式为QCQP提供了一类新的、能够刻画非凸二次结构且保持稀疏性的有效割平面，有望改善非凸QCQP松弛质量，未来可进一步研究其在算法求解中的实际性能。

Abstract: We study cutting planes for nonconvex quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) through a project-then-lift approach inspired by mixed-integer rounding (MIR) inequalities. Given two base valid inequalities for the extended QCQP formulation, we project the associated two-row relaxation into a two-dimensional set and analyze the joint range of quadratic functions in two base inequalities. For the nonconvex joint range, we give a closed-form convex hull description of the projected set; for the convex joint range, we give its semidefinite representation. This yields a new family of joint-range inequalities, which can be lifted back to the extended QCQP formulation. MIR inequalities can handle ``mixed'' terms: continuous variables or fractional linear combinations of integer variables. Similarly, we propose more flexible secant mixed-joint-range inequalities, which better expose and exploit the nonconvex joint range. The proposed approach preserves sparsity, since the support of each lifted inequality is controlled by that of the base inequalities. In preliminary geometric experiments, the joint-range inequalities yield substantial area reduction of the projected relaxation constructed via reformulation-linearization-technique.

</details>


### [185] [Dynamic Proximal Point Method for Unconstrained Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03349)
*Enrico Bertolazzi,Alberto De Marchi,Davide Stocco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对无约束优化的新型动态近端点算法，通过自适应更新对角加权矩阵调节二次正则项，内层使用牛顿法与线搜索求解子问题，外层根据内层求解器性能调整参考点和正则化参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近端点算法在处理无约束优化问题时，固定或单一的正则化参数限制了求解效率和收敛速度，需要一种能够自适应调节正则化结构的动态方法，以更好地利用目标函数的导数信息。

Method: 该算法采用双层结构：外层迭代更新参考点并动态调整对角加权矩阵和正则化参数；内层使用带线搜索的牛顿法求解带自适应对角加权正则化项的近端子问题，并推导了计算牛顿步的简化线性系统及相应的价值函数。

Result: 论文推导了牛顿步计算所需的简化线性系统，定义了相应的价值函数，并探讨了从导数信息构造对角缩放矩阵的实用方法，同时提供了面向实现的伪代码和与算法一致的停止准则。

Conclusion: 该动态近端点算法通过自适应调节正则化结构并结合内外层协同机制，为无约束优化提供了一种全局收敛的理论框架和实用的实现方案。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel dynamic proximal point algorithm for unconstrained optimization. The method generates a sequence of proximal subproblems, where the quadratic regularization term is weighted by a diagonal matrix that is updated adaptively at each iteration. Each subproblem is solved using an inner Newton's method combined with a line search, which provides a global convergence mechanism for the nonlinear solver. At the outer level, the algorithm updates the reference point and adjusts the regularization parameter based on the performance of the inner Newton solver. We derive the reduced linear system used to compute the Newton step, define the corresponding merit function, and discuss practical approaches for constructing the diagonal scaling matrix from derivative information. The paper also provides implementation-oriented pseudocode and stopping criteria that are consistent with the proposed method.

</details>


### [186] [Fast and slow mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03376)
*Roxana Dumitrescu,Julian Gutierrez Pineda,Peter Tankov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双时间尺度结构的方法，用于在具有公共噪声的均值场博弈（MFG）中构造近似纳什均衡，利用有效MFG（无公共噪声）的均衡构造显式ε-MFG均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有公共噪声的均值场博弈通常非常复杂，直接求解纳什均衡在计算上具有挑战性。作者希望利用双时间尺度结构来简化问题，通过平均化方法将公共噪声消除，从而构建近似均衡。

Method: 采用双时间尺度结构：公共噪声由快速变量承载（遵循遍历动力学），慢变量则受控制或停止。核心思想是用有效MFG（系数关于快尺度过程的平稳测度平均化，无公共噪声）的均衡来逼近完整MFG的解。构造显式ε-MFG均衡，利用带随机化控制和停止的有效MFG均衡，并依赖两尺度扩散的强收敛结果，以时间尺度分离参数控制纳什均衡条件中的误差。

Result: 获得了带随机化停止的MFG均衡存在性的新结果；证明了两尺度扩散在MFG结构假设下的强收敛性；建立了时间尺度分离参数与纳什均衡条件误差之间的定量关系，从而证明了所构造的ε-MFG均衡的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为具有公共噪声的复杂MFG提供了一种可行的高效近似求解途径，通过时间尺度分离实现降维，并以可量化的误差保证近似均衡的质量。

Abstract: We propose a framework for constructing approximate Nash equilibria in mean-field games (MFG) with common noise based on a two-time-scale structure. In our model, the common noise is carried by a fast variable evolving under ergodic dynamics, while the slow variable is either optimally controlled or stopped. The fast variable enters the dynamics of the slow one through the drift coefficient. The key idea is to construct an approximation to the solution of the full MFG with common noise using an ``effective'' MFG without common noise, where the coefficients are averaged with respect to the stationary measure of the fast-scale process. We construct an explicit $\varepsilon$-MFG equilibrium for the full MFG from the equilibrium for the effective MFG with randomized control and stopping. To this end, we obtain new results on existence of MFG equilibria with randomized stopping. We rely on strong convergence results for two-scale diffusions under structural assumptions on the MFG, and show that the time-scale separation parameter controls the error in the Nash equilibrium condition.

</details>


### [187] [A proximal difference of convex functions algorithm using Barzilai-Borwein step size with nonmonotone line search and extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03465)
*Kelin Wu,Hongpeng Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的近端DC（difference-of-convex）算法框架，用于求解一般非凸非光滑优化问题。通过将Barzilai-Borwein步长与非单调线搜索策略相结合，克服了标准近端DC算法步长保守和稳定性不足的问题，并开发了外推机制以加速收敛，同时在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质下严格证明了全局收敛性。数值实验表明，该方法在SCAD正则化最小二乘问题和图像分割模型上具有显著的高效性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准近端DC算法在求解非凸非光滑优化问题时存在步长选择过于保守且稳定性不足的缺陷，导致收敛速度慢、实际求解效率低下。本文旨在通过引入Barzilai-Borwein步长和非单调线搜索策略来克服这些局限，同时设计外推机制进一步提升收敛速度。

Method: 提出一个近端DC算法框架，核心创新包括：(1) 引入Barzilai-Borwein（BB）步长替代传统保守步长；(2) 采用非单调线搜索策略提升稳定性并避免陷入局部停滞；(3) 开发外推（extrapolation）机制加速收敛；(4) 在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）性质假设下建立全局收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在SCAD正则化最小二乘问题和基于图形的Ginzburg-Landau图像分割模型上进行了数值实验，结果表明所提出的方法相比现有DC算法具有更优的计算效率和更高的求解精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于BB步长与非单调线搜索的近端DC算法框架有效解决了传统方法的步长保守和稳定性问题，在保证全局收敛性的前提下实现了高效求解，为一般非凸非光滑优化问题提供了更具竞争力的求解工具。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel proximal difference-of-convex (DC) algorithmic framework to solve general non-convex, non-smooth optimization problems. By combining Barzilai-Borwein (BB) step sizes with nonmonotone line search strategies, our approach effectively overcomes the conservative step sizes and stability issues inherent in standard proximal DC algorithms. Furthermore, we develop extrapolation mechanisms to accelerate convergence while ensuring global stability. The global convergence of the proposed algorithms is rigorously established under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property. Numerical experiments on the SCAD-regularized least squares problem and graphic Ginzburg-Landau image segmentation models demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve highly competitive efficiency and accuracy compared to existing DC algorithms.

</details>


### [188] [Pursuing Optimal Stepsize in Adaptive Gradient-Based Quadratic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03546)
*Yifan Wang,Luca Ballotta,Ruggero Carli,Xianghui Cao,Luca Schenato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二次函数的梯度下降收敛速度问题，提出一种无需先验全局函数参数的轻量级自适应步长算法。通过运行中估计局部曲率的最小和最大值，该算法被证明能收敛到实现最快收敛速度的最优常数步长。仿真表明在二次函数和逻辑回归分类任务中，其收敛速度与近期自适应方法相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降的收敛速度高度依赖于步长的选择，而最优步长通常需要已知函数的全局参数（如利普希茨常数、强凸常数）。在实际应用中，这些先验信息往往不可得或难以估计，因此需要设计一种无需全局参数先验知识即可实现快速收敛的自适应步长算法。该问题在凸优化领域具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Method: 受平滑凸函数自适应步长算法启发，本文提出一种计算开销极低的策略：利用运行过程中估计的局部最小曲率和局部最大曲率来动态调整步长。具体而言，算法维护对局部曲率下界和上界的增量估计，据此确定当前迭代的步长，并在理论上证明该步长序列逐渐收敛到最优常数步长。

Result: 理论结果表明，所提算法能收敛到使梯度下降收敛最快的常数步长。仿真实验验证了在二次函数优化中，该算法的收敛速度与近期自适应方法相当或更优；在逻辑回归分类的初步测试中也表现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种无需全局参数先验知识即可实现快速收敛的轻量级自适应梯度下降方法。该算法兼顾了理论收敛保证与实用性，为无先验信息条件下的优化问题提供了一种高效且计算代价低廉的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of achieving fast convergence in gradient descent for quadratic functions without relying on a priori knowledge of global function parameters. Inspired by adaptive stepsize algorithms for smooth convex functions, we propose a computationally lightweight strategy based on running estimates of minimal and maximal local curvatures. We prove that our proposed algorithm converges to the optimal constant stepsize which achieves the fastest convergence. Simulations show that the convergence rate achieved by our proposed algorithm is comparable or superior to recent adaptive approaches both in the quadratic case under consideration and in a preliminary test on logistic classification.

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Stepsizes With Certified Convergence in Distributed Gradient Tracking With Quadratic Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03548)
*Yifan Wang,Luca Ballotta,Ruggero Carli,Andrea Iannelli,Xianghui Cao,Luca Schenato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对标量二次问题中具有异质曲率的分布式梯度跟踪算法，提出了一种具有收敛保证的自适应步长规则。该规则仅依赖于共识矩阵的本质谱半径和局部代价曲率的异质性（两者均可在线计算），并通过优化导出可解析解释的最优常步长，对任意网络拓扑和曲率异质性均保证收敛，在完全图和同质曲率下证明紧致。数值实验显示其显著优于现有离线和在线步长选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式梯度类算法的收敛性严重依赖合适的步长选择，但现有理论界往往过于保守，实际中常依赖经验调参。在线自适应策略虽已出现但性质与性能未被充分理解。为获得分析性洞见，作者聚焦于标量二次代价这一信息丰富且可解析处理的设置，以显式刻画网络拓扑与曲率异质性之间的交互作用。

Method: 推导以共识矩阵本质谱半径和局部代价曲率异质性为参数的收敛界（二者均可在无先验知识下在线计算）；对该界进行优化，得到计算上易于处理且可解析解释的收敛速率代理量与最优常步长；该步长对任意网络拓扑和曲率异质性保证收敛，且在完全图和同质曲率下证明其紧致性。

Result: 所得自适应步长规则具有解析形式，对任意网络拓扑和曲率异质性均保证收敛，在完全图与同质曲率情形下达到紧致界。大量数值仿真表明，该分布式自适应策略在所考虑设定下显著优于现有的离线和在线步长选择规则。

Conclusion: 本文填补了分布式梯度跟踪中自适应步长理论分析的空白，证明了在网络拓扑与曲率异质性仅需在线可得的两个标量参数下即可构造具有收敛保证且性能优异的最优步长，为分布式优化中的在线步长自适应提供了理论依据与实用方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an adaptive stepsize rule with guaranteed convergence for Distributed Gradient Tracking applied to scalar quadratic problems with heterogeneous curvatures. Most distributed gradient-based algorithms require a suitable stepsize selection. Available theoretical bounds are often overly conservative, while practical implementations typically rely on empirically tuned heuristics. Online adaptive strategies have only recently emerged for general distributed convex optimization, but their properties and performance remain only partially understood. To gain analytical insight, we focus on the informative setting of scalar quadratic costs, which allows us to explicitly capture the interplay between network topology and curvature heterogeneity. We derive a convergence bound parameterized only by the essential spectral radius of the consensus matrix and the heterogeneity of the local cost curvatures, both computable online without any prior knowledge of the optimization problem. Optimizing this bound yields a computationally tractable surrogate for the convergence rate and the optimal constant stepsize. The resulting stepsize admits an analytical interpretation, guarantees convergence for arbitrary network topologies and curvature heterogeneity, and is provably tight for complete graphs and homogeneous curvatures. Finally, extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed distributed adaptive strategy significantly outperforms existing offline and online stepsize selection rules in the considered setting.

</details>


### [190] [Sliding Methods for Hölder-Smooth Convex--Concave Minimax Optimization with Bilinear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03846)
*Nhat Trung Nguyen,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有双线性耦合结构的凸-凹极小极大优化问题，提出一种滑动方法，通过依据各组件（f、g、耦合算子）各自的Hölder光滑性以不同频率查询预言机，在Hölder连续条件下建立收敛保证，复杂度界显式依赖于Hölder指数/常数、强凸参数及耦合矩阵谱性质，并在随机情形下给出期望间隙界与噪声底限分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸-凹极小极大优化问题广泛出现于博弈论、对抗训练、均衡求解等场景，而具有双线性耦合结构的问题覆盖从非光滑（有界次梯度变化）到光滑（Lipschitz连续梯度）的广泛区间。现有方法通常以最差的函数光滑性为基准进行预言机查询，忽略了各个组件光滑性不一致的结构性特点，导致计算效率低下。作者旨在利用问题的复合结构，根据不同组件的个体光滑性分配不同查询频率，从而提升总体计算复杂度。

Method: 提出一种基于单调变分不等式递归滑动方案的方法：将原始问题转化为单调变分不等式，通过分层滑动的策略，对f、g及双线性耦合算子各自的预言机按由各自性质（Hölder指数、Hölder常数、强凸参数等）决定的不同频率进行查询。分析覆盖非强凸与部分强凸情形；对随机问题，在退化情形给出均匀期望间隙界，在环境光滑且具有正有效曲率时给出显式噪声底限。

Result: 在Hölder连续性假设下建立了收敛保证，复杂度界显式依赖于Hölder指数、Hölder常数、强凸性参数及耦合矩阵的谱性质。随机问题中证明了退化情形下的均匀期望间隙界，以及在环境光滑和正有效曲率条件下的噪声底限收敛。数值实验复现了理论预测的Hölder指数，确认了每个函数所需梯度评估次数按其自身光滑度分层而非取两者中更差的；断层成像基准测试表明当梯度评估代价高于额外矩阵-向量乘积时存在运行时间收益。

Conclusion: 该滑动方法有效利用了复合结构的个体光滑性差异，实现了预言机查询频率的分离与自适应分配，在确定性及随机情形下均取得了与各组件自身性质匹配的复杂度保证，并通过数值实验验证了理论预测的实际效果。

Abstract: We study convex-concave minimax optimization problems with bilinear coupling of the form $\min_{x\in \mathcal X}\max_{y\in \mathcal Y} \; f(x)+\langle y,\mathbf{B}x\rangle-g(y),$ where the functions $f$ and $g$ have Hölder continuous (sub)gradients. This setting covers a broad range of regimes, from nonsmooth problems with bounded subgradient variation to smooth problems with Lipschitz continuous gradients; for a smooth component used in the coupling-induced regularizer, its Lipschitz-gradient constant is assumed to hold in the ambient space. We propose a sliding method that exploits the composite structure of the problem by querying the oracles associated with $f$, $g$, and the bilinear coupling operator at prescribed frequencies determined by their individual properties. The method is based on a recursive sliding scheme for monotone variational inequalities. We establish convergence guarantees under Hölder continuity and show how the resulting complexity bounds depend explicitly on the Hölder exponents, Hölder constants, strong convexity parameters, and spectral properties of the coupling matrix. Our analysis covers nonstrongly convex and partially strongly convex regimes. For stochastic problems, we prove a uniform expected-gap bound in the degenerate regime and, under ambient smoothness and positive effective curvature, convergence up to an explicit noise floor. Numerical experiments reproduce the predicted Hölder exponents and confirm that the number of gradient evaluations required for each function separates according to its own smoothness level rather than the worse of the two. A tomographic benchmark shows runtime gains when gradient evaluations are more expensive than the additional matrix-vector products.

</details>


### [191] [A Robust Chance Constrained Approach to Surgery Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03931)
*Marco Caserta,Antonio García Romero,Miguel Vaquero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒机会约束框架用于择期手术排程，通过"缓冲引擎"将手术时长的不确定性转化为依赖可靠性的缓冲时长，并将可靠性作为内生的调度决策（而非固定服务水平参数），在HLA Moncloa医院真实数据上相比确定性均值基线将超过90分钟的延误降低97%、95分位延误降低约620分钟、总加班减少42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于平均时长或固定缓冲规则的手术排程虽然在事前看起来高效，但在执行中会因早期手术超时在当天传播累积，导致后续手术暴露于延迟风险中，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 将不确定性量化与排程优化分离的两阶段框架：(1) 缓冲引擎将分布信息转化为依赖可靠性级别的缓冲时长；(2) 排程模型联合决策手术分配、排序、开始时间及来自离散菜单的手术级可靠性水平。框架支持平均可靠性、最差日和硬目标三种风险姿态。

Result: 在HLA Moncloa医院的滚动计划案例研究（10个实例，45至227台手术）中，相比确定性均值基线：超过90分钟的延误降低97%，95分位延误降低约620分钟，总加班减少42%。与常见可靠性和均匀比例缓冲基准相比，菜单式方法通过利用手术间异质性实现更高的资源分配效率。

Conclusion: 通过按不确定性和运营暴露度分配缓冲，该框架将异质时长数据与风险偏好转化为执行中更可靠、且比一刀切规则更不保守的排程方案。

Abstract: We study elective surgery scheduling under uncertain procedure durations. Schedules based on mean durations or fixed buffering rules may appear efficient ex ante but become fragile in execution, as early overruns propagate through the day and expose later surgeries to accumulated delay. We propose a robust chance-constrained framework that separates uncertainty quantification from schedule optimization. A buffer engine converts distributional information into reliability-dependent buffered durations, while the scheduling model jointly selects assignments, sequences, start times, and surgery-level reliability levels from a discrete menu. Reliability therefore becomes an endogenous scheduling decision rather than a fixed service-level parameter. The framework accommodates average-reliability, worst-day, and hard-target risk postures. Comparisons with common-reliability and uniform proportional-buffer benchmarks show that the menu derives its value from exploiting surgery-level heterogeneity, allocating protection where it has the greatest operational value while avoiding unnecessary conservatism. In a rolling-origin case study at HLA Moncloa Hospital in Madrid, covering 10 instances with 45 to 227 surgeries, the approach reduces delays exceeding 90 minutes by 97%, lowers the 95th-percentile delay by approximately 620 minutes, and reduces total overtime by 42% relative to a deterministic mean-based baseline. By allocating buffers according to uncertainty and operational exposure, the framework translates heterogeneous duration data and risk preferences into schedules that are more reliable in execution and less conservatively buffered than one-size-fits-all rules.

</details>


### [192] [Simulation-Based Neural Policies for Portfolio Choice: Architecture, Training, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03933)
*Jules Viard,Alexander Michaelides,Panos Parpas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在低维生命周期消费-储蓄问题中系统比较了四种神经网络策略架构（单时间条件网络、跨制度切换拼接网络、逐日期反向训练网络及其约束变体），以动态规划网格解为基准，从福利、无需参考解的Bellman残差、形状约束和政策函数四个维度进行联合评估，揭示了不同架构在实现相似效用目标时在经济合理性上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经策略在有限时域随机控制问题中通常仅在高维场景下被研究，而高维恰恰缺少参考解，导致无法隔离和诊断单一架构或训练选择的具体贡献。本文通过选择低维但足够表达基准解的问题，将架构和求解方法本身作为研究对象，以便对神经策略设计进行可诊断的系统性比较。

Method: 构建一个状态空间足够低维从而可精确求解动态规划的终身消费-储蓄问题作为评估基准。比较四种架构：(1) 单一时间条件网络；(2) 跨制度切换的两个网络拼接；(3) 每个日期一个网络、使用冻结下游策略进行反向训练；(4) 每日期架构的约束变体。训练采用方向主导的优化方法以归一化梯度量级。评估维度包括福利（已实现效用）、无需参考解的Bellman残差、形状约束以及最终策略函数形态。

Result: 时间解耦的逐日期架构结合方向主导优化，使每个日期具有短而良定的目标函数；不同的架构虽可能达到相似的已实现效用目标，但在是否尊重底层问题的经济规律（如形状约束、Bellman残差）上存在显著差异，需联合评估才能全面刻画各设计的优劣。

Conclusion: 仅以已实现效用作为评价指标不足以甄别神经策略架构的优劣，必须结合福利、Bellman残差、形状约束和政策函数进行综合评估。逐日期解耦架构配合方向主导优化是构建既有良好数值表现又符合经济结构约束的神经策略的有效途径。

Abstract: Many economic decision problems, lifecycle consumption-saving and dynamic portfolio choice, are finite-horizon stochastic control problems with continuous states and actions. When the state is low-dimensional these problems are solved by dynamic programming on a grid. The grid cost grows exponentially in the state dimension, known as the curse of dimensionality, which motivates replacing the value-function grid with a neural policy optimized directly through simulation. Such policies are usually studied in the high-dimensional settings that motivate them, precisely where no reference solution exists. So the contribution of any single architectural or training choice cannot be isolated and diagnosed. We therefore take a step back and treat both the architecture and the solution method as the objects of study. To this end, we consider a lifecycle problem with a sufficiently low-dimensional normalized state space to admit an accurate dynamic programming solution, which is used for evaluation. We compare four architectures. The simplest consists of a single time-conditioned network. We then consider two networks concatenated across the regime switch, followed by one network per date trained backward against frozen downstream policies. Finally, we evaluate a constrained variant of the per-date architecture. Decoupling the policy across time gives each date a short, well-posed objective, which we pair with direction-dominant optimization that normalizes away gradient magnitude. Architectures that lead to similar realized utility objective can nevertheless differ in whether they respect the underlying problem's economics. We therefore evaluate each design jointly based on welfare, a solution-free Bellman residual, shape restrictions, and the resulting policy functions.
